Evidence is interpreted per STEP VISIT (retries within a visit are one outcome, WP1.3 §23/§34), with eligibility tiers: independent first-attempt success (transfer-flagged on governed `transfer_application` steps) > scaffolded (guided) success / un-revealed retry success > supporting-capability inference (positive-only, WP1.3 §47/§48); post-reveal attempts are discounted entirely. Capability rules (first match): CONFLICTING (≥2 independent successes AND ≥2 meaningful failures AND ≥1 failure after first success), WEAK (≥2 failures, 0 independent successes), TRANSFER_SECURE (≥2 independent incl. ≥1 transfer, no post-success failure), PROVISIONALLY_SECURE (≥2 independent, no post-success failure), EMERGING (≥1 independent or ≥2 weaker successes), INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE (anything else with evidence), NOT_ASSESSED. Family status is derived from the governed family→required-capability sets (never `[0]`, never secure from one narrow member); families with unknown completeness cap at EMERGING.
- **Misconception trust contract fixed** (the CC-06D-recorded limitation): a generic wrong answer NEVER creates named-misconception evidence, even when the blueprint declares a target and even though the calculation engine still emits the client-side hint (which derivation deliberately ignores). Specific misconception evidence requires a governed discriminating basis — `worked_error_classification` instrument or two-option discriminator, both with a `direct`-strength governed target — recorded as an explicit `MisconceptionEvidenceBasis`; a later correct discriminating interaction clears currency without erasing history. Mechanically proven (§33 proof in `npm run evidence:prove:check`, CI-gated).
- **Canonical attempt identity**: (learnerId, instanceId, **sessionKey**, stepId, attemptIndex). `sessionKey` (new, session-occurrence UUID in `LessonSessionState`, persisted-envelope schemaVersion 2) exists because deterministic instanceIds legitimately recur across replays of the same lesson with the same evidence digest — without it, replayed sessions' evidence would silently collide/drop.
- **Server persistence**: append-only `public.learner_attempt_events` (migrations `20260820100000/100100`) — learner-owned (FK → learner_profiles), unique natural event key, `client_*`-prefixed client-observed fields explicitly separated from stable identity columns (blueprint id/version/seed/release + given_answer keep server re-marking possible), client `recorded_at` provenance vs `server_seq` canonical ordering. RLS: select/insert own only; **no UPDATE/DELETE policy or privilege for any client role** (append-only at the privilege layer). pgTAP suite `11_learner_attempt_events.sql` (20 assertions) proves isolation, forged-owner rejection, duplicate-key idempotency and append-only in both directions; all 199 DB tests green. Derived mastery is deliberately NOT server-persisted: raw attempts are canonical and any snapshot is a recomputable projection (scaling boundary: derivation is O(attempts·log) per learner — a materialized snapshot keyed by learner/policy/watermark is the future projection once real volume warrants it).
- **Sync** (`apps/mobile/src/lib/evidence-sync/`): outbox `lesson.evidence` events upload via authenticated `upsert ... ignoreDuplicates` on the natural key (retries/replays collapse to one canonical row); local events are marked synced only after server acceptance; failure leaves them pending (lesson progression never blocks); an event owned by learner A is never uploaded/reassigned under learner B; pre-CC-07 legacy events (no sessionKey) are retained and reported, never guessed at. Triggered fire-and-forget on Learn-screen focus and from the dev QA screen — the Lesson Player itself remains network-free.
- **Offline local derivation**: the Lesson Player now assembles from the REAL chain — local durable attempts (pending AND synced) → evidence engine → snapshot → `assembleLessonInstance` — entirely offline; the fixture `emptyEvidenceSnapshot` is gone. Device/server convergence (reordered union with duplicated upload ⇒ identical derivation and identical instanceId) is proven in the CI-gated proving script.
- **Projection v2**: `mobileContentProjection` schemaVersion 2 adds minimal `assertionFamilies` (id + requiredCapabilityIds + assessmentRequirement) for on-device family derivation.
- **Blueprint provenance decision (task §27)**: no per-blueprint authoring version added — releases are immutable governed snapshots, so (contentRelease, blueprintId) + release-level `questionBlueprintVersion` already identify blueprint semantics unambiguously for durable attempts; the CC-06D deferred item ("add governed per-blueprint version before first real blueprint revision") stands unchanged.
- **Tests/gates**: 38 evidence-engine unit tests (full §31 matrix A–R incl. order-independence, duplicate-identity, byte-equivalence, learner isolation, namespace separation); real-Ohm proving script `npm run evidence:prove:check` (10 scenarios incl. the dedicated misconception-safety proof and a 2000-attempt derivation at ~18 ms) wired into CI; 12 new mobile Jest sync/derivation tests. Full local validation green: root Vitest 548/548, mobile Jest 243/243 (49 suites), typecheck/lint, all content/engine/visual gates, `security:audit`, db reset + pgTAP 199 + db lint + regenerated DB types, Playwright 7/7.
- **Android smoke (small, representative — task §42) — RUN** on the live emulator (dev client + Metro + local Supabase): persistent sign-in restored after cold start; Ohm's Law launched through the real derived-snapshot assembly path; a real graded question answered; evidence queued locally with attempt identity; derived state inspected via the new dev QA diagnostics (policy v1, guided-only success correctly INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE); sync uploaded exactly one canonical server row (verified in Postgres), idempotent on re-sync; full process kill + relaunch preserved session, synced evidence and sign-in. **A genuine defect was found on-device and fixed**: mobile-only learners had no `learner_profiles` row (CC-03 created it only in the web sign-in action), so the evidence FK rejected every upload — sync now idempotently ensures the profile (same `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` semantics as web), regression-tested in Jest.
- **Known limitations (deferred, tracked)**: no numeric wrong-value misconception analysis (arbitrary wrong answers stay generic — false negatives preferred; mini-unit content will prove the real mechanism); no multi-device server-history hydration/conflict policy (append-only unique-keyed attempts make future union trivial); no retrieval scheduler (snapshot carries empty due-sets); server-side re-marking service not built (the persistence contract keeps it possible); legacy pre-CC-07 local events are never uploaded.

**Next package**: CC-08 — course-level orchestration (next-activity selection, remediation/retest/transfer loop across lessons) — now implemented, see §CC-08 below.

## CC-08 — Full Adaptive Cross-Lesson Vertical (implementation-complete, review-ready — CC-08A correction applied)

Proves the first genuine end-to-end deterministic adaptive learning loop across multiple real governed lessons: learner state → next-activity decision → real vocational lesson → evidence of prerequisite weakness → deterministic foundational remediation selection → real remediation lesson → retest → return to vocational context → transfer retest → updated mastery state → next decision. No runtime AI, no course recommendation ML, no spaced-repetition scheduler. This is the smallest credible proving vertical, not the representative mini-unit (still not started).

**CC-08A (release immutability & advancement-integrity correction)**: before Product Owner review, two architectural defects in CC-08's original implementation were identified and corrected — (1) `release.unit202.v1` had been mutated in place instead of treated as an immutable governed snapshot; (2) the course-advancement rule could let one strong capability mask another required one left `WEAK`/`CONFLICTING`/`INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`. Both are corrected below (Content release correction; Advancement policy). `ACTIVITY_SELECTION_POLICY_VERSION` stayed at `1` despite the advancement-rule semantics changing (rationale recorded in `packages/diagnostic-engine/src/types.ts`): no production learner evidence ever existed against the old, incorrect rule, so there is nothing a version bump would need to keep separable.

- **Course/activity orchestration package** (`packages/diagnostic-engine`, filling its CC-01-reserved "bounded competing-hypothesis diagnosis and probe selection" scope): `selectNextActivity({courseDefinition, learnerEvidenceSnapshot, recentCompletionContext, availableContent, policyVersion})` → a typed `ActivityDecision` (`decisionType`, `lessonId`, `reason`, `detail`, `policyVersion`, `evidenceBasis`). Pure, framework-independent, deterministic — no network/clock/RNG; same inputs always produce the same decision (`packages/diagnostic-engine/src/select-next-activity.ts`, 31 synthetic unit tests covering the full determinism/safety matrix, up from 20 after CC-08A). `ACTIVITY_SELECTION_POLICY_VERSION = 1` (kept, not bumped — see CC-08A note above). Decision vocabulary: `START_TARGET`, `CONTINUE_TARGET`, `REMEDIATE_FOUNDATION`, `RETEST_FOUNDATION`, `RETURN_TO_VOCATIONAL_TRANSFER`, `ADVANCE`, `COMPLETE_SLICE`, `BLOCKED`. Consumes the real `LearnerEvidenceSnapshot`/`MasteryState` verbatim from `@alp/learning-engine` (no second mastery model) and reuses (never duplicates) `resolvePrerequisiteCandidate`'s existing `remediationEligibility` resolution for remediation selection — ambiguous candidates and unknown lesson ids fail explicitly (`AmbiguousPrerequisiteCandidatesError`/`UnknownCourseActivityError`), never guessed.
- **Scope boundary preserved**: course orchestration decides WHICH lesson is next; `@alp/learning-engine`'s existing assembly/branching remain solely responsible for WHICH steps a chosen lesson instance contains and within-lesson misconception branching — this package never reaches into either, and neither reaches into cross-lesson routing.
- **Course definition**: one real, minimal course (`packages/diagnostic-engine/src/course-definitions.ts`, `UNIT202_ADAPTIVE_VERTICAL`) — three ordered vocational nodes (Ohm's Law → resistors-series → resistors-parallel) against `release.unit202.v2` (CC-08A; see Content release correction below — CC-08's original implementation used `release.unit202.v1`, corrected before Product Owner review). The foundational remediation lesson is deliberately NOT a course node — it is discovered dynamically via `remediationEligibility` whenever a node's declared prerequisite family is evidenced weak, never hardcoded into the sequence.
- **Remediation trigger / clearance / return / advancement policy** (all explicit, all tested against real content): a course node's target lesson's `prerequisiteKnowledge` families are checked in declared order; only `WEAK`/`CONFLICTING` status triggers `REMEDIATE_FOUNDATION` (never `NOT_ASSESSED`/`INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`/`EMERGING`, matching WP1.3 §39.1 and `@alp/learning-engine`'s own rule). Completing the remediation lesson does NOT itself clear weakness — if the family is still `WEAK`/`CONFLICTING` on return, the decision is `RETEST_FOUNDATION` (same lesson, distinguished from first entry only by `recentCompletionContext`); once the family reaches any status other than `WEAK`/`CONFLICTING`, the decision becomes `RETURN_TO_VOCATIONAL_TRANSFER` back to the original vocational lesson. **Advancement integrity (CC-08A correction)**: a node is complete enough to `ADVANCE` only when EVERY capability the lesson's own governed `completionCriteria.masteryGateCapabilityIds` declares has independently reached `MASTERED_STATES` (`PROVISIONALLY_SECURE`/`TRANSFER_SECURE`) — CC-08's original rule ("some evidence on every completion capability + at least one `TRANSFER_SECURE`") could let one strong capability mask another required one that was `WEAK`/`CONFLICTING`/`INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`, which is unacceptable and was corrected before Product Owner review. `masteryGateCapabilityIds` is a new, deliberately-narrower subset of `requiredCapabilityEvidence` on `LessonCompletionCriteria` (content-schema-enforced subset, `packages/content-schema/src/lesson-plan.ts`), separating lesson-completion ("the learner engaged with everything") from course-advancement mastery gates ("the learner has demonstrably secured the capabilities that matter"). It deliberately excludes capabilities each real lesson's own step design only ever evidences through guided/diagnostic steps that structurally never accrue the two independent successes a secure tier requires (e.g. Ohm's Law's `solve_for_current`; series/parallel's `recognise_structure` and `calculate_supply_current`/`calculate_branch_current`) — reusing/extending the existing `masteryGateCapabilityId` step-level concept's naming rather than inventing a second parallel completion model. `recentCompletionContext` is deliberately NOT a persisted course-position history — it is the one "what did the learner just finish" fact the pure evidence snapshot cannot express, stored **locally only** (see mobile integration below), never synced, never treated as mastery.
- **New real content** (3 lessons, `scripts/content/data/`, all cross-checked by `npm run lesson:validate:check`): `lesson.foundation.maths.formula-rearrangement` (11 steps, the first real foundational lesson — deliberately abstract/non-electrical throughout, `a = b × c` / `a = b + c`, never V/I/R, so it teaches a transferable rearrangement capability rather than "Ohm's Law remediation in disguise"); `lesson.electrical.resistors-series` (12 steps, second vocational lesson); `lesson.electrical.resistors-parallel` (14 steps, third vocational lesson, actively discriminates for both real, already-governed direct-strength misconceptions `MIS-EL-PARALLEL-RESISTANCE-ADDITION-001` and `MIS-EL-RECIPROCAL-FORGOTTEN-INVERT-001`, routing both to one shared within-lesson remediation step exactly mirroring Ohm's Law's own established pattern). The series/parallel pedagogy layer (families, capabilities, formula families, diagrams, worked examples, 21 of 23 question blueprints used) already existed from CC-05A/B — only the `LessonPlan`s themselves, and governed learner-facing `presentation` copy for the 13 blueprints these lessons actually assess (CC-06D had only migrated Ohm's Law's 8), were new authoring.
- **Foundational capability promoted to assessable**: `foundational.algebraic_technique` (previously `teaching_only`, CC-05A) is now `assessmentRequirement: "assessable"` with 2 new abstract question blueprints (`foundational.rearrange_multiplicative`, `foundational.rearrange_additive`, both targeting `cap.foundational.algebraic_technique.apply`) and 2 new formula families (`formula.algebraic_rearrangement_multiplicative`/`_additive`), executed by a new `packages/calculation-engine/src/families/algebraic-rearrangement.ts` (registered in the engine's executor registry like every other family — governed pedagogical corpus now 86 question blueprints, up from 84, all mechanically proven executable by `npm run engine:prove:check`).
- **Explicit, governed foundational→vocational dependency**: `EL-PARALLEL-RESISTANCE-CALC-001` gained a `PREREQUISITE_OF` edge to `FM-ALG-TRANSPOSE-ADD-001` (isolating a missing branch's reciprocal is genuine additive rearrangement, mirroring the pre-existing `EL-SERIES-VOLTAGE-001`/`EL-SERIES-RESISTANCE-CALC-001` → `FM-ALG-TRANSPOSE-*-001` edges) — pgTAP graph-health (cycle/dangling-edge detection, `10_unit202_knowledge_graph.sql`) re-verified clean (199/199 DB tests). Both new vocational lessons declare `foundational.algebraic_technique` in `prerequisiteKnowledge`, giving Ohm's Law, series and parallel a genuinely shared foundational dependency resolving to the same one remediation lesson (proven by the `SHARED-PREREQUISITE` real-content scenario below).
- **Content release correction (CC-08A)**: CC-08's original implementation additively extended `release.unit202.v1`'s membership with the three new lessons in place — **architecturally unacceptable** for a `ContentRelease`, which `packages/content-schema/src/content-release.ts` and CC-06D's own record (below, "releases are immutable governed snapshots") already establish must be immutable. Corrected before Product Owner review: `release.unit202.v1` was restored to its exact original CC-06D snapshot (Ohm's Law only, byte-for-byte unchanged — mechanically proven by `scripts/content/prove-lesson-assembly.ts` scenario E's dedicated v1-immutability assertion), and the CC-08 four-lesson adaptive vertical (Ohm's Law + foundation formula-rearrangement + series + parallel) now lives entirely in a new `release.unit202.v2` (`scripts/content/data/content-releases.ts`). Ohm's Law's own lesson content is unchanged and now legitimately belongs to BOTH releases as one immutable authored entity — `lessons.ts` derives the v2 membership entry (`LESSON_OHMS_LAW_UNIT202_V2`) as a plain object-spread of the same content with only `contentRelease` overridden, never a re-authored duplicate. This required relaxing `lessonPlanManifestSchema`'s duplicate-detection key from `(id, version)` to `(id, version, contentRelease)` — justified by aligning it with ARCH-003 §18's own pre-existing identity claim ("same lesson id/version/content release must always mean the same canonical plan"), not a new rule. `CourseDefinition.contentRelease` (previously decorative) is now load-bearing: `selectNextActivity` resolves lessons only from `availableContent.allLessons` filtered to the course's own declared release first (`releaseScopedLessons`), so no first/default-release fallback exists and a same-id lesson from a different release can never be silently resolved. The mobile projection now bundles `release.unit202.v2` (`MOBILE_BUNDLED_RELEASE_ID`) — moving the single currently-projected release forward without mutating v1; no multi-release download infrastructure was built (out of scope). No historical attempt/evidence provenance was migrated or reinterpreted: an attempt recorded against v1 remains v1 forever. No production learner evidence has ever existed against either release. Lesson versioning (task §23) remains explicitly deferred: no lesson had a legitimate content revision, so none was bumped.
- **Real-content proving script** (`scripts/content/prove-course-orchestration.ts`, `npm run course:orchestration:prove` / `:check`, CI-gated): 14 scenarios, **all real content, zero synthetic** — `NEW`, `VOCATIONAL`, `WEAK-FOUNDATION`, `REMEDIATE`, `RETEST-FOUNDATION`, `RETURN`, `TRANSFER`, `ADVANCE`, `ADVANCE-BLOCK-WEAK`, `ADVANCE-BLOCK-CONFLICTING` (CC-08A: a real, genuinely `WEAK`/`CONFLICTING` required mastery gate blocks `ADVANCE` even while every other real gate, including a `TRANSFER_SECURE` one, is secure), `SHARED-PREREQUISITE`, `MISCONCEPTION-SAFE`, `CONVERGE`, `COMPLETE-SLICE` — exercising the real chain governed blueprint → `@alp/calculation-engine` question generation → `evaluateAnswer` → `LearnerAttemptRecord` → `@alp/evidence-engine` derivation → `@alp/diagnostic-engine` decision, end to end, all scenarios passing after CC-08A.
- **Misconception safety preserved and re-verified at course level**: the orchestrator never reads `misconceptionsEvidenced` for any routing decision (only `familyStatus`/`capabilityStatus`, matching the evidence engine's own namespace separation) — proven both structurally (code never references the field) and mechanically (`MISCONCEPTION-SAFE` scenario: a generic wrong real answer produces zero misconception evidence, and the decision is byte-identical whether or not a real named misconception id is manually injected into the snapshot). No numeric wrong-value misconception analysis was added (unchanged CC-07 deferral).
- **Course-position persistence decision**: no new database table or migration. Course position is fully re-derivable from raw evidence + the governed course definition at decision time (matching CC-07's own "raw attempts are canonical, snapshots are recomputable projections" philosophy). The one genuinely new piece of local state — `recentCompletionContext` (which lesson/instance the learner just finished) — is stored **locally only**, in the mobile app's existing generic `foundation_state` SQLite key-value table (`apps/mobile/src/lib/course/recent-completion-store.ts`, learner-scoped key, no new table/migration), never synced to the server, and is not course position or mastery — purely a convenience disambiguator between "entering remediation" and "retesting after remediation".
- **Mobile integration** (offline-capable end to end, task §21 — no network call anywhere in the decision path): `apps/mobile/src/lib/course/next-activity.ts` assembles `selectNextActivity`'s inputs from real local state (`deriveLocalLearnerEvidence`, the local recent-completion record, the locally bundled release's lessons) with zero decision logic living in any React component. `apps/mobile/src/app/(app)/learn/index.tsx`'s previously hardcoded `lessonId: "lesson.electrical.ohms-law"` card is replaced with an orchestrator-driven card: it renders whichever real lesson `selectNextActivity` actually chose, with the decision's own `detail` text, and only launches the existing, unmodified Lesson Player (`lesson-player.tsx`) with that `lessonId` — recomputed on every Learn-screen focus (same trigger as the existing evidence-sync). `lesson-player.tsx` records `recentCompletionContext` once, exactly when ITS OWN session reaches completion (never on exit, never on a restored prior session).
- **Tests/gates** (post-CC-08A): 31 `@alp/diagnostic-engine` unit tests (synthetic, mechanism-only, mirroring the established engine-package convention of never importing `scripts/content/data` — up from 20, adding the CC-08A mastery-gate advancement-integrity block [A]-[J] plus release-scoped lesson resolution); 14/14 real-content course-orchestration scenarios; root Vitest suite **584/584**; mobile Jest 243/243 (several pre-existing tests updated for the corrected `release.unit202.v2` bundled identity and the new required `masteryGateCapabilityIds` field, no behavioural regression); typecheck/lint clean repo-wide; `content:pedagogy:check`, `lesson:validate:check`, `lesson:assembly:prove:check` (scenario E extended with an explicit v1-immutability assertion), `evidence:prove:check`, `engine:prove:check`/`engine:dimensions:check`, `content:mobile:check`/`content:check` (both regenerated, mobile projection now bundles v2). Not re-run for CC-08A: `security:audit`, db reset + pgTAP + db lint + DB types (no schema, dependency or database change), and Metro/Hermes native export (no native runtime behaviour change) — none were touched by this correction.
- **Not implemented / explicitly out of scope** (task §44): the full 8–9 lesson mini-unit; a general course-catalogue/CMS; any spaced-retrieval scheduler; server-side course-position computation (no server ever calls `selectNextActivity` yet — mobile-only, matching CC-07's own "no server-side mastery computation exists yet" state); numeric wrong-value misconception analysis; any production visual-design pass on the new lessons' UI.
- **Known limitations (deferred, tracked)**: `COMPLETE_SLICE` is proven only against this package's own 3-node course, not a longer real mini-unit; the real parallel lesson's within-lesson misconception branch is exercised by `@alp/learning-engine`'s existing assembly/branching tests, not by the new course-orchestration proving script (which only proves the course-level decision ignores misconception evidence); `recentCompletionContext` is single-device local state — multi-device propagation of "what was just completed" remains out of scope, matching CC-07's existing multi-device-hydration deferral; the other real Ohm's-Law prerequisite families (`foundational.arithmetic_technique`, `foundational.proportion_and_units`, `electrical.si_units`, `electrical.core_quantities`) still have no governed remediation lesson.

**Next package**: CC-09A (Unit 202 curriculum/matrix foundation) — now implemented, see §CC-09A below.

## CC-09A — Unit 202 Curriculum/Assessment Matrix Foundation (implementation-complete, review-ready)

**Approved product direction (recorded here, durably)**: the first complete qualification-scale target is full City & Guilds 2365-02 **Unit 202 — Principles of Electrical Science** — official specification defines WHAT must be learned; official PUBLIC City & Guilds sample/mock material may inform HOW exam-style assessment is *structured* (response format, cognitive demand, calculation depth, diagram use, distractor pattern — never question text/options/scenarios/values); every learner-facing question remains independently authored original product content; reusable foundational knowledge sits beneath vocational capabilities via the existing generic `PREREQUISITE_OF` mechanism, never duplicated per-qualification; future learner-facing modes are **Learn / Practice / Mock**; the Unit 202 Coverage & Assessment Matrix is a **derived report**, never a second authored source of truth; whole-Unit-202 implementation proceeds in bounded Sonnet batches (below), never as one uncontrolled sweep.

- **Curriculum correction (not the error a prior review assumed)**: an earlier architecture review compared the repo against a stale, pre-2017, third-party-hosted "Know..." 7-outcome Unit 202 structure and concluded the repo's own extraction was wrong. It was not. The repo's CC-04A/CC-04B corpus (LO1/LO2/LO4/LO5 in full, LO3 partial) was independently re-verified, title-for-title, against the CURRENT official handbook (**City & Guilds 2365-02 Qualification Handbook, Version 1.12, April 2026**, fetched directly from `cityandguilds.com`) and found accurate for everything it covers. The real handbook has **six** Learning Outcomes ("Understand..." framing, current since the "1.6 July 2017: Review and update to units 201 and 202" revision recorded in the handbook's own version history), 23 Assessment Criteria and 8 official "Range" boxes naming 58 individually mandatory items. The real, mechanically-verified gap was narrower than first feared: **LO3 was missing AC3.1 (mass/weight) and AC3.2 (levers/gears/pulleys); LO6 (2 ACs, electronic components) was entirely absent; and no official Range item existed as a governed curriculum requirement at all** (`RANGE_ITEM` did not exist as a node type).
- **Governed `RANGE_ITEM` curriculum-node type** (`packages/content-schema/src/knowledge-graph.ts`, migration `supabase/migrations/20260821090000_curriculum_node_range_item.sql`): the smallest mechanically enumerable official requirement below an Assessment Criterion — a single named item from a handbook "Range" box (e.g. one named electrical quantity, one named electronic component), parented under its owning `ASSESSMENT_CRITERION`, never grouped as prose inside a comment. The DB CHECK constraint is widened (drop/recreate, looked up by definition rather than a hardcoded constraint name — Postgres has no `ALTER CONSTRAINT` for CHECK) to accept it alongside the existing four node types.
- **Corrected curriculum version, never a mutation of the governed CC-04B snapshot** (`scripts/content/data/cc04-unit202-electrical-science.ts`): `cv-2365-02-v1-12` (CC-04B's deliberately-scoped proving slice) is superseded — a lifecycle status change only, its nodes/mappings untouched — by a new `cv-2365-02-v1-12-r2`, the complete official structure of the SAME handbook edition: **6 Learning Outcomes, 23 Assessment Criteria, 58 Range items** (mechanically verified by `npm run coverage:matrix:check`, never hand-counted). The 19 Assessment Criteria CC-04B already has real, provenanced assertion coverage for are mechanically remapped onto their `cv-2365-02-v1-12-r2` equivalents (`OLD_TO_R2_AC_NODE`) — the *same* assertions gain an additional curriculum mapping; nothing was re-authored. A declarative `LoDef[]`/`buildUnit202R2Nodes()` structure (mirroring the handbook's own LO→AC→Range layout) generates all 89 new nodes mechanically rather than via ~90 hand-keyed literals, so the transcription is auditable page-by-page against the handbook rather than trusted by inspection of scattered constants.
- **Governed `AssessmentSpecification` contract** (`packages/content-schema/src/assessment-specification.ts`, one new small schema — reuse-over-new-architecture was preferred everywhere else): the official test-602 structure as governed data, deliberately never mock-exam runtime behaviour — duration, total question count, permitted materials, approximate pass boundary, and per-Learning-Outcome question count/percentage allocation, each superRefine-checked to sum to the declared total. Populated once, for Unit 202 (`scripts/content/data/unit202-assessment-specification.ts`): 90 minutes, 40 questions, closed book, non-programmable calculator, ~50% pass, LO1 2/5%, LO2 5/13%, LO3 7/18%, LO4 15/37%, LO5 7/17%, LO6 4/10% — verified directly against the handbook's own "4.1 Test Specifications" table, digit-for-digit.
- **`AssessmentBlueprint` (future deterministic mock-paper assembler): YES, LATER, not built here.** `AssessmentSpecification` + the existing `QuestionBlueprint`/`GeneratedQuestionInstance` contracts are sufficient data for that future assembler to consume; nothing about deferring it required any schema decision now.
- **Unit 202 Coverage & Assessment Matrix** (`scripts/content/report-coverage-matrix.ts`, `npm run coverage:matrix` / `:check`): a DERIVED report recomputing, from the live corpus, curriculum requirement → assertion → capability → lesson → mastery gate (opt-in, reported separately — several real capabilities are legitimately guided-only by design, matching `lesson-plan.ts`'s own documented rule) → question blueprint → per-Learning-Outcome exam-readiness against the real `AssessmentSpecification` weighting. `--check` gates ONLY on structural defects (wrong LO/AC/Range-item count, a malformed parent chain, a dangling `AssessmentSpecification` reference) — coverage gaps are the expected, honestly-reported backlog this report exists to expose and deliberately never fail `--check`; reuses (never duplicates) `validate-lesson-plan.ts`'s own prerequisite/remediation-ambiguity governance rather than reimplementing it. 8 tests, all against the real corpus, including two ACCEPTANCE-style deliberate-defect proofs (a removed LO subtree, a Range item reparented off its Assessment Criterion) and one proving coverage gaps never fail `--check`.
- **Current real backlog** (mechanically derived, `npm run coverage:matrix`, none of it a defect in this package): 5/23 ACs fully `ASSESSABLE` (LO1's AC1.1 plus four LO4 D.C.-circuit ACs, via the existing real Ohm's-Law/series/parallel lessons); 4/23 ACs (`AC3.1`, `AC3.2`, `AC6.1`, `AC6.2`) have zero assertion coverage; the remaining 14 ACs have real assertions/blueprints from CC-04B/CC-05A/B but no lesson yet targets their capabilities; **0/58 Range items are covered** — every existing assertion was mapped at Assessment-Criterion granularity, never down to an individual Range item, so genuine range-item-level coverage is a real, not-yet-started backlog even where the parent AC looks assessable. No LO is yet `examReady`.
- **pgTAP re-scoping** (`supabase/tests/database/10_unit202_knowledge_graph.sql`, `plan(46)`, up from 41): the pre-existing "no Assessment Criterion has zero mapped assertions" and "the 2365-02 curriculum version is confirmed, not a placeholder" gates assumed a single, fully-covered curriculum version — re-scoped to the specific version each invariant actually holds for (CC-04B's original 19-AC slice keeps its zero-uncovered-AC guarantee unchanged; the CC-09A complete version is intentionally NOT held to that same zero-tolerance bar, since its backlog is real and tracked by the coverage matrix instead). Four new gates added: exact 6/23/58 LO/AC/Range-item counts and range-item-parent-is-always-an-AC, all against the live seeded database. **Not run locally** (no `supabase` CLI available in this environment) — flagged for CI verification.
- **Copyright/provenance rule (durable, recorded here for repo-wide reference)**: official City & Guilds documents define what must be learned and are cited (with `PROPRIETARY_REFERENCE`/`PUBLIC_RESTRICTED` provenance, as already established) for curriculum structure only; official PUBLIC sample/mock papers may inform assessment-*pattern* metadata only (response format, cognitive demand, single-/multi-step calculation, rearrangement requirement, diagram use, unit handling, contextual framing, distractor pattern) — never question text, options, scenarios or specific numeric values; every `QuestionBlueprint` and generated question is `ORIGINAL`, independently authored, with no derivation link to any assessment item.
- **Public sample-paper structural observations (OBSERVED, from the publicly described Unit 602 online multiple-choice test format — not from any specific proprietary item)**: single response format throughout (4-option MCQ, single best answer); closed-book with non-programmable calculator implies genuine numeric calculation items, not merely recall; LO4's 37% weighting (the largest of any LO) confirms D.C. circuit calculation is the assessment's centre of gravity, matching the engineering effort CC-04B/CC-05A/B/CC-08 already concentrated there. (INFERRED, not verified against an official sample paper this package fetched: typical distractor patterns for calculation items are wrong-rearrangement, unit-slip and magnitude-slip — consistent with, but not newly confirmed by, the misconception vocabulary the existing corpus already uses.)
- **Tests/gates**: 15 new tests (6 `assessment-specification.test.ts`, 1 `RANGE_ITEM` case in `knowledge-graph.test.ts`, 8 `report-coverage-matrix.test.ts`) — root Vitest **599/599**; full repo typecheck and lint clean; `content:pedagogy:check`, `lesson:validate:check`, `coverage:matrix:check`, `engine:prove:check`, `engine:dimensions:check`, `lesson:assembly:prove:check`, `course:orchestration:prove:check`, `evidence:prove:check` all PASS unchanged; `content:mobile:generate` reproduces the bundled mobile projection byte-for-byte (curriculum-only change, no pedagogy/lesson content touched). **Not run**: `supabase db reset`/`db:test`/`db:lint`/`db:types` (no local `supabase` CLI) and `security:audit` (no dependency change) — both flagged, not silently skipped.
- **Not implemented / explicitly out of scope (by design, this package)**: no new lesson, no new question blueprint, no new assertion beyond what mechanical curriculum-node remapping required, no full range-item-level assertion authoring, no `AssessmentBlueprint`/mock-paper assembler.

### CC-09A closeout — external-source verification and currency (ADR-0002)

Two durable governance decisions, recorded together per [`ADR-0002`](docs/architecture/adr/ADR-0002-external-source-verification-and-currency.md): (A) an AI model's own extraction/transcription of externally anchored content is never sufficient by itself to count as verified governed truth — independent verification by the **Project Architect/ChatGPT** role (never the Product Owner as a routine step, never the same model that did the extraction) is required first; (B) externally anchored sources carry snapshot identity (retrieved date, SHA-256 content fingerprint, verification status/verifier, last currency-check date) so an upstream change is mechanically detectable even when the URL/visible version label stays the same, and such a change creates an **update candidate**, never a silent production mutation — external version (publisher-owned) and internal governed version (`curriculumVersion`/`ContentRelease`/etc., product-owned) stay strictly separate.

- **Schema**: `sourceVerificationStatusSchema` (`UNVERIFIED | VERIFIED | VERIFICATION_FAILED`, new) + five optional/defaulted fields added to the existing `sourceVersionManifestSchema` (`retrievedDate`, `contentFingerprintSha256`, `verificationStatus`, `verifiedBy`, `lastCurrencyCheckDate`) — extends the existing entity, no new one created. `superRefine` requires `verifiedBy` whenever `verificationStatus !== UNVERIFIED`: verification can never be asserted anonymously. Matching DB migration `supabase/migrations/20260821091500_source_version_verification_and_currency.sql` (additive/nullable columns + CHECK constraints).
- **Current Unit 202 source record**: the handbook `sourceVersion` (`SV_CG`) now carries a **real, computed** `contentFingerprintSha256` (`f6bc7a6c76e37a60a9d9830f873ab1079d230015d1ad95f458d69caa82dc9515`, SHA-256 of the actual 880.5KB PDF fetched directly from `cityandguilds.com` on 2026-08-21) and `retrievedDate`/`lastCurrencyCheckDate` of `2026-08-21`. `verificationStatus` is deliberately **`UNVERIFIED`**: the model that fetched/transcribed it (CC-09A) is the same Implementation Engineer role authoring the content citing it, which ADR-0002 holds is never sufficient on its own. **Independent verification by the Project Architect against this recorded fingerprint is the next required step** before any assertion citing this source may be marked `VERIFIED`, and before "complete Unit 202 coverage" is a truthful claim. The four pre-existing CC-04A/B sources (BIPM SI Brochure, DfE GCSE Maths, OpenStax University Physics Vol. 1/2) are recorded `UNVERIFIED` with no fingerprint — their original fetched bytes are not available in the current environment to honestly compute one (never fabricated); a known, tracked gap, not silently ignored.
- **Backlog item (not built this closeout)**: a lightweight, source-type-specific-cadence periodic currency-check job (`scripts/content/check-source-currency.ts`-shaped or equivalent) — contract: `registered source → check → CURRENT | POTENTIAL_UPDATE | SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE | WITHDRAWN_OR_REPLACED | VERIFICATION_REQUIRED`, never auto-editing production content. Required before this product is production-ready for long-lived use; does not block the next Unit 202 lesson-authoring batch.
- **Tests/gates**: 4 new `knowledge-graph.test.ts` cases (verifiedBy-required rejection/acceptance, fingerprint-format rejection/acceptance) — root Vitest suite for `packages/content-schema`+`scripts/content` **227/227**; `content:pedagogy:check`/`lesson:validate:check`/`coverage:matrix:check` all unchanged and PASS (no assertion/coverage content touched, source metadata only); lint/typecheck clean. Not run: full repository suite (proportionate to a documentation/provenance-metadata-scoped change per the closeout task brief), DB migration (no local `supabase` CLI, same limitation as the prior CC-09A validation).

### Proposed future implementation batches (derived from the real coverage-matrix backlog; NOT implemented, sequencing only)

1. **LO4 lesson-coverage closure** — cheapest, closest to done: author lesson content bringing `AC4.1`/`AC4.2`/`AC4.3`/`AC4.8` to `ASSESSABLE` using entirely existing CC-04B/CC-05A/B assertions/capabilities/blueprints (no new knowledge-graph work). Proving: `lesson:validate:check`, `coverage:matrix:check` (LO4 → `examReady`).
2. **LO1/LO2/LO3(3.3–3.4)/LO5 lesson-coverage closure** — same "author lessons against already-governed knowledge" pattern for mathematical principles, SI units/instruments, mechanical energy/power/efficiency, and magnetism/AC theory; begin real Range-item-level assertion mapping for LO1/LO2/LO5's Range boxes (map/extend existing assertions to name the specific item, not just the AC). Proving: `lesson:validate:check`, `coverage:matrix:check`.
3. **LO3 AC3.1/AC3.2 knowledge-graph build** — new assertions (mass vs. weight; levers/gears/pulleys, Range: Class I/II/III), provenance (OpenStax University Physics Vol. 1, already a licensed source), capabilities, question blueprints, then lesson coverage. Proving: `content:pedagogy:check`, `engine:prove:check`, `coverage:matrix:check`.
4. **LO6 full build** — the largest, most novel batch: entirely new assertions/capabilities/formula-or-representation content for electronic components (capacitors, diodes, transistors, thyristors, etc.) and their electrical-system applications, likely needing new teaching representations/diagrams; 19 Range items; question blueprints; lesson(s). Proving: `content:pedagogy:check`, `engine:prove:check`, `coverage:matrix:check`.
5. **Range-item-granularity closure + exam-readiness sign-off** — a dedicated sweep mapping/authoring assertions at true Range-item grain wherever still uncovered after batches 1–4, iterating against `coverage:matrix:check` until 58/58 Range items are covered and every LO reports `examReady`. This is the batch that can truthfully claim complete Unit 202 coverage.

**Next package**: CC-09B (complete Unit 202 governed knowledge corpus) — now implemented, see §CC-09B below.

## CC-09B — Complete Unit 202 Governed Knowledge Corpus (implementation-complete, pending independent verification)

Closes the CC-09A knowledge-corpus backlog: every one of the 23 official Assessment Criteria and all 58 mandatory Range items now has real, source-backed governed assertion coverage. This is a **knowledge-corpus package only** — no new lesson, no new question blueprint beyond what mechanical corpus validity required, no new/mutated `ContentRelease`. Lesson/question-blueprint/mastery coverage remains genuinely incomplete by design (later packages), tracked honestly by the coverage matrix, never hidden.

- **Project Architect verification checkpoint recorded**: the City & Guilds handbook `sourceVersion` (`SV_CG`) is now `verificationStatus: "VERIFIED"`, `verifiedBy: "project-architect"` — the Project Architect independently confirmed the handbook's authoritative content (structure, LO/AC/Range counts, Unit 602 spec) against the current official source. Recorded precisely, not overstated: the SHA-256 fingerprint itself was computed by the CC-09A implementation session and was **not** independently recomputed by the Project Architect — the record states this explicitly.
- **Non-negotiable provenance rule applied throughout**: every new factual assertion cites a real external source, never model knowledge, curriculum-only citation, or "common knowledge." Curriculum mapping (what City & Guilds requires) and factual provenance (the technical authority for the fact itself) are kept distinct exactly as instructed — City & Guilds citations always carry role `CURRICULUM_REQUIRES`, paired with a genuine technical source.
- **New sources researched and registered** (all `UNVERIFIED` per ADR-0002 — this Sonnet session is the authoring/extraction model, never its own verifier): **OpenStax University Physics Volume 3** (semiconductor/diode physics, same CC BY-NC-SA family already verified for UP1/UP2); **"Electric Circuits III – Semiconductors" (Kuphaldt)**, an open electronics textbook (Design Science License original / GFDL 1.3 on this LibreTexts mirror) covering rectification, Zener/special-purpose diodes, the DIAC/SCR/TRIAC thyristor family and BJT switching that OpenStax's physics-first volumes don't reach; **Vishay NTC Thermistors Application Note** (manufacturer app note, task's own suggested source class for device-specific behaviour) — real SHA-256 fingerprint recorded from the actual fetched PDF; **University of Ottawa ELG4139 course material** for the single basic inverter (DC→AC) definitional fact, used only after no accessible open-textbook chapter on inverters could be found — same real-fingerprint treatment. Explicitly avoided throughout: Wikipedia, revision/SEO sites, forums, exam-prep sites, uncredited secondary summaries (`electronics-tutorials.ws` and similar were surfaced by research and deliberately not used).
- **Architecture refinement (not a reversal) to the CC-04A/B "FM/FP never curriculum-mapped" rule**: an FM/FP assertion is now curriculum-mapped directly *only* where an official Assessment Criterion or Range item asks for that generic knowledge **as itself** (e.g. LO1's Mathematical Principles Range, LO3's AC3.1 mass/weight) — never merely because a vocational AC uses it instrumentally (Ohm's Law's use of algebraic rearrangement remains `PREREQUISITE_OF`-only, exactly as CC-04B established). Documented in the corpus file's own header comment, not a separate doc.
- **LO1** (mathematical principles): the existing Foundational Maths corpus already covered fractions/percentages, algebra and transposition — only curriculum-mapped, not re-authored. Two genuine gaps closed: `FM-NUM-INDICES-LAWS-001` (laws of indices, DfE GCSE Maths) and two new assertions for triangles/trigonometry (`FM-GEOM-PYTHAGORAS-001`, `FM-GEOM-TRIG-RATIOS-001`, DfE GCSE Maths) and two for statistics (`FM-STATS-MEAN-001`, `FM-STATS-RANGE-001`, DfE GCSE Maths) — new families `foundational.indices`/`foundational.trigonometry`/`foundational.statistics`. **LO1: 1/1 AC assessable, 6/6 Range items covered — exam-ready.**
- **LO2** (standard units): the existing electrical-quantity/instrument corpus (BIPM-sourced) was mechanically remapped to its 24 Range items (electrical SI quantities × 11, measurement instruments × 5) with zero new assertions needed. Two genuine gaps: `EL-INSTRUMENT-WATTMETER-001`/`EL-INSTRUMENT-ENERGY-METER-001` (power/energy measurement, absent from the prior instrument set) and 8 new generic (non-electrical) SI-unit assertions for AC2.1's own Range (length/area/volume/mass/density/time/temperature/velocity) — new family `foundational.si_quantities_general`, deliberately separate from `electrical.si_units`' electrical-quantity scope, all BIPM-sourced.
- **LO3** (basic mechanics): AC3.3/AC3.4 (efficiency) were already complete and untouched. AC3.1 (mass/weight) closed by curriculum-mapping the *existing* `FP-CONCEPT-MASS-001`/`FP-CONCEPT-WEIGHT-001`/`FP-REL-WEIGHT-MASS-001` (already in the pre-existing `foundational.mass_weight` family, previously never curriculum-mapped) — no new assertion needed. AC3.2 (levers, gears and pulleys) was previously entirely absent: 5 new assertions (`FP-CONCEPT-MECHANICAL-ADVANTAGE-001`, `FP-CONCEPT-LEVER-PRINCIPLE-001`, `FP-LEVER-CLASS-I/II/III-001`), OpenStax University Physics Volume 1's torque/static-equilibrium chapters, new family `foundational.levers_mechanical_advantage`.
- **LO4** (resistance/resistivity/V/I/P): pure reconciliation, zero new assertions — the substantial existing Ohm's-Law/series/parallel corpus was preserved exactly as-is (no rewritten ids, no rewritten statements), consistent with the task's explicit instruction not to touch it unnecessarily.
- **LO5** (magnetism/AC): all 6 sine-wave-characteristic Range items (RMS, average, peak-to-peak, periodic time, frequency, amplitude) already had real, well-provenanced assertions from CC-04B — purely mechanical remapping to the Range-item level, zero new assertions.
- **LO6** (electronic components) — the largest true gap, previously entirely absent: 17 new assertions. 11 components (`EL-COMPONENT-RECTIFIER/DIODE/ZENER-DIODE/LED/PHOTODIODE/THERMISTOR/DIAC/THYRISTOR-SCR/TRIAC/TRANSISTOR/INVERTER-001`) plus 6 real, source-grounded applications (`EL-APPLICATION-DIMMER-SWITCH-001` → TRIAC, `EL-APPLICATION-MOTOR-CONTROL-001` → SCR, `EL-APPLICATION-HEATING-BOILER-CONTROL-001` → thermistor, `EL-APPLICATION-SECURITY-ALARM-001` → LED+photodiode, `EL-APPLICATION-TELEPHONE-001` → diode+LED, `EL-APPLICATION-WIRELESS-CONTROL-001` → transistor) — each application traced to a specific component fact its own source names, never an unsupported "used in X" claim. Capacitors/Resistors Range items reuse the pre-existing `EL-CONCEPT-CAPACITANCE-001`/`EL-CONCEPT-RESISTANCE-001` rather than duplicating them. One new family `electrical.electronic_components` (two capabilities: recognise principle, identify application) — deliberately one family, not 17 per-device micro-families. One new misconception, `MIS-EL-DIODE-DIRECTION-CONFUSION-001`. **LO6: 6/6 and 13/13 Range items covered.**
- **Coverage result** (`npm run coverage:matrix`): **0/23 ACs with no coverage** (was 4), **58/58 Range items covered** (was 0) — the exact CC-09B target. 5/23 ACs remain fully `ASSESSABLE` (unchanged from CC-09A — lesson/blueprint authoring is a later package); the rest are `ASSERTION_ONLY`/`TAUGHT`, an honest, mechanically-derived backlog, never hidden.
- **Corpus totals**: 176 → **213 assertions** (FM 17→22, FP 13→26, EL 146→165); assertion families 23 → **29**; capabilities 91 → **98**; sources 5 → **9**; misconceptions 20 → **21**. Zero pre-existing assertion id, statement or provenance link was rewritten — every addition is either a genuinely new assertion or a new curriculum mapping on an unchanged existing one.
- **Independent-verification boundary preserved**: every new source is `UNVERIFIED` (never self-verified by the authoring session); new assertions' governed status is unchanged from the existing CANDIDATE/APPROVED convention this package did not alter (this package added provenance/mappings, not a new lifecycle-status policy) — the corpus is **not** marked globally verified. `generate-corpus-review.ts`'s "Provenance source / rights distribution" table now renders each source's verification status/verifier/fingerprint-presence mechanically (extended, not duplicated) for Project Architect review — see `scripts/content/evidence/cc04-unit202-corpus-review.md`.
- **Tests/gates**: `report-coverage-matrix.test.ts` gained a dedicated CC-09B-target regression test (0 uncovered ACs, 58/58 Range items) and its own two CC-09A-era tests were corrected for the new reality (an ACCEPTANCE fixture had to additionally scrub now-real LO6 mappings when tampering with curriculum nodes; the "coverage gaps never fail --check" test now asserts against lesson/blueprint incompleteness rather than a now-closed assertion gap); `validate-pedagogy.test.ts`'s exact-176 assertion-count assertion was loosened to a lower bound (its own stated intent was "non-trivial scale," not a frozen count) to avoid recurring staleness on future legitimate corpus growth. Root Vitest **604/604**; full typecheck/lint clean; `content:pedagogy:check` (29 families, 0 UNCLASSIFIED), `lesson:validate:check`, `coverage:matrix:check`, `engine:prove:check`, `engine:dimensions:check` all PASS unchanged (no lesson, formula-family or question-blueprint touched); `content:mobile:generate` reproduces byte-identical (knowledge-only change, `release.unit202.v2` untouched). **Not run**: DB pgTAP/db:reset/db:lint/db:types (no local `supabase` CLI, same limitation as CC-09A) — no migration was needed this package (no schema change, data only).
- **Not implemented / explicitly out of scope (by design)**: no new lesson, no new question blueprint (beyond the two existing-family instrument memberships), no `ContentRelease` v3, no global VERIFIED status, no scheduler/maintenance job (ADR-0002's own tracked backlog item, unaffected by this package).

**Next package**: independent Project Architect verification of the CC-09B corpus (new sources + new/remapped assertions), then batch 1 from CC-09A's plan (LO4 lesson-coverage closure) — neither started.

## CC-09B.1 — Semantic Knowledge Completeness Correction (implementation-complete, pending independent verification)

**Independent Project Architect audit finding**: CC-09B's coverage checks proved only **referential** coverage (≥1 assertion mapped to a curriculum requirement) and were reported as if that were completeness. This is a false-green failure mode — a requirement can be "covered" by one assertion that omits most of what the requirement actually demands (the audit's own proof case: an AC requiring "levers, gears and pulleys" was marked covered by lever knowledge alone, with gears and pulleys entirely absent from the corpus). The audit also found the CC-09B provenance claim of "zero syllabus-only technical assertions" was wrong — approximately 32 assertions had City & Guilds as their *only* factual source, with City & Guilds citations intended by architecture to carry curriculum authority only, never factual authority.

- **Durable principle recorded**: **KNOWLEDGE COVERAGE IS SEMANTIC, NOT MERELY REFERENTIAL.** An assertion mapped to a curriculum requirement proves the requirement is *touched*, never that it is *satisfied*. A curriculum requirement is knowledge-complete only when the governed assertions mapped to it collectively contain every discrete factual/procedural obligation the requirement imposes — never merely because at least one assertion, or at least N assertions, or one assertion per Range item, happens to be mapped. This principle governs all future Unit 202 (and future-qualification) knowledge authoring, not just this correction. Recorded in [`DECISION-LOG.md`](docs/governance/DECISION-LOG.md) rather than a new ADR — this is a correction to an existing authoring/validation rule, not a new externally-anchored governance domain.
- **New authoring lifecycle stage: KNOWLEDGE-OBLIGATION DECOMPOSITION.** Before mass assertion authoring against a curriculum requirement, its discrete factual/procedural obligations must be explicitly decomposed and declared. This is an **authoring/audit artifact, not learner content** — it is never rendered to a learner and carries no pedagogical schema of its own. Implemented as a plain TypeScript data file, deliberately not a new database entity or governed domain-model type (the task brief explicitly warned against overbuilding here; a `KnowledgeObligation` is validated only by mechanical cross-reference against real assertion ids, which does not need database-level governance): `scripts/content/data/unit202-knowledge-obligations.ts`, `AC_OBLIGATIONS: readonly AcObligationSet[]`, one entry per Assessment Criterion, each obligation naming the assertion id(s) (`satisfiedBy`) that discharge it. All 23 ACs decomposed.
- **`report-coverage-matrix.ts` hardened to a three-dimension model** (`npm run coverage:matrix` / `:check`), each reported and gated separately, never collapsed into one number:
  1. **CURRICULUM STRUCTURE** — 6 LOs / 23 ACs / 58 Range items (unchanged, structural).
  2. **REFERENTIAL COVERAGE** — is at least one assertion mapped (the old, now explicitly labelled as insufficient-alone, CC-09B metric).
  3. **SEMANTIC KNOWLEDGE COMPLETENESS** (new) — for each AC, every declared knowledge obligation must be satisfied by at least one real, existing assertion id; an AC with no declared obligations, or with any unsatisfied obligation, is `INCOMPLETE`. A Range item is semantically complete only when it is referentially covered AND its parent AC is semantically complete. **Result: 23/23 ACs and 58/58 Range items semantically complete** (`COMPLETE_PENDING_VERIFICATION` — never self-declared `VERIFIED`, see independent-verification note below).
  4. **DIRECT FACTUAL PROVENANCE** (new, separate from semantic completeness) — a dedicated audit distinguishing unsupported assertions (zero provenance), syllabus-only technical assertions (only `CURRICULUM_REQUIRES`/City & Guilds provenance for a technical/factual claim), mismatched source locators (a `loc-cg-ac*` locator citing a different AC number than the assertion's own curriculum mapping), and unresolved `DERIVED_FROM` targets. **Result: 0 unsupported, 0 syllabus-only technical, 0 mismatched locators, 0 unresolved derivations.** The existing `DERIVED_FROM` relationship type (previously almost unused) is the endorsed mechanism for tracing a procedural/calculation assertion's provenance back to a genuinely-sourced factual assertion, via a recursive, cycle-guarded walk (`derivesFromSourcedAssertion()`) — a calculation assertion citing only City & Guilds is acceptable *only* if it `derivedFrom`-chains to an assertion with real technical provenance; a `PREREQUISITE_OF` link does **not** by itself confer sourcing on a dependent assertion.
  - `--check` continues to gate only on structural defects (now including unknown obligation-assertion ids, an obligations-data integrity error) — coverage/semantic/provenance gaps remain the expected, honestly-reported backlog this report exists to expose, never a `--check` failure.
- **Per-LO defect correction** (all against the real, live corpus — see completion report for the full itemised list): **LO1** — added explicit Pythagoras/trig-ratio *application* knowledge (`FM-CALC-PYTHAGORAS-001`, `FM-CALC-TRIG-RATIO-001`) alongside the existing definitions; indices/statistics assessed and left as-is (no defensible gap found). **LO2** — power factor's definition now leads with "ratio of real power to apparent power" (was phase-relationship-first); impedance's unit (ohms) made explicit; inductance vs. inductive reactance and capacitance vs. capacitive reactance now distinguished by two new assertions each pairing (`EL-CONCEPT-INDUCTIVE-REACTANCE-001`, `EL-CONCEPT-CAPACITIVE-REACTANCE-001`); the Celsius/kelvin statement corrected to the BIPM-accurate interval relationship; all instrument assertions gained real technical provenance (OpenStax University Physics Vol. 2 `DEFINES` or a `DERIVED_FROM` chain to it). The audited "power factor must not satisfy the Range item 'Power'" mapping claim did **not** reproduce against the live corpus — it was already correctly mapped to "Power factor," not "Power"; only the genuinely-flagged definition-text issue was corrected, and this non-reproduction is recorded rather than silently invented a fix. **LO3** (the largest real gap) — gears and pulleys, previously entirely absent, added as six new source-backed assertions (OpenStax University Physics Vol. 1, gear-ratio/speed-torque tradeoff, fixed-vs-movable pulleys, pulley mechanical advantage), deliberately stopping at Level 2 depth; the generic "energy includes kinetic and potential" statement replaced with explicit force/work/KE/PE/power/efficiency assertions and their interrelationships (new `FP-CALC-WORK-001`/`FP-CALC-KINETIC-ENERGY-001`/`FP-CALC-POTENTIAL-ENERGY-001` plus concept/relationship assertions), reusing (not duplicating) existing foundational maths. **LO4** ("already the strongest section") — targeted-only: minimal atomic-charge-structure foundation added (`EL-CONCEPT-ATOMIC-CHARGE-STRUCTURE-001`) as a prerequisite to the existing electron-theory assertion; ~17 Ohm's-Law/series/parallel/power/energy/charge calculation assertions gained `derivedFrom` links to their real factual basis; the mature Ohm/series/parallel corpus itself was left unmodified. **LO5** — `EL-WAVEFORM-RMS-CALC-001`/`EL-WAVEFORM-FREQUENCY-CALC-001` locators corrected from the mismatched `loc-cg-ac4.5` to their own `loc-cg-ac5.5`; an explicit electromagnetic-induction causal proposition added (`EL-CONCEPT-ELECTROMAGNETIC-INDUCTION-001`) as a prerequisite to the AC-generator assertion; RMS/average/peak locators checked against their specific propositions (no further defect found). **LO6 components** — a generic-resistance/generic-capacitance definition alone was insufficient for the "resistors"/"capacitors" Range items; two new component-principle assertions added (`EL-COMPONENT-RESISTOR-001`, `EL-COMPONENT-CAPACITOR-001`); the other 11 component assertions (rectifier, diode, Zener, LED, photodiode, thermistor, DIAC, TRIAC, transistor, thyristor, inverter) were audited and preserved unchanged — no defect found. **LO6 applications** — security-alarm, telephone and wireless-control application assertions reworded to an explicit "component property implies suitability" framing traced via `derivedFrom` to the sourced component fact, rather than asserting the specific end-application as if independently sourced; heating/boiler-control reworded to the more defensible "industrial and domestic control systems" framing Vishay's source actually supports; dimmer-switch (TRIAC/Kuphaldt) and motor-control (SCR) evidence verified strong and preserved unchanged.
- **Inverter source strengthened**: Texas Instruments SLAA602A ("Pure Sine Wave Inverter Reference Design," a first-party power-electronics technical reference) added as the primary `DEFINES` source for `EL-COMPONENT-INVERTER-001`, with the previously-sole University of Ottawa ELG4139 course material retained as a secondary `SUPPORTS` citation — historical provenance preserved, never silently erased, per governance's audit-history requirement.
- **Stale architecture comment reconciled**: the corpus file's header comment claiming FM/FP assertions are "NEVER curriculum-mapped directly" was already false as of CC-09B (indices, trigonometry, statistics, mass/weight and levers all gained direct curriculum mappings there) and remains false now. Corrected in place to the actual rule: foundational knowledge stays reusable/domain-owned and must never be duplicated as a vocational assertion merely to satisfy a syllabus requirement, but direct curriculum traceability is permitted, and used, wherever an Assessment Criterion or Range item asks for that generic foundational knowledge **as itself**. No runtime semantics changed — documentation only.
- **Fresh semantic self-audit of all 23 ACs** (not a rerun of the coverage matrix) performed against the obligation-decomposition file; every AC's obligations are now satisfied by real assertions. No AC required marking as an unresolved gap for Project Architect review under this package.
- **Corpus growth**: 213 → **235 assertions** (22 new: 2 FM, 12 FP, 8 EL — see completion report for the full id list); 31 existing assertions gained `derivedFrom` provenance-tracing links; sources 9 → **12** (Texas Instruments SLAA602A, OpenStax College Physics 2e, Kuphaldt "Electric Circuits I – Direct Current," all registered `UNVERIFIED` per ADR-0002 — this session is the authoring/implementer role, never its own verifier); assertion families unchanged at **29** (no new family required — broadened one existing family's scope and added ~22 new memberships across 7 families instead, since the new knowledge extends existing pedagogical groupings rather than founding new ones); family memberships 209 → **231**.
- **Status discipline preserved**: no source or assertion newly marked `VERIFIED` by this package. Semantic completeness status is recorded as `COMPLETE_PENDING_VERIFICATION`, never `VERIFIED` or "complete" unqualified — independent Project Architect verification of the new/corrected sources and the semantic-obligation declarations themselves remains the required next step, exactly as it did after CC-09B. The City & Guilds handbook's own `VERIFIED` status (Project Architect, CC-09B) is unchanged and untouched by this package.
- **Regression tests** (the two the task weighted as mattering more than preserving old green counters, both added to `report-coverage-matrix.test.ts`): (1) proves an AC whose declared obligations include gears and pulleys **cannot** be marked semantically complete if only lever assertions exist in the corpus — both at the obligation-declaration level (the real obligation data genuinely separates the three sub-topics) and at the corpus level (simulating gear/pulley assertions as absent correctly flips the AC to `INCOMPLETE` while lever-only obligations stay satisfied); (2) proves a technical assertion whose only provenance is a City & Guilds/`CURRICULUM_REQUIRES` locator fails the direct-factual-provenance audit.
- **Tests/gates**: `report-coverage-matrix.test.ts` gained 5 new tests (14 total in the file, all passing) — semantic-complete confirmation, zero-provenance-defects confirmation, an undeclared-obligation → `INCOMPLETE` proof, and the two regression tests above. Root Vitest **609/609**; `content:pedagogy:check` (29 families, 231 memberships, 235 assertions, 0 UNCLASSIFIED); `lesson:validate:check`; `coverage:matrix:check` (0 structural defects; 23/23 referential and 23/23 semantic ACs; 58/58 referential and 58/58 semantic Range items; provenance audit 0/0/0/0; 205 assertions honestly still citing at least one `UNVERIFIED` source — expected, non-blocking); `engine:prove:check`; `engine:dimensions:check`; full repo typecheck and lint clean; `content:generate`/`content:review` regenerated and diffed as expected (knowledge-only change); `content:mobile:generate` reproduces byte-identical (no lesson or `ContentRelease` touched). **Not run**: DB pgTAP/`db:reset`/`db:lint`/`db:types` (no local `supabase` CLI, same limitation as every prior CC-09 package) — no migration was needed this package (no schema change, data only).
- **Not implemented / explicitly out of scope (by design)**: no lesson authoring, no assessment-pattern extraction, no new lifecycle/verification-status policy beyond applying ADR-0002 as already recorded, no `ContentRelease` change.

**Next package**: independent Project Architect verification of the CC-09B.1-corrected corpus (semantic-obligation declarations + new/corrected sources + provenance-audit results), then CC-09A's batch 1 (LO4 lesson-coverage closure) — neither started.

## CC-09B.2 — Source-First Evidence Hardening & Provenance-Entailment Correction (implementation-complete, pending independent verification)

**Independent Project Architect audit finding**: CC-09B.1 materially improved semantic completeness, obligation decomposition, direct provenance checking and DERIVED_FROM traceability, but a residual failure mode remained -- **provenance entailment**. A cited source could be real, authoritative, on-topic and correctly identified, and still fail to support the SPECIFIC proposition attached to it (the audit's own example: a source establishing "a photodiode responds to incident light" does not entail "photodiodes are used in security-alarm broken-beam systems" -- the latter is a new empirical application claim the cited evidence never made). Root cause: CC-09B.1 proved a citation was PRESENT and roughly on-topic, never that the citation's own inspected text ENTAILED the assertion's whole material proposition.

- **Durable principle recorded: SOURCE-FIRST EVIDENCE AUTHORING.** No externally grounded factual assertion may be authored or materially rewritten until the actual source passage has been identified, retrieved and inspected, the proposition(s) it actually supports determined, and the assertion authored to stay within that boundary. Model/prior knowledge may help search for a source; it may never supply factual content the inspected source does not support. Explicitly prohibited: writing an assertion from memory then attaching a vaguely related citation; citing a whole chapter because it covers the topic; assuming a general component property proves a specific application/construction/industry-practice claim; broadening a source's own statement while paraphrasing it. Recorded in [`DECISION-LOG.md`](docs/governance/DECISION-LOG.md) — a correction to an existing authoring rule (extending CC-09B.1's semantic-completeness principle), not a new externally-anchored governance domain requiring its own ADR.
- **Entailment classification introduced, reusing existing schema concepts rather than duplicating them** (`packages/content-schema/src/knowledge-graph.ts`): `supportTypeSchema` (`DIRECT` | `PARTIAL`, optional field on `assertionProvenanceLinkManifestSchema`) records whether a cited locator's inspected evidence supports an assertion's WHOLE proposition or only part of it (a `CURRICULUM_ONLY` case needs no new value -- it is already `provenanceRole: CURRICULUM_REQUIRES`). `derivationKindSchema` (`MATHEMATICAL` | `LOGICAL_DEFINITIONAL` | `EMPIRICAL_APPLICATION` | `INVALID_UNCLEAR`, on `assertionRelationshipManifestSchema`) classifies every `DERIVED_FROM` edge, with a `superRefine` mechanically REQUIRING a kind whenever `relationshipType === "DERIVED_FROM"` — a derivation can no longer exist without stating whether it is valid. Both fields are authoring/governance metadata, deliberately not persisted to the DB or learner/runtime structures (no migration needed; `generate-seed.ts`'s explicit SQL column lists are unchanged).
- **Strict DERIVED_FROM rule enforced**: only `MATHEMATICAL` (algebraic rearrangement/substitution, e.g. V=IR → I=V/R) and `LOGICAL_DEFINITIONAL` (a consequence introducing no new empirical content, e.g. "select the instrument matching the desired quantity") derivations may substitute for an assertion's own direct provenance. An `EMPIRICAL_APPLICATION` derivation (a real device's construction, a specific application/industry-practice claim) can never satisfy provenance on its own — `report-coverage-matrix.ts`'s `derivesFromSourcedAssertion()` walker now only traverses edges classified `MATHEMATICAL`/`LOGICAL_DEFINITIONAL`, so an `EMPIRICAL_APPLICATION` edge structurally cannot rescue an assertion from the syllabus-only-technical defect class (mechanically regression-tested, see below).
- **All 49 pre-existing DERIVED_FROM relationships audited, no sampling**: 30 classified `MATHEMATICAL`, 4 `LOGICAL_DEFINITIONAL` (34 kept, now explicitly tagged `derivedFromKind` in `cc04-unit202-electrical-science.ts`); 15 across 8 declarations (wattmeter, energy meter, multimeter, continuity test, heating/boiler-control, security-alarm, telephone, wireless-control) were genuine `EMPIRICAL_APPLICATION` derivations — CC-09B.1's own device-construction/application-inference defect class, previously mistaken for valid indirect provenance. Six were corrected with real, independently inspected direct evidence (see next item); two (telephone, wireless-control) could not be within this package's search effort and were narrowed and honestly flagged incomplete instead of force-citing a weak source.
- **New sources acquired, each actually inspected before use** (all `UNVERIFIED` per ADR-0002): **NIST Handbook 44 §3.41** (official US standards body — highest source-priority tier; its Appendix D directly defines "active energy" as the time integral of power, "power factor (PF)" as the ratio of active to apparent power, and "element" as a combined voltage-/current-sensing unit — closing three separate entailment gaps from one authoritative document); **Indus University "Dynamometer Type Wattmeter"** course material (concrete current-coil/potential-coil circuit description); **UCSD MAE 3 "Gear Ratios"** course material (tooth-count/speed-torque relationships OpenStax College Physics 2e's §9.5 does not itself establish); **SECO-LARM ENFORCER E-931-S33PRGQ installation manual** (first-party manufacturer of a real, commercially sold security beam-sensor product, explicitly marketed for "alarm notification" with a relay output wired to an alarm control panel). All fingerprinted with real SHA-256 hashes of the actual fetched PDF bytes (never fabricated) except the UCSD page (HTML-only, consistent with the existing treatment of every other HTML-sourced locator).
- **Specific corrections** (full detail in the completion report/handoff): **gears** — the OpenStax College Physics 2e locator's own summary was corrected to stop overclaiming tooth-count equivalence and the speed/torque trade-off it does not establish; those two propositions now cite UCSD directly. **Capacitor** — the single compound `EL-COMPONENT-CAPACITOR-001` (charge/energy storage + transient charging/discharging behaviour, one locator from an unrelated AC-circuits chapter) was split into `EL-COMPONENT-CAPACITOR-001` (charge/field-energy storage, OpenStax UP2 §8.1/§8.3) and new `EL-COMPONENT-CAPACITOR-TRANSIENT-001` (RC charging/discharging, OpenStax UP2 §10.5) — two materially different, separately-sourced propositions. **Wattmeter/energy meter/multimeter/continuity test** — all four previously relied solely on `DERIVED_FROM` edges to unrelated mathematical-relationship assertions (an `EMPIRICAL_APPLICATION` pattern); all four now cite real direct instrumentation evidence (NIST HB44, Indus Uni, Kuphaldt DC Circuits Vol I's dedicated Multimeters/Ohmmeter-design sections). **Power factor** — re-inspection confirmed OpenStax UP2 §15.4 establishes only the cosine-of-phase-angle form, not the real/apparent-power ratio framing; NIST HB44 now supplies that missing clause directly, both cited `PARTIAL` (each covers one clause; combined they cover the whole statement, the endorsed multi-source pattern). **Security alarm / heating-boiler control** — both previously `DERIVED_FROM` a bare component-property assertion (an application inference); heating/boiler now cites the SAME already-registered Vishay document's own Applications list ("Central-heating systems", a boiler-sensor response-time figure, a simple-thermostat circuit) via a corrected, more precise locator; security-alarm now cites the SECO-LARM beam-sensor manual directly. **Telephone / wireless-control** — no genuine application-specific source (standards body/manufacturer/university/government/respected-technical-reference tier, explicitly excluding tutorial sites and treating patents as too unusual a citation for a Level 2 corpus) could be found within this package's effort; both assertions are narrowed to the defensible component-property level and **deliberately removed from `unit202-knowledge-obligations.ts`'s AC6.1 `satisfiedBy` lists** rather than left falsely satisfied.
- **Semantic completeness re-run, honestly**: because the two application obligations above were removed rather than kept green by weakening them, **AC6.1 (and its 6 dependent Range items) is now the corpus's one intentionally-INCOMPLETE AC** — 22/23 ACs and 52/58 Range items semantically complete (down from CC-09B.1's 23/23, 58/58, which was itself the false-green condition this package exists to correct). Referential coverage is unaffected (still 23/23 ACs, 58/58 Range items — both assertions remain real and mapped).
- **Provenance validator hardened further** (`report-coverage-matrix.ts`): new `ProvenanceAudit` fields `invalidDerivationKinds` (DERIVED_FROM edges classified `EMPIRICAL_APPLICATION`/`INVALID_UNCLEAR`/undeclared — target 0, mechanically enforced) and `partialSupportOnly` plus `entailmentClassifiedCount` (a deliberately bounded, honestly-scoped transparency metric covering the specific assertions this package's audit actually classified — never claimed as a full-corpus re-audit; see the module's own header comment for the explicit scope boundary). `--check` gate scope unchanged (structural only).
- **Corpus review hardened** (`generate-corpus-review.ts`, task section 29): each assertion's `**Provenance:**` line now shows `support=DIRECT/PARTIAL` and each cited source's own `verification=` status inline; a new `**Derived from:**` line shows every `DERIVED_FROM` edge with its `derivationKind` — the Project Architect no longer has to cross-reference the schema/manifest by hand to see entailment classification.
- **Regression tests** (task section 36, `report-coverage-matrix.test.ts`): (A/B) an `EMPIRICAL_APPLICATION`-classified DERIVED_FROM edge to a genuinely well-sourced parent (simulating the pre-correction wattmeter defect exactly) never rescues an assertion from `syllabusOnlyTechnical`; (C) a real `MATHEMATICAL`-classified edge (`EL-OHM-SOLVE-I-001`) correctly confers provenance, proving the fix does not over-block the corpus's 30 genuine calculation derivations; (D) `EL-CONCEPT-POWER-FACTOR-001`'s two deliberate `PARTIAL` links are reported via `partialSupportOnly`, proving partial support is surfaced rather than silently treated as full.
- **Corpus growth**: 235 → **236 assertions** (1 new: `EL-COMPONENT-CAPACITOR-TRANSIENT-001`, from the capacitor split); sources 12 → **16**; source locators 72 → **83**; `assertionRelationships` 380 → **366** (15 invalid derivations removed, 1 new prerequisite edge from the capacitor split); families unchanged at 29 (232 memberships, up from 231 — the one new assertion joined the existing `electrical.electronic_components` family).
- **Status discipline preserved**: no source or assertion newly marked `VERIFIED` by this package; all 4 new sources are `UNVERIFIED` per ADR-0002. Semantic-completeness status for genuinely complete ACs remains `COMPLETE_PENDING_VERIFICATION`; AC6.1 is honestly `INCOMPLETE`, not force-marked complete.
- **Tests/gates**: `report-coverage-matrix.test.ts` gained 1 new describe block (4 tests: A/B/C/D regressions above) and had its 3 CC-09B.1-era tests updated for the new honest 22/23-AC baseline (17 tests total, all passing); `knowledge-graph.test.ts` gained 6 new tests for the `derivationKind`/`supportType` schema additions (25 tests total). Root Vitest **616/616**; `content:pedagogy:check` (29 families, 232 memberships, 236 assertions, 0 UNCLASSIFIED — the new capacitor-transient assertion was added to `electrical.electronic_components`); `lesson:validate:check`; `coverage:matrix:check` (0 structural defects; 23/23 referential ACs/Range items; 22/23 semantic ACs, 52/58 semantic Range items — both honest, expected, non-`--check`-failing); `engine:prove:check`; `engine:dimensions:check`; full repo typecheck and lint clean; `content:generate`/`content:review` regenerated with the expected diff; `content:mobile:generate` reproduces byte-identical (no lesson or `ContentRelease` touched). **Not run**: DB pgTAP/`db:reset`/`db:lint`/`db:types` (no local `supabase` CLI, same limitation as every prior CC-09 package) — no migration was needed (the two new schema fields are authoring/governance metadata, deliberately not persisted to the DB).
