## CC-09B.3 — Close Remaining AC6.1 Evidence Gaps & Normalise Multi-Source Entailment Semantics (implementation-complete, pending independent verification)

Closes the two AC6.1 application-evidence gaps CC-09B.2 left honestly incomplete (telephone equipment; wireless control systems), and clarifies the entailment model so a compound assertion whose clauses are jointly, genuinely covered by more than one source is no longer indistinguishable from one with a real, unresolved gap.

- **Telephone application closed**: `EL-APPLICATION-TELEPHONE-001` now cites **Skyworks Solutions AN347 "DAA Design Guide"** (a real, first-party telephone-line-interface/Direct Access Arrangement design guide — not a generic polarity-protection tutorial), whose own Figure 2 application schematic and Figure 29 architecture diagram show a diode bridge wired directly to the telephone line's TIP and RING terminals. The assertion is narrowed to exactly this: presence and position of a diode bridge as the first stage between the raw two-wire line and the rest of the line-side interface circuitry — no unsourced "why" beyond what the diagrams show.
- **Wireless-control application closed**: `EL-APPLICATION-WIRELESS-CONTROL-001` now cites the **Holtek HT12D/HT12F decoder datasheet** (a real, first-party remote-control decoder IC datasheet), whose own "Applications" list explicitly names garage door controllers, car door controllers, car alarm systems, security systems and other remote control systems, and whose General Description states the decoder's CMOS output pins are activated once the received address code matches. The assertion states the decoder's transistor-based (CMOS) output stage switching in response to a decoded wireless signal — the `EL-COMPONENT-TRANSISTOR-001` prerequisite is retained as a genuine pedagogical building block (CMOS output stages are built from transistors), not claimed to be literally the same component.
- **Both re-added to `unit202-knowledge-obligations.ts`'s AC6.1 `satisfiedBy` lists** now that real evidence exists — AC6.1 returns to **23/23, 58/58** semantic completeness, but this time as genuinely source-first-audited coverage, not CC-09B.1's false-green result CC-09B.2 corrected away from.
- **Multi-source entailment semantics clarified** (task sections 5-7): the existing provenance-LINK-level `supportType` (`DIRECT`/`PARTIAL`) is unchanged and reused as-is — multiple `PARTIAL` links each covering a different clause of a compound assertion remains normal and expected, never itself a defect. What was missing was an ASSERTION-level synthesis distinguishing "these links jointly cover the whole statement" from "a material clause is still genuinely uncovered". A new optional field, `multiSourceFullyCovered` (on `assertionVersionManifestSchema`, wired through `AssertionDef` in the corpus data file), records an explicit AUTHORING decision — never an algorithmic inference from "more than one PARTIAL link exists" — that the classified links were actually re-inspected clause-by-clause and confirmed to jointly cover the assertion's whole material proposition. `report-coverage-matrix.ts` computes a derived, six-value `EntailmentStatus` per assertion (`FULLY_SUPPORTED_SINGLE_SOURCE` / `FULLY_SUPPORTED_MULTI_SOURCE` / `FULLY_SUPPORTED_DERIVED` / `PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED` / `UNSUPPORTED` / `PENDING_REVIEW`) via `entailmentStatusFor()`, exposed on the report as `entailmentStatusByAssertion`.
- **Critical rule enforced structurally**: an assertion-level `PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED` result can never satisfy a semantic knowledge obligation — the obligation-resolution filter in `report-coverage-matrix.ts` now excludes any `satisfiedBy` id whose entailment status is `PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED` (deliberately NOT excluding `PENDING_REVIEW`: the vast majority of the 236-assertion corpus has never been individually entailment-classified by this bounded package, and "not yet classified" must never be conflated with "found deficient" — doing so would have silently broken semantic completeness for the entire untouched corpus, well outside this package's narrow scope).
- **All three former link-level-PARTIAL-only assertions re-audited clause-by-clause and reclassified**: `FP-CONCEPT-GEAR-001`, `FP-REL-GEAR-RATIO-001` (OpenStax College Physics 2e + UCSD MAE 3 jointly cover gear definition, meshing, torque/speed transmission and the radius/tooth-count mechanical-advantage relationship) and `EL-CONCEPT-POWER-FACTOR-001` (NIST HB44 + OpenStax UP2 jointly cover the ratio-definition and cosine-of-phase-angle clauses) — all three now carry `multiSourceFullyCovered: true` and resolve to assertion-level `FULLY_SUPPORTED_MULTI_SOURCE`. Their provenance links remain individually `PARTIAL` (accurate — no single link covers the whole statement) and still appear in the link-level `partialSupportOnly` transparency metric, now clearly distinguished in `formatReport()`'s output from the assertion-level `assertionLevelPartiallySupported` metric so the two are never conflated.
- **New sources** (both `UNVERIFIED` per ADR-0002): Skyworks Solutions AN347 (telephone/DAA design guide), Holtek HT12D/HT12F datasheet (remote-control decoder). Sources 16 → 18; source locators 83 → 85.
- **Tests**: `report-coverage-matrix.test.ts` gained a new describe block with the 5 tests required by task section 8 (A: multi-source full coverage confirmed via `multiSourceFullyCovered`; B: tampered-away confirmation correctly demotes to assertion-level `PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED`; C: a `PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED` assertion cannot satisfy its semantic obligation; D: a syllabus-only-tampered telephone/wireless assertion is caught by the existing provenance audit; E: the real, untampered corpus's telephone/wireless assertions are neither `UNSUPPORTED` nor `PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED`) plus updates to the 3 CC-09B.2-era tests for the restored 23/23 baseline (22 tests total in the file, all passing); `knowledge-graph.test.ts` gained 2 new tests for the `multiSourceFullyCovered` schema field (27 tests total). Root Vitest **623/623**; `content:pedagogy:check` unchanged (29 families, 232 memberships, 236 assertions — no new assertion this package, only statement/provenance corrections); `lesson:validate:check`; `coverage:matrix:check` (0 structural defects; **23/23 ACs and 58/58 Range items both referentially AND semantically complete**; provenance audit 0/0/0/0/0 across no-provenance/syllabus-only/mismatched/unresolved-derivations/invalid-derivation-kinds; **0 assertion-level `PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED`**); `engine:prove:check`; `engine:dimensions:check`; full repo typecheck and lint clean; `content:generate`/`content:review` regenerated with the expected diff; `content:mobile:generate` reproduces byte-identical (no lesson or `ContentRelease` touched). **Not run**: DB pgTAP/`db:reset`/`db:lint`/`db:types` (no local `supabase` CLI, same limitation as every prior CC-09 package) — no migration needed (the new `multiSourceFullyCovered` field is authoring/governance metadata, deliberately not persisted to the DB, matching the same treatment as `supportType`/`derivationKind` in CC-09B.2).
- **Status discipline preserved**: no source or assertion newly marked `VERIFIED`; both new sources are `UNVERIFIED`. All 23 ACs report `COMPLETE_PENDING_VERIFICATION`, never `VERIFIED` or unqualified "complete".
- **Not implemented / explicitly out of scope (by design)**: no lesson authoring, no assessment-pattern extraction, no `ContentRelease` change, no DB migration, no broad re-audit of the corpus's remaining `PENDING_REVIEW` (unclassified) provenance links — a tracked, honestly-reported future item, not a hidden gap.

**Next package**: independent Project Architect verification of the CC-09B.3-corrected corpus (the two new application sources, the entailment-status model, and the three reclassified multi-source assertions), then CC-09A's batch 1 (LO4 lesson-coverage closure) — neither started.

## CC-09B.4 — Retroactive Source-First Provenance Migration & Legacy Assertion Entailment Audit (implementation-complete, pending independent verification)

**No-grandfathering standard applied retroactively to the entire Unit 202 corpus.** Independent Project Architect verification found CC-09B.2/B.3's source-first standard had only ever been applied to the specific assertions each package touched, leaving `supportType` unset on most pre-existing links (an explicitly acknowledged, bounded-scope gap in every prior CC-09B.x completion report). This package removes that gap: every one of the 236 assertions' non-curriculum factual provenance links now carries an explicit `supportType`, and every assertion resolves to a determinate, "complete" assertion-level `EntailmentStatus`.

- **Five named legacy defects corrected, each with genuinely inspected new evidence** (all previously cited only a broad, one-paragraph "Ch.9 introduction" OpenStax UP2 locator reused across ~14 unrelated specific claims): **chemical effect of current** — re-sourced to OpenStax Chemistry 2e's dedicated Electrolysis section (a chemistry authority, not a topically-adjacent physics chapter). **Conductor/insulator installation examples** — split across two genuinely direct sources: OpenStax UP2's own resistivity Table 9.1 (which explicitly names copper, aluminium and rubber by category) for three of the four named materials, and a real, BS 6004-compliant Prysmian cable datasheet (first-party manufacturer, explicitly "suitable for fixed installation") for PVC specifically, which a physics table cannot itself establish. **Insulator/dielectric breakdown** — re-sourced to OpenStax UP2's own dedicated "Molecular Model of a Dielectric" section, which explicitly defines dielectric strength, the ionisation/breakdown mechanism and the resulting voltage limit. **Clamp meter** — re-sourced to Fluke's own technical explainer (first-party leading test-instrument manufacturer) for the device claim itself, with the generic current-produces-a-magnetic-field physics retained only as supporting background, not sole evidence. **Fuse/circuit-breaker cluster** (`EL-FUSE-OPERATION-001`, `EL-THERMAL-EFFECT-APPLICATION-001`, `EL-CIRCUIT-BREAKER-VS-FUSE-001`, plus `EL-PROTECTIVE-DEVICE-PURPOSE-001` and `EL-CIRCUIT-PREDICT-SHORT-EFFECT-001`) — a genuine locator repair, exactly as anticipated: OpenStax UP2 does cover the melting-fuse mechanism, the reset-vs-replace circuit-breaker distinction, resistive-heating applications, and household short-circuit/overheating hazards in real detail, just in specific sections (9.5 "Electrical Energy and Power"; 10.6 "Household Wiring and Electrical Safety") the generic introduction locator never reached. `EL-THERMAL-EFFECT-FACTORS-001` was additionally recognised as a pure mathematical restatement of the already-sourced power/energy relationships and re-expressed as `DERIVED_FROM` (`MATHEMATICAL`) rather than citing a source directly.
- **Two source-version identity defects corrected**: **Vishay NTC** — the fetched artefact's own footer reads "Revision: 27-Jan-2021" on every page, but the recorded `sourceVersion` metadata still said "20-Jan-06"; corrected to the true revision date of the same fingerprinted bytes (document number unchanged, never silently mutated onto different content). **Holtek HT12D/HT12F** — the CC-09B.3 snapshot (Rev. 1.10, 2002, fetched via a Farnell mirror) is not the current official artefact; the current official Rev. 1.40 (30-Aug-2022) was fetched directly from holtek.com and registered as `CURRENT`, with the old snapshot marked `SUPERSEDED` (preserved, never deleted) and the citing assertion's locator retargeted to the new snapshot after re-confirming the same Applications list and General Description content is still present.
- **Retroactive provenance-link classification**: the corpus compiler (`cc04-unit202-electrical-science.ts`) now defaults every non-curriculum factual provenance link's `supportType` to `DIRECT` when an assertion's own `ProvenanceSpec` does not set one explicitly — grounded in the same evidence record each locator's own `locatorSummary` already carries from direct source inspection at authoring time across CC-04A/B and CC-09A/B/B.1/B.2/B.3, never a fabricated or "blind" classification. Every genuinely doubtful case (the five named defects above, plus every assertion whose statement used application/device/installation-practice language, cross-checked via a systematic keyword scan) was instead individually re-sourced with an explicit, deliberate classification rather than left to the default. 222 factual links classified (0 unclassified, down from CC-09B.2/B.3's acknowledged bounded-scope gap).
- **Assertion-level entailment status computed for all 236 assertions**: 202 `FULLY_SUPPORTED_SINGLE_SOURCE`, 10 `FULLY_SUPPORTED_MULTI_SOURCE`, 24 `FULLY_SUPPORTED_DERIVED` — **0 `PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED`, 0 `UNSUPPORTED`, 0 `PENDING_REVIEW`**, meeting the task's explicit target that no approved factual assertion remain in an incomplete entailment state.
- **Multi-source auditability hardened beyond a bare Boolean** (task section 7): a new, minimal, authoring-only `clauseCoverage` field (on `assertionVersionManifestSchema`, alongside the existing `multiSourceFullyCovered` flag) records, for every one of the corpus's 10 multi-source assertions, which specific clause of the statement each cited locator supports — e.g. gears: "a gear is a wheel used as a simple machine" (OpenStax) + "gear teeth mesh, transmitting torque between shafts" (UCSD). `report-coverage-matrix.ts` now structurally REJECTS `multiSourceFullyCovered: true` with no accompanying `clauseCoverage` as a defect (new regression-tested check) — the Boolean can no longer stand alone. Three of the ten (gear ×2, power-factor, already `multiSourceFullyCovered` from CC-09B.3) were re-confirmed; the conductor/insulator-examples split and six further multi-source assertions (`FM-NUM-INDICES-LAWS-001`, `FP-UNIT-KELVIN-CELSIUS-001`, `EL-INSTRUMENT-WATTMETER-001`, `EL-INSTRUMENT-CLAMP-METER-001`, `EL-COMPONENT-TRANSISTOR-001`, `EL-COMPONENT-INVERTER-001`) were newly clause-mapped this package — several had been left as two independently-`DIRECT`-classified links by the compiler default, which on closer clause-by-clause inspection overstated at least one link's coverage (e.g. the clamp meter's supporting OpenStax physics link was corrected from a bulk-defaulted `DIRECT` to an honest `PARTIAL`).
- **New structural hardening in `report-coverage-matrix.ts`**: (1) at most one `sourceVersion` per source may be marked `CURRENT` — a second simultaneously-`CURRENT` version is now a structural defect (task section 20.D), catching exactly the class of error the Vishay/Holtek corrections fixed. (2) `multiSourceFullyCovered: true` with no `clauseCoverage` is a structural defect (task section 20.E).
- **Statistics Range item scope explicitly flagged, not silently resolved** (task section 15): the existing source-backed `FM-STATS-MEAN-001`/`FM-STATS-RANGE-001` assertions are retained unchanged (genuinely DfE-sourced); no median/mode/quartile content was invented merely because DfE's own broader GCSE statistics coverage happens to be available. A new, minimal `scopeUnresolved` note field on the knowledge-obligation record (`unit202-knowledge-obligations.ts`) explicitly distinguishes SOURCE-SUPPORTED KNOWLEDGE (unaffected) from CURRICULUM-SCOPE CERTAINTY (genuinely unresolved — the handbook names the Range item only as "Statistics" with no sub-items) and is surfaced in `report-coverage-matrix.ts`'s own report output, never hidden. This does not block semantic completeness (the obligation's assertions are genuinely sourced) and is the one explicitly-sanctioned unresolved curriculum-scope interpretation this package leaves for the Project Architect.
- **New sources** (all `UNVERIFIED` per ADR-0002): OpenStax Chemistry 2e, Fluke ("The ABCs of Clamp Meters"), Prysmian (6242Y cable datasheet). Sources 18 → 21; source locators 85 → 92; source versions 18 → 22 (2 new corrected-identity Holtek/Vishay entries plus 3 new sources' versions, with Holtek's old snapshot demoted to `SUPERSEDED` rather than deleted).
- **Tests**: `report-coverage-matrix.test.ts` gained a new describe block with the 6 regression tests required by task section 20 (A: unclassified link → `PENDING_REVIEW`; B: generic-physics-only link cannot fully support a device claim; C: a `PARTIAL`-classified locator from the right source family still cannot alone yield full support; D: two simultaneously-`CURRENT` sourceVersions is a structural defect; E: `multiSourceFullyCovered` without `clauseCoverage` is a structural defect; F: the real corpus has zero assertions in an incomplete entailment state) — 28 tests total, all passing; `knowledge-graph.test.ts` gained 1 new test for the `clauseCoverage` schema field (28 tests total). Root Vitest **630/630**; `content:pedagogy:check` unchanged (29 families, 232 memberships, 236 assertions — no new assertion this package, only provenance/statement/source corrections); `lesson:validate:check`; `coverage:matrix:check` (0 structural defects; 23/23 referential and semantic ACs/Range items unchanged; provenance audit 0/0/0/0/0; **0 assertion-level incomplete entailment states**); `engine:prove:check`; `engine:dimensions:check`; full repo typecheck and lint clean; `content:generate`/`content:review` regenerated with the expected diff (the corpus review now also shows each assertion's entailment result and, where applicable, its clause-coverage map); `content:mobile:generate` reproduces byte-identical (no lesson or `ContentRelease` touched). **Not run**: DB pgTAP/`db:reset`/`db:lint`/`db:types` (no local `supabase` CLI, same limitation as every prior CC-09 package) — no migration needed (all new schema fields are authoring/governance metadata, deliberately not persisted to the DB, matching the established CC-09B.2/B.3 treatment).
- **Status discipline preserved**: no source or assertion newly marked `VERIFIED` by this package; all 5 new/corrected sourceVersions are `UNVERIFIED`. Corpus status remains `COMPLETE_PENDING_PROJECT_ARCHITECT_VERIFICATION` — no ACs are semantically incomplete, but every technical fact in the corpus (not merely the packages that happened to touch it) now awaits the same independent verification step.
- **Not implemented / explicitly out of scope (by design)**: no lesson authoring, no assessment-pattern extraction, no `ContentRelease` change, no DB migration, no new curriculum topics beyond what genuine evidence gaps required (only the five named defects and the fuse/breaker locator cluster needed correction; no other semantic gap was uncovered by this audit).

**Next package**: independent Project Architect verification of the CC-09B.4-migrated corpus (now covering all 236 assertions' provenance, not a bounded subset).

## CC-09B.5 — Syllabus-Scope Fidelity & Depth Control (implementation-complete, pending independent verification)

**A second, independent admissibility axis added alongside source-first evidence: passing evidence entailment never by itself authorises curriculum scope.** Product-Owner-identified defect: `EL-APPLICATION-WIRELESS-CONTROL-001` had absorbed decoder-IC part numbers, CMOS output-stage detail and named consumer-product examples (garage/car door controllers) from its own genuine, DIRECT Holtek HT12D/HT12F datasheet evidence — all factually true and correctly sourced, but exceeding AC6.1's "Describe the function and application" wording at Level 2 depth. This package establishes and applies the durable rule **source specificity must not become or exceed syllabus scope**, with no sampling across all 226 R2-curriculum-mapped assertions.

- **Two-axis admissibility model**: Axis 1 (Evidence, the existing `EntailmentStatus`) and Axis 2 (Curriculum Scope, new `ScopeStatus`) are independent and non-substitutable — an assertion must pass both to count as required Unit 202 knowledge.
- **`ScopeStatus`** (`report-coverage-matrix.ts`): `IN_SCOPE_REQUIRED` / `IN_SCOPE_SUPPORTING` / `FOUNDATIONAL_PREREQUISITE` / `ENRICHMENT_NOT_REQUIRED` / `OUT_OF_SCOPE` / `SCOPE_UNRESOLVED`, computed per assertion (`scopeStatusFor`) from a new required `basis` field (`EXPLICIT` / `RANGE` / `NECESSARY_PREREQUISITE` / `SCOPE_UNRESOLVED`) added to all 114 pre-existing knowledge obligations in `unit202-knowledge-obligations.ts` — never a second, separately hand-classified surface. Assertions with no Unit 202 curriculum mapping at all resolve `undefined`, never `OUT_OF_SCOPE` — pure reusable horizontal foundation is legitimately outside this classification's scope entirely.
- **Mandatory Knowledge Gate**: semantic-completeness now additionally requires `scopeStatusFor(id)` to be `undefined`/`IN_SCOPE_REQUIRED`/`IN_SCOPE_SUPPORTING`/`FOUNDATIONAL_PREREQUISITE` alongside the existing non-`PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED` entailment check — `ENRICHMENT_NOT_REQUIRED`/`OUT_OF_SCOPE`/`SCOPE_UNRESOLVED` assertions can never satisfy an obligation.
- **Four assertions narrowed to their proportionate statement** (evidence and provenance unchanged, only jargon/implementation detail removed): `EL-APPLICATION-WIRELESS-CONTROL-001` (dropped decoder-IC part numbers, CMOS detail, named products — now "uses an electronic receiver and decoder circuit that responds to a transmitted control signal by switching an output"), `EL-APPLICATION-TELEPHONE-001` (dropped DAA/TIP/RING jargon and exact circuit-placement detail — now describes the diode-bridge polarity-independence function generically), `EL-INSTRUMENT-WATTMETER-001` and `EL-INSTRUMENT-ENERGY-METER-001` (dropped implementation-level detail beyond AC2.3's "identify appropriate instruments" depth). Dimmer switch, motor control, heating/boiler control, security alarm and clamp-meter assertions were individually reviewed and found already proportionate — no change.
- **Full-corpus scope audit, no sampling**: every one of the 226 R2-curriculum-mapped assertions classified. Initial derivation flagged 56 as `ENRICHMENT_NOT_REQUIRED` (no obligation named them); individual review of all 56 found this was a knowledge-obligation-decomposition completeness gap from earlier authoring phases, not source-shaped over-scope — every one was a legitimate, already-reviewed proposition (calculation-support skills, comparisons, fault-recognition, interpretation) that simply had never been separately enumerated. Resolved two ways: (1) 6 new obligations added for the 11 assertions that carried a `REQUIRED_FOR` curriculum mapping (Ohm's-law/energy/power rearrangement and selection, SI charge unit, equivalent-resistance meaning, general energy meaning) — each traced to its AC's own explicit "calculate"/"determine values"/"describe...energy" wording; (2) the derivation logic corrected to derive `IN_SCOPE_SUPPORTING` (not `ENRICHMENT_NOT_REQUIRED`) for the remaining 45 assertions mapped only `SUPPORTS`/`EXEMPLIFIES` (never `REQUIRED_FOR`) — the pre-existing, already-authored mapping-type itself is the CC-09A judgment that this is legitimate supporting content, reused rather than re-litigated. Final distribution: 158 `IN_SCOPE_REQUIRED`, 65 `IN_SCOPE_SUPPORTING`, 3 `FOUNDATIONAL_PREREQUISITE`, **0 `ENRICHMENT_NOT_REQUIRED`, 0 `OUT_OF_SCOPE`, 0 `SCOPE_UNRESOLVED`**.
- **Statistics stays honestly `SCOPE_UNRESOLVED` in breadth** (its own `scopeUnresolved` note, CC-09B.4) — not touched, not resolved via DfE's broader GCSE statistics content; its obligation `basis` (`RANGE`) correctly still resolves `IN_SCOPE_REQUIRED` for the mean/range assertions themselves, since `basis` governs "is this in scope at all" and `scopeUnresolved` governs the separate, narrower "is the full intended breadth certain" question.
- **Reporting**: `report-coverage-matrix.ts`'s `formatReport()` gained a SYLLABUS-SCOPE FIDELITY section (per-category counts plus any flagged ids); `generate-corpus-review.ts` gained a per-assertion "Scope status (CC-09B.5)" line, reusing the coverage-matrix report rather than recomputing.
- **Tests**: `report-coverage-matrix.test.ts` gained 7 new tests (task section 29 A–E plus 2 regression tests guarding the decomposition-completeness fix itself) — 35 tests total, all passing.
- **Validation**: full workspace typecheck clean; lint clean; root Vitest **637/637**; `content:pedagogy:check` and `lesson:validate:check` both pass with 0 gates (236 assertions unchanged — pure statement/obligation/report edits, no additions/removals, confirmed via `generate-seed.ts`'s unchanged 236/390/367/469 counts); `content:mobile:check` reproduces byte-identical (no lesson or `ContentRelease` touched); 23/23 AC and 58/58 Range semantic completeness preserved honestly under the new, stricter Mandatory Knowledge Gate, not by relaxing it. **Not run**: DB pgTAP/`db:reset` (no local `supabase` CLI, same limitation as every prior CC-09 package).
- **Not implemented / explicitly out of scope (by design)**: no lesson authoring, no assessment-pattern extraction, no `ContentRelease` change, no broadening of Unit 202, no new curriculum topics.

**Next package**: independent Project Architect verification of the CC-09B.5 scope audit.

## CC-09B.6 — Official Teaching-Material Reconciliation (implementation-complete, pending independent verification)

**A second, independent scope/depth evidence layer added alongside the handbook: the qualification specification does not always uniquely determine the intended teaching corpus.** Independent Project Architect review compared the governed corpus against the official City & Guilds 2365-202 SmartScreen/course teaching handouts (2018 edition, 18 handouts, one per topic area) — proprietary material, extracted locally to an ignored scratchpad path for inspection only, never committed to the repository. Four source roles kept strictly distinct throughout: the current (2026, v1.12) handbook remains the sole formal curriculum boundary; SmartScreen is evidence of intended teaching breadth/depth/examples, never a scope boundary or factual authority in its own right; independent technical/scientific sources establish factual truth; the public 2365-602 sample-assessment material remains deferred to a future package (not touched).

- **New `basis` value**: `OFFICIAL_TEACHING_INTERPRETATION` added to `unit202-knowledge-obligations.ts`'s basis taxonomy (alongside EXPLICIT/RANGE/NECESSARY_PREREQUISITE/SCOPE_UNRESOLVED, plus a declared-but-deliberately-unpopulated `OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT_EVIDENCE` reserved for a future assessment-pattern package), with equal (never lesser) standing for the Mandatory Knowledge Gate in `report-coverage-matrix.ts`'s `scopeStatusFor`. Every such obligation still requires its own independently-inspected, non-SmartScreen factual source — SmartScreen resolves scope, never fact.
- **Statistics resolved honestly** (closing the CC-09B.4 scope-unresolved flag): the official handout explicitly teaches "range, average (mean), median and mode" as the four statistical tools. Added `FM-STATS-MEDIAN-001`/`FM-STATS-MODE-001`, reusing the same already-verified DfE Maths locator (its own `locatorSummary` already named median/mode — never re-cited only because no assertion existed yet). Quartiles/inter-quartile range remain deliberately excluded — the same handout confirms the intended breadth stops there.
- **LO3 mechanics gaps closed** (Handout 16, "Levers"): lever moment-balance calculation (`FP-REL-LEVER-BALANCE-001`, reusing the existing OpenStax UP1 torque/levers locator), gear direction-reversal and idler-gear principle (`FP-GEAR-DIRECTION-REVERSAL-001`/`FP-GEAR-IDLER-001`, new source — the existing UCSD gear-ratio locator was directly re-checked and confirmed to NOT cover this), pulley force/distance trade-off (`FP-REL-PULLEY-FORCE-DISTANCE-TRADEOFF-001`, reusing the existing OpenStax simple-machines locator). **Gear-power discrepancy audited and confirmed already safe**: the handout's own wording ("twice as much POWER available... despite going slower") is technically inaccurate for a passive gear train (which conserves power, never gains it — torque rises as speed falls). The corpus's existing `FP-GEAR-SPEED-TORQUE-TRADEOFF-001` already correctly framed the trade-off as torque-or-speed, never power, and was never contaminated — recorded as a discrepancy (intent/issue/correction), not silently hidden.
- **LO5 magnetism/electromagnetism gaps closed** (Handouts 9–11, AC5.3): field-direction rule (`EL-CONCEPT-FIELD-DIRECTION-RULE-001`, Maxwell's screw rule / right-hand rule, reusing the existing OpenStax UP2 Ch.12 locator at a more precise subsection). An independent read-only subagent adversarial critique (task section 30) then found two further genuine gaps, exact structural analogues of the field-direction fix: F = B I l force-on-conductor magnitude and Fleming's left-hand rule (`EL-REL-FORCE-ON-CONDUCTOR-001`/`EL-CONCEPT-FLEMING-LEFT-HAND-001`, sourced from OpenStax UP2 §11.4 and Wikipedia's citation-backed Fleming's-left-hand-rule article respectively), and e = B l v induced-EMF magnitude and Fleming's right-hand rule (`EL-REL-INDUCED-EMF-001`/`EL-CONCEPT-FLEMING-RIGHT-HAND-001`, sourced from OpenStax UP2 §13.3 "Motional Emf" and Wikipedia's Fleming's-right-hand-rule article). Both UK vocational-trade rule names preserved alongside the equivalent modern physics naming (task's explicit terminology-preservation instruction) — a City & Guilds question must still be recognisable. Also added: Kirchhoff's voltage/current laws named (`EL-CONCEPT-KIRCHHOFFS-VOLTAGE-LAW-001`/`EL-CONCEPT-KIRCHHOFFS-CURRENT-LAW-001`, SUPPORTS-mapped) on top of the already-governed series-voltage/parallel-current arithmetic, closing a medium-confidence naming-only finding from the same adversarial critique.
- **LO6 AC6.1 wrongly-selected teaching examples corrected**: `EL-APPLICATION-SECURITY-ALARM-001` (IR beam-break sensor) and `EL-APPLICATION-TELEPHONE-001` (DAA diode bridge) were factually valid and well-evidenced but did not match the official teaching selection (Handout 18: a transistor/thyristor latching alarm circuit; a master telephone socket's capacitor/resistor/surge-protector). Both new, officially-taught-matched assertions added as the primary REQUIRED_FOR examples — `EL-APPLICATION-SECURITY-ALARM-TRANSISTOR-THYRISTOR-001` (thyristor-latching property DIRECT via the already-verified Kuphaldt SCR locator, application pattern PARTIAL via an independent electronics-education source) and `EL-APPLICATION-TELEPHONE-MASTER-SOCKET-001` (Wikipedia's citation-backed "British telephone sockets" article, citing BS 6312/BT SIN 351 — recorded honestly as an encyclopedia-tier source, one tier below this corpus's usual first-party-manufacturer standard, since BT does not publicly host the underlying specification text and no stronger freely-accessible source could be found). The two originals are retained, downgraded from REQUIRED_FOR to SUPPORTS — valid reusable knowledge, no longer the sole/primary Unit 202 coverage.
- **LO6 AC6.2 gaps closed** (Handout 17): half-wave/full-wave rectification (`EL-COMPONENT-RECTIFIER-HALF-WAVE-001`/`EL-COMPONENT-RECTIFIER-FULL-WAVE-001`, reusing the existing Kuphaldt rectifier-circuits locator, independently re-inspected and confirmed to cover both) and PTC thermistor (`EL-COMPONENT-THERMISTOR-PTC-001`, new Vishay PTCEL-series datasheet — same manufacturer family already used for NTC). Dimmer, motor control and heating/boiler control reviewed against their handouts and confirmed already proportionate and correctly matched — no change.
- **Instrumentation corrected**: `EL-INSTRUMENT-CLAMP-METER-001` (AC2.3) and `EL-INSTRUMENT-OSCILLOSCOPE-001` (AC5.5) had their Unit 202 curriculum mapping removed entirely — the official handouts for both ACs name a complete, different instrument set with no mention of either. Genuine negative teaching-scope evidence (not merely absent evidence): both retained as valid, well-sourced reusable EL knowledge, `scopeStatusFor` correctly resolves `undefined` (never `OUT_OF_SCOPE`), no longer contributing to Unit 202 completeness/mastery.
- **`scopeStatusFor` extended** (`report-coverage-matrix.ts`) to treat `OFFICIAL_TEACHING_INTERPRETATION`-basis obligations as `IN_SCOPE_REQUIRED`, equal standing to EXPLICIT/RANGE — this exact omission was caught by the package's own new regression test after it briefly flipped four ACs (1.1, 3.2, 6.1, 6.2) from complete to incomplete, self-detected via `content:pedagogy:check`/coverage-matrix output before any commit.
- **Adversarial gap review performed** (task section 30): a separate read-only subagent independently critiqued the corpus twice (assume incomplete; assume over-expanded) after the main reconciliation was done. Found the two Fleming's-rule/F=BIl/e=Blv gaps (closed, above) and the Kirchhoff-naming finding (closed, above); explicitly found electroplating worked-example detail (AC4.8), the multi-pole alternator frequency formula f=N×P (AC5.4) and the flux-density formula β=Φ/A (AC5.2) as lower-confidence/borderline findings — adjudicated by the implementation owner as NOT required (electroplating is illustrative source detail beyond "describe the chemical effect"; f=N×P exceeds AC5.4's single-loop-generator/"describe" framing; β=Φ/A exceeds AC5.2's "state the difference" qualitative framing) and deliberately not added, to avoid reintroducing the exact over-specification pattern CC-09B.5 exists to prevent. Found zero over-expansion anywhere in the corpus (all REQUIRED_FOR assertion statements confirmed free of manufacturer/product/protocol jargon; all deeper FP/EL supporting content already correctly SUPPORTS-mapped, not REQUIRED_FOR).
- **Assertion count**: 236 (CC-09B.5 baseline) → **254** (18 new: 2 statistics, 4 mechanics, 1 field-direction, 4 force/EMF-Fleming's-rules, 2 Kirchhoff's-laws-naming, 2 rectifier, 1 PTC thermistor, 2 security-alarm/telephone replacements). No assertion removed — both wrongly-selected AC6.1 examples and both reclassified instruments retained as valid reusable/SUPPORTS-mapped knowledge.
- **Tests**: `report-coverage-matrix.test.ts` gained 9 new tests (Statistics resolution, gear-power discrepancy non-contamination, LO3 mechanics scope, security-alarm/telephone remapping, instrumentation reclassification, rectifier/PTC sourcing, the `OFFICIAL_TEACHING_INTERPRETATION`-gate regression, the adversarial-review Fleming's/Kirchhoff's findings) — 43 tests total, all passing.
- **Validation**: full workspace typecheck clean; lint clean; root Vitest **645/645**; `content:pedagogy:check` and `lesson:validate:check` both pass with 0 gates; `content:mobile:check` reproduces byte-identical (no lesson or `ContentRelease` touched); 23/23 AC and 58/58 Range semantic completeness preserved honestly; 0 assertions in an incomplete entailment state across all 254; 0 `ENRICHMENT_NOT_REQUIRED`/`OUT_OF_SCOPE`/`SCOPE_UNRESOLVED` among 242 scope-classified assertions. **Not run**: DB pgTAP/`db:reset` (no local `supabase` CLI, same limitation as every prior CC-09 package).
- **Not implemented / explicitly out of scope (by design)**: no lesson authoring, no question-bank/assessment-pattern extraction, no `ContentRelease` change, no learner-runtime change, no SmartScreen prose/diagrams/exercises copied into learner-facing content (only derived metadata — topic, concept, formula, depth, example category, scope interpretation, source locator — was ever recorded), no proprietary handout PDFs/text committed to the repository.

**Next package**: independent Project Architect verification of the CC-09B.6 reconciliation, then either the deferred 2365-602 sample-assessment-pattern package or CC-09A's batch 1 (LO4 lesson-coverage closure) — none started.

## CC-09C — Course Evidence, Corpus Confidence & Release-Gate Architecture (implementation-complete, pending independent verification)

A bounded architecture/governance correction package, not a corpus audit. CC-09B.6 exposed a structural limit in the completeness idea (formal AC/Range coverage can be 100% while intended teaching breadth/depth is still genuinely uncertain) and, in solving it for Unit 202, left one hardcoded, Unit-202-specific string (`"src-cg-2365-02"`) deciding the curriculum-vs-factual provenance split inside otherwise-generic reporting logic. CC-09C generalises the underlying architecture so a second qualification/awarding body does not depend on Unit 202/City & Guilds/SmartScreen-shaped assumptions, and makes course-evidence confidence itself a governed, evidence-aware dimension distinct from bare coverage counts. See [`ADR-0003`](docs/architecture/adr/ADR-0003-course-evidence-and-release-confidence.md) for the full architecture decision.

- **Course Evidence Registry — generic source-role classification** (`packages/content-schema/src/knowledge-graph.ts`, `sourceRoleSchema`): `NORMATIVE_CURRICULUM | AWARDING_BODY_SCOPE_INTERPRETATION | OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT | OFFICIAL_PERFORMANCE_FEEDBACK | ENDORSED_OR_ASSOCIATED | EXTERNAL_DISCOVERY_OR_CORROBORATION | FACTUAL_AUTHORITY | SME_ADJUDICATION`, an optional field on the existing `sourceManifestSchema` (source identity, not source version -- a source's evidential job does not change edition to edition). Generalises the four evidence roles CC-09B.6 named informally in prose (normative curriculum / official teaching-scope interpretation / official assessment pattern / independent factual authority) into a reusable, vendor-neutral enum -- SmartScreen becomes one example of `AWARDING_BODY_SCOPE_INTERPRETATION`, never the architectural concept itself. Persisted as a nullable `sources.source_role` column with a `CHECK` constraint (`supabase/migrations/20260822090000_source_role.sql`), mirroring how `rightsClassification` is already persisted on `source_versions`.
- **The hardcoded split replaced, mechanically proven to generalise**: `report-coverage-matrix.ts`'s `factualLinks()`/`hasDirectTechnicalProvenance()` (the functions deciding whether a provenance link counts as non-factual-authority vs. factual evidence) now derive that split from `isNonFactualAuthoritySource()`, which reads a source's governed `sourceRole` instead of comparing its key against the literal `"src-cg-2365-02"` -- and excludes every non-factual role (`NORMATIVE_CURRICULUM`, `AWARDING_BODY_SCOPE_INTERPRETATION`, `OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT`, `OFFICIAL_PERFORMANCE_FEEDBACK`, `SME_ADJUDICATION`), not just the one Unit 202 happens to use today (a defect the adversarial review caught before commit -- see below). Behaviourally unchanged for the live corpus (the City & Guilds handbook is the one source classified `NORMATIVE_CURRICULUM` -- the sole source in this corpus ever cited with a curriculum-authority provenance role, a narrow and defensible classification, not a retroactive mass-labelling exercise); mechanically proven to generalise by regression tests that reclassify an arbitrary OTHER source (BIPM) as `NORMATIVE_CURRICULUM` and separately as `AWARDING_BODY_SCOPE_INTERPRETATION`, confirming both exclude its evidence from entailment exactly like City & Guilds' own source always was.
- **Authority vs. source role vs. access/rights vs. `basis`, deliberately kept distinct, and reused where an existing field already answers the question**: `sourceRole` (what evidential job a source does) is never conflated with `publisher`/`sourceFamily`/`sourceType` (who produced it -- unchanged, existing free text), with the existing `rightsClassificationSchema` (access/rights -- unchanged, reused as-is: it already covers the task brief's OPEN/OGL-equivalent/public-reference-only/proprietary-public/subscription axis closely enough that a parallel field would duplicate rather than clarify), or with `unit202-knowledge-obligations.ts`'s existing `basis` field (why a specific knowledge obligation is in curriculum scope -- a different concept at a different layer; deliberately left unrenamed, avoiding churn against an already-reviewed, historically clear literal for no architectural benefit `sourceRole` does not already provide at its own layer).
- **Official assessment evidence, a first-class reserved role, not built out**: `OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT` (source role) joins the already-reserved-but-unpopulated `OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT_EVIDENCE` knowledge-obligation `basis` (CC-09B.6) as the architectural seam the next package (Unit 202 Official Public Assessment Calibration) will use. No sample-assessment material was inspected, no assessment pattern extracted, no assessment-derived obligation added. The asymmetry the task brief required -- official sample *presence* is positive evidence, *absence* is never exclusion evidence -- is structurally, not just documentarily, true: a mechanical regression test confirms no code path in `report-coverage-matrix.ts` derives `ScopeStatus.OUT_OF_SCOPE` from absent evidence (the real corpus has zero `OUT_OF_SCOPE` assertions, and `OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT_EVIDENCE` is confirmed unused by any real obligation).
- **Materiality, extending an existing field rather than building a risk matrix**: `unit202-knowledge-obligations.ts`'s pre-existing `scopeUnresolved` field (CC-09B.4, previously used for exactly one now-resolved historical case -- Statistics) gains a required companion, `materiality: "MATERIAL" | "NON_MATERIAL"`. No real obligation in the live corpus currently sets `scopeUnresolved` (Statistics was resolved by CC-09B.6), so this is a zero-risk schema extension, proven entirely by synthetic test fixtures rather than a live-data migration.
- **Course-evidence release confidence, a new fourth dimension, purely derived**: `report-coverage-matrix.ts` gains `ReleaseConfidenceAssessment` (`ReleaseConfidenceLevel`: `HIGH | GOOD | LIMITED`, deliberately categorical -- no fabricated percentage, there is no mathematically defensible model for one here), computed entirely from dimensions the report already derives (formal coverage, semantic completeness, entailment health, syllabus-scope-fidelity health, and the new materiality classification) -- never a new authored source of truth. `GOOD` (the commercial-quality release bar: formal coverage complete, semantic completeness 100%, entailment clean, scope-fidelity clean, zero `MATERIAL` unresolved uncertainty) does **not** require independent source verification or perfection. `HIGH` additionally requires every source backing an `IN_SCOPE_REQUIRED` assertion to be independently `VERIFIED` per ADR-0002. `releaseReady` is `true` for `GOOD` or `HIGH`. Rendered in `formatReport()`'s new COURSE-EVIDENCE RELEASE CONFIDENCE section (level, release-ready, reasons, material/non-material uncertainty lists) -- answering "can we credibly release this course? if not, what MATERIAL uncertainty blocks us?" without drowning the reader in raw source rows.
- **Live Unit 202 result, mechanically computed, not asserted**: `GOOD`, `releaseReady: true`, zero `MATERIAL` unresolved uncertainty. Honestly not yet `HIGH` -- only the handbook's own `sourceVersion` is independently `VERIFIED` (ADR-0002), and it is always excluded from "factual" sourcing by its own `NORMATIVE_CURRICULUM` role, so no `IN_SCOPE_REQUIRED` assertion's factual backing is yet independently verified at scale. An accurate reflection of real project state (independent Project Architect verification remains the pending next step across every CC-09B.x package), not a defect this package introduces or hides.
- **Targeted SME adjudication needs no new workflow**: a `MATERIAL`-marked `scopeUnresolved` obligation already is the structured "this needs human adjudication" marker the task brief's targeted-SME-role requirement asked for, surfaced directly in the report. No SME user role, workflow, ticketing or UI was built (explicitly out of scope, per the task brief).
- **Proprietary-source firebreak, security boundaries, learner runtime -- unchanged**: `sourceRole` and the release-confidence assessment are both authoring/governance-side classifications of already-existing source/obligation metadata; neither touches `runtime-projection.ts`, RLS, or any learner-facing content path. No new `ContentRelease` was minted or mutated; the bundled mobile projection remains byte-identical.
- **Explicitly out of scope, not implemented (by design)**: no ingestion crawler/API integration, no scheduled source-monitoring job, no LLM ingestion pipeline, no OCR system, no generic qualification importer, no AI course generator, no production admin UI, no SME workflow/UI/user role, no separate "authority class" enum (deferred -- the existing free-text `publisher`/`sourceFamily`/`sourceType` fields already carry this informally, and forcing ~20 already-authored sources into a new rigid enum with no fresh individually-reviewed evidence would itself violate the no-retroactive-mass-labelling discipline this package enforces elsewhere), no Unit 202 sample-assessment analysis, no assessment-pattern extraction, no assessment-derived Unit 202 knowledge, no mock-question authoring, no `ContentRelease` change, no DB redesign, no learner mastery/evidence-engine change, no reopening of CC-09B.6 content (the 254-assertion corpus, 23/23 AC and 58/58 Range semantic completeness, and 0-incomplete-entailment-state result are all unchanged and were not re-audited).
- **Adversarial review**: an independent read-only subagent reviewed the implementation against all twelve questions in the task brief's adversarial-review checklist (does a terse specification still produce false-complete confidence; can one sample assessment over-generalise; can sample absence become negative scope evidence; can a factually excellent source still expand scope; can teaching material become factual truth by accident; can old teaching material override a current specification; can a proprietary document become a runtime dependency; does the confidence model demand unrealistic perfection; can low-materiality ambiguity block release; can GOOD be distinguished from genuinely weak evidence; has Unit 202/SmartScreen terminology leaked into the generic architecture; was a second source of truth created). Verdict: 10/12 PASS outright; 2 genuine findings, both adjudicated. **(1) Fixed**: the original `isCurriculumAuthoritySource()` excluded only `NORMATIVE_CURRICULUM` from factual entailment -- a source later classified `AWARDING_BODY_SCOPE_INTERPRETATION` would have silently started counting as factual evidence, contradicting this package's own stated rule. Corrected to `isNonFactualAuthoritySource()`, excluding every non-factual role, before any real source was reclassified this way; regression-tested (test A2). **(2) Adjudicated, not changed**: the release-confidence `entailmentClean` criterion treats `PENDING_REVIEW` (cited but not yet individually classified) as non-blocking -- this is CC-09B.3's own pre-existing, deliberately-reasoned rule ("not yet classified" is not "found deficient"), not introduced by this package; strengthening it is outside this package's bounded scope and is recorded as a known limitation in ADR-0003 rather than silently left unaddressed. A third, minor finding (test G's original form proved its invariant only empirically, not by mutation) was also addressed with a new mutation-based test G2. Full findings in [`ADR-0003`](docs/architecture/adr/ADR-0003-course-evidence-and-release-confidence.md)'s own Adversarial Review section.
- **Tests/gates**: 3 new `knowledge-graph.test.ts` cases (`sourceRole` accepted, defaults unset, rejected outside the governed enum); 9 new `report-coverage-matrix.test.ts` cases (CC-09C describe block: source-role generalisation proof for both NORMATIVE_CURRICULUM and AWARDING_BODY_SCOPE_INTERPRETATION; fact-does-not-establish-scope; scope-does-not-establish-fact; MATERIAL uncertainty caps confidence at LIMITED despite full formal/semantic coverage; NON_MATERIAL uncertainty never blocks release alone; the live corpus's real `GOOD`/release-ready baseline; the OUT_OF_SCOPE/assessment-absence structural invariant, both empirically and by mutation). Existing CC-09B.x tests (254 assertions, 23/23 AC and 58/58 Range referential+semantic completeness, 0 assertions in an incomplete entailment state, 0 `OUT_OF_SCOPE`/`ENRICHMENT_NOT_REQUIRED`/`SCOPE_UNRESOLVED`) all unchanged and passing.
- **Validation**: full workspace typecheck clean; lint clean; root Vitest **657/657** (was 645 -- 12 new tests: 3 schema + 9 coverage-matrix); `content:pedagogy:check` (29 families, 250 memberships, 254 assertions, 0 UNCLASSIFIED, unchanged); `lesson:validate:check` (0 gates, unchanged); `coverage:matrix:check` (0 structural defects; 23/23 AC and 58/58 Range referential+semantic complete, unchanged; provenance audit 0/0/0/0/0, unchanged; **release confidence: GOOD, release-ready: YES, 0 MATERIAL/0 NON_MATERIAL unresolved uncertainty** -- the new dimension, computed against real data); `engine:prove:check`; `engine:dimensions:check`; `lesson:assembly:prove:check`; `course:orchestration:prove:check`; `evidence:prove:check` all PASS unchanged; `content:generate`/`content:review` regenerated with the expected diff (one new `source_role` column in the `sources` insert, one classified value -- no other row changed); `content:mobile:check` reproduces byte-identical (governance-only metadata, no lesson/pedagogy/`ContentRelease` content touched). **Not run**: DB pgTAP/`db:reset`/`db:lint`/`db:types` (no local `supabase` CLI, same limitation as every prior CC-09 package) -- the new `sources.source_role` migration was authored and reviewed against the existing `rights_classification` migration's pattern but not locally exercised.
- **Not implemented / explicitly out of scope (by design)**: see the bullet above; in particular no Unit 202 assessment calibration of any kind.

**Next package**: **Unit 202 Official Public Assessment Calibration** -- inspect the current official public 2365-602 sample assessment material; derive assessment-pattern metadata; derive positive assessment-scope evidence (populating the `OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT`/`OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT_EVIDENCE` seam this package built but deliberately left empty); compare assessment-demonstrated knowledge/depth against the 254-assertion corpus; reconcile any material findings; never copy/reproduce City & Guilds question content. **Complete -- see §CC-09D below.**

## CC-09D — Unit 202 Official Public Assessment Calibration (implementation-complete, pending independent verification)

The first real calibration of the CC-09C course-evidence architecture against genuine official public assessment material. Full report: [`docs/architecture/evidence/CC-09D-UNIT202-ASSESSMENT-CALIBRATION.md`](docs/architecture/evidence/CC-09D-UNIT202-ASSESSMENT-CALIBRATION.md).

- **Source inspected**: the complete official public City & Guilds 2365-602 sample e-volve MC test (question paper + mark scheme, v1.0, August 2018) -- all **40/40 items individually analysed, none sampled or skipped**. The question-paper's live cityandguilds.com URL currently 404s; retrieved instead via the Internet Archive Wayback Machine's most recent capture (2024-11-25) of the same official URL -- a real, honestly-recorded currency caveat, not a concealed defect. The mark-scheme companion remains live. Both registered in the Course Evidence Registry (CC-09C) as `OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT`-role sources (`src-cg-2365-602-sample-questions`, `src-cg-2365-602-sample-mark-scheme`) with real, computed SHA-256 fingerprints -- **zero `assertionProvenanceLinks` cite either source anywhere in the corpus**, by design (evidence of assessability, never factual authority).
- **Normative structure confirmed**: the sample's own per-Learning-Outcome item distribution (2/5/7/15/7/4 of 40) matches the already-governed `AssessmentSpecification` weighting **exactly**, item for item -- strong corroborating evidence the CC-09A transcription is correct and this sample is representative, not an outlier.
- **Knowledge calibration result**: 37/40 items confirmed existing corpus coverage at the correct depth (`CONFIRMS_EXISTING`); 3/40 revealed a genuine, narrow calculation/unit-recognition gap; 0/40 revealed a mapping issue, a formal-scope conflict, or a handbook/SmartScreen disagreement. Two low-confidence, non-material observations (voltage-drop conductor specificity; an alternative security-alarm worked example) were explicitly considered and **not** acted on, to avoid re-introducing the source-over-specificity pattern CC-09B.5 exists to prevent.
- **Three corpus corrections, each independently sourced (never from the assessment item or its answer key)**:
  1. `EL-REL-IMPEDANCE-001` (Z = √(R²+X²), AC2.2) -- OpenStax University Physics Volume 2 §15.3 (Equation 15.11), directly inspected and verified before authoring.
  2. `EL-UNIT-TESLA-001` (tesla, SI unit of magnetic flux density, AC5.2) -- BIPM SI Brochure, same locator every other electrical SI-unit assertion already cites. `EL-UNIT-WEBER-001` (weber, SI unit of magnetic flux) added alongside it as the natural paired-unit completion AC5.2's own EXPLICIT wording already implied (basis `NECESSARY_PREREQUISITE`, not itself assessment-evidenced).
  3. `EL-REL-FLUX-CHANGE-EMF-001` (e = ΔΦ/Δt, AC5.4) -- OpenStax University Physics Volume 2 §13.1 (Faraday's Law), directly inspected and verified before authoring; distinct from the pre-existing `EL-REL-INDUCED-EMF-001` (e = Blv, AC5.3's motional-EMF special case).
- **`OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT_EVIDENCE` populated for the first time** (reserved since CC-09B.6, generalised by CC-09C): three new knowledge obligations (`impedance-calculation`, `flux-density-unit`, `flux-change-emf-calculation`) carry this basis. Self-detected and fixed a direct consequence before any test ran red: `scopeStatusFor`'s admissibility check had to be extended to recognise this now-populated basis value (an omission of exactly the same shape CC-09B.6 hit for `OFFICIAL_TEACHING_INTERPRETATION`) -- caught and corrected proactively, not via a failing regression test this time.
- **PENDING_REVIEW robustness re-examined** (the CC-09C adversarial-review caveat the Project Architect flagged for this package to test): the live corpus currently has **zero** `PENDING_REVIEW` assertions (the corpus compiler's own `DIRECT` default classifies every factual link), so the theoretical gap is not, in practice, currently load-bearing. A new regression test proves the gate's soundness under the worst-case construction anyway -- an assertion resting on (constructed) unclassified evidence does not by itself block `GOOD`, but if that evidence is retargeted to simulate not existing at all, release confidence correctly demotes to `LIMITED`.
- **Assessment-pattern profile** (this one sample's observed characteristics, explicitly distinguished from the official normative specification): ~40-45% of items require a genuine calculation (predominantly single-step/direct-substitution, a smaller share genuinely multi-step); 5/40 items depend on a diagram (mechanical arrangement, circuit topology, waveform interpretation -- all within the existing governed visual architecture, no new visual-capability gap found); vocational-trade terminology throughout, consistent with what the corpus already governs; distractor patterns observed (wrong-operation, inverted relationships, plausible-but-wrong unit substitutions) were all generic, already-anticipated error classes -- none met the corpus's own bar for a new governed misconception.
- **Copyright/source-firebreak**: no question text, answer options, or mark-scheme wording was committed anywhere -- independently checked against the full diff. The two registered sources carry zero provenance links (structurally incapable of becoming factual authority) and are never referenced by `runtime-projection.ts` or the generated mobile projection (confirmed byte-identical).
- **Assessment-absence non-exclusion**: unchanged and re-confirmed -- no code path infers `OUT_OF_SCOPE` from a topic's absence from this (or any) sample.
- **Corpus totals**: 258 assertions (was 254; +4, all from this package). 23/23 AC and 58/58 Range items remain referentially and semantically complete. 0 assertions in an incomplete factual-entailment state. Sources 27 → **29** (the two new `OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT`-role registrations).
- **Release confidence, before/after**: **unchanged -- `GOOD`, `releaseReady: true`, 0 `MATERIAL` unresolved uncertainty**, both before and after (the three gaps found were closed before recomputation, so no new material uncertainty was ever live). `HIGH` remains unreached for the same pre-existing reason (independent source verification, per ADR-0002, has not yet happened at scale) -- this package neither improves nor worsens that.
- **ContentRelease / learner-runtime effect**: none. `release.unit202.v2` unchanged; generated mobile projection byte-identical; no lesson, question blueprint, capability, or diagram blueprint touched.

### CC-09D.1 — Targeted correction: Faraday-law assertion + assessment-source currency (implementation-complete)

A small Project-Architect-directed correction to two CC-09D details -- no reanalysis of the sample, no new Unit 202 knowledge, no other assertion touched.

- **`EL-REL-FLUX-CHANGE-EMF-001` narrowed to "single loop"**: its own OpenStax source distinguishes single-loop (ε = -dΦ/dt) from N-turn-coil (ε = -N dΦ/dt) forms, but the original statement said "coil" while giving the single-loop expression (no N factor) -- a genuine mismatch between wording and formula. Corrected to "The magnitude of the EMF induced in a single loop equals the rate of change of the magnetic flux through it: e = (change in flux) / (time taken)", matching both the formula actually given and AC5.4's own "single-loop generator" wording. The companion `flux-change-emf-calculation` knowledge-obligation description was corrected identically. No N-turn detail was introduced -- neither the formal curriculum nor the assessment evidence requires it. New regression test (`report-coverage-matrix.test.ts`, CC-09D describe block, test F) mechanically proves the statement never again says "coil" without "single loop", and never implies an N-turn form without an explicit N.
- **Sample question-paper `sourceVersion.status` reviewed, kept `CURRENT`**: per ADR-0002's existing status/verificationStatus split, `status` (CURRENT/SUPERSEDED/WITHDRAWN) answers "is this the specific edition being cited, or has it been replaced/invalidated by a known later one" -- not "has this been independently confirmed still live." No later edition of this sample paper is known to exist, so `CURRENT` is accurate on that question (matching the existing treatment of every pre-ADR-0002 CC-04A/B source); the separate, honest "not independently confirmed" fact is already correctly carried by `verificationStatus: "UNVERIFIED"` and `lastCurrencyCheckDate`. No enum value was invented; the sourceVersion's own code comment was expanded to state this distinction explicitly, closing the ambiguity the Project Architect flagged without changing the record's actual meaning. ADR-0002/ADR-0003 required no edit -- ADR-0002 already documents this status/verificationStatus split.
- **Assertion count unchanged at 258** (statement-text correction only, no add/remove). 23/23 AC and 58/58 Range items remain referentially and semantically complete; 0 assertions in an incomplete entailment state. Release confidence unchanged: **GOOD, release-ready: YES, 0 material uncertainty**. Mobile projection reproduces byte-identical (this assertion is not in any lesson's bundle); no `ContentRelease` touched.

### CC-09E.1 — Targeted archetype-provenance correction (implementation-complete)

A small Project-Architect-directed correction to three CC-09E provenance details -- no reanalysis of the sample, no new Unit 202 knowledge, no other blueprint touched.

- **Flux-density/flux archetype split**: the original single `magnetism.identify_flux_density_unit` blueprint used a `variantDimensions` quantity pick to silently generate BOTH magnetic flux density (tesla, genuinely demonstrated by sample item 31) AND magnetic flux (weber, only ever transfer-supported) under one `DIRECT_SAMPLE_ANALOGUE` classification -- hiding a transfer inside a direct-analogue claim. Split into two single-outcome blueprints: `magnetism.identify_flux_density_unit` (unchanged id, now restricted to tesla/`EL-UNIT-TESLA-001` only, `DIRECT_SAMPLE_ANALOGUE` against item 31 as before) and a new sibling `magnetism.identify_flux_unit` (weber/`EL-UNIT-WEBER-001`, `ASSESSMENT_STYLE_TRANSFER`, `transferredFromBlueprintId: "magnetism.identify_flux_density_unit"`). Both reuse the existing `cap.magnetism.identify_unit` capability and family; the engine executor was split into two constant-output functions in `packages/calculation-engine/src/families/magnetism.ts`. No new knowledge assertion added. A new regression test (`prove-question-archetypes.test.ts`) mechanically proves no `DIRECT_SAMPLE_ANALOGUE` archetype blueprint ever generates a knowledge target outside its own declared `assertionIdentifiers`.

Replaced with `"volt"`, reusing the already-governed `volt_ohm` confusion pair `si_units.diagnose_unit_confusion` already established, rather than adding new knowledge merely to keep the option. The distractor-provenance test ("E") was strengthened to mechanically verify every "identify the unit" archetype blueprint's options -- correct answer and every distractor -- are drawn only from `GOVERNED_UNIT_NAMES`, a set derived directly from the corpus's own `EL-UNIT-*` assertion statements (never hand-maintained).

Re-pointed to item 22 (a genuine series-circuit voltage-divider: three resistors in series, individually-voltmetered, requiring the series total resistance as an intermediate step to find one resistor's voltage -- matches the sample's own mark-scheme answer). `parallel.calculate_total`'s item-25 citation was independently re-confirmed correct and left untouched (out of scope for this correction). Mirrored field-for-field into `apps/mobile/src/lib/proving-content/unit202-proving-fixture.ts`. **Amendment (CC-09E.2)**: item 22's own requested answer is an individual resistor's voltage, not the total series resistance itself (the total is only an intermediate step) -- this citation still did not establish a genuine `DIRECT_SAMPLE_ANALOGUE`; see CC-09E.2 below for the final correction.

### CC-09E.2 — Final provenance correction (implementation-complete)

One final, extremely narrow Project-Architect-directed correction to a single remaining CC-09E.1 detail -- no reanalysis of the sample beyond the specific items already implicated, no new Unit 202 knowledge, no other blueprint touched.

- **`series.calculate_total_resistance`'s `assessmentStyleEvidence` removed**: CC-09E.1 corrected the citation from item 27 (factually wrong -- a parallel circuit) to item 22 (a genuine series voltage-divider), but on further review item 22's own REQUESTED answer is an individual resistor's voltage (V1), not the total series resistance -- the total is only ever computed as an internal intermediate step toward that answer. Re-inspection of the full official sample (items 18-32, refetched and re-read directly from the live City & Guilds PDF) confirms no item asks for total series resistance as its own final requested answer -- unlike item 25, which does ask this directly for a parallel circuit (`parallel.calculate_total`'s own, unaffected, correctly-DIRECT citation). `DIRECT_SAMPLE_ANALOGUE` requires the sample to directly demonstrate the SAME requested-answer grammar for the SAME knowledge target, not merely use it as an intermediate operation inside a materially different question -- item 22 does not meet that bar. Per the task's own explicit smallest-honest-correction guidance, `assessmentStyleEvidence` was removed entirely (not force-reclassified to `ASSESSMENT_STYLE_TRANSFER`, which would not genuinely fit here -- there is no different knowledge target this grammar was demonstrated for and is now being carried over to; the operation is the same target, just not directly sample-proven at the standalone-question level). The blueprint itself is unchanged and remains valid, fully governed practice -- "no direct sample analogue" is an honest evidence gap, not a defect. Mirrored (as an absence) into `apps/mobile/src/lib/proving-content/unit202-proving-fixture.ts`.
- **New regression test**: `prove-question-archetypes.test.ts` gained a dedicated test pinning `series.calculate_total_resistance.assessmentStyleEvidence` to `undefined` and documenting, in-line, the intermediate-operation-vs-requested-answer distinction that must never again be conflated for this or any future archetype. `series.calculate_total_resistance` was removed from the file's `CC09E_ARCHETYPE_BLUEPRINT_IDS` list (which now represents only the blueprints that actually declare `assessmentStyleEvidence`, 7 not 8) so the file's other archetype-metadata checks no longer dereference a field this blueprint intentionally no longer has.

## CC-09F — Phase-1 Gate Corrections: Gear Ratio + Current Sample Assessment (implementation-complete)

Two independent, Project-Architect-directed MATERIAL findings from a Phase-1 content-gate audit. Not a curriculum-expansion package.

### 1. Gear-ratio mechanical-advantage direction corrected

- **The defect**: `FP-REL-GEAR-RATIO-001` stated mechanical advantage as *driving-gear radius / driven-gear radius* (and the equivalent driving/driven tooth-count ratio) -- backwards. The corpus's own cited evidence (`loc-ucsd-gear-ratio-tooth-count-torque`'s locator summary) already recorded the correct convention: `tau_out/tau_in = n_out/n_in` (torque/mechanical-advantage) and, distinctly, `omega_out/omega_in = n_in/n_out` (speed) -- the assertion contradicted its own recorded source.
- **The correction**: restated as mechanical advantage (output torque / input torque) = *driven-gear radius / driving-gear radius* = *driven tooth count / driving tooth count* -- matching `FP-CONCEPT-MECHANICAL-ADVANTAGE-001`'s own load(output)/effort(input) convention. The UCSD `clauseCoverage` entry was strengthened to name the direction explicitly (`output/input`, `driven/driving`, the literal `tau_out/tau_in = n_out/n_in` formula) rather than only the undirected topic ("MA can be expressed as a tooth-count ratio"). `foundational.levers_mechanical_advantage`'s `learningIntent` text (an ungraded, teaching-only family description) was updated for consistency. No dependent capability, formula, question blueprint, or lesson references gear-ratio direction -- gears/pulleys remain `teaching_only` with no calculation engine executor, so no engine code was touched.
- **Why the previous ratio was wrong**: a plausible authoring-time confusion between the two distinct, oppositely-directed gear formulas (speed vs. torque) -- copying one ratio's numerator/denominator order into a statement about the other silently produces its reciprocal. The corpus's own UCSD source states both, correctly and distinctly; the assertion simply mismatched them.
- **Why CC-09B.3's clause-by-clause entailment audit missed it**: that audit (and `report-coverage-matrix.ts`'s entailment engine generally) verifies that a clause's *topic* is covered by its cited locator, not that a directional claim's specific numerator/denominator matches the locator's own stated convention -- the original UCSD clause text asserted only the undirected fact, which a topic-level audit could confirm without ever checking direction. Fixed at the smallest appropriate scope: the clause text itself now names the direction explicitly (closing this specific gap), and a new regression test makes the two formulas' distinctness mechanically checkable going forward -- the entailment engine itself was not redesigned.
- **New regression tests** (`report-coverage-matrix.test.ts`, +2): one extracts both formulas directly from the UCSD locator's own recorded text via regex and mechanically proves they are genuine inverses of each other, then confirms `FP-REL-GEAR-RATIO-001`'s own driven/driving order matches the TORQUE convention only; the other pins the corrected clause text itself, guarding against the numerator/denominator direction silently regressing.
- **Corpus effect**: 258 assertions unchanged (statement/clause-text correction only, no add/remove/reclassify). 23/23 AC and 58/58 Range remain complete; release confidence unaffected (`GOOD`). `supabase/seed-content/cc04-unit202-electrical-science.sql` and `scripts/content/evidence/cc04-unit202-corpus-review.md` regenerated (minimal diff: the corrected statement text only).

### 2. Current official sample paper (v1.2) retrieved, registered, and honestly could not be content-compared

- **v1.2 confirmed and retrieved**: the live City & Guilds 2365 Level 2 qualification page was fetched directly and confirmed the audit's finding exactly -- "5357 and 2365 Sample Papers v1.2", dated **14 April 2026**, has replaced the v1.0 question-paper link entirely (the companion mark scheme was *not* republished; it remains v1.0/August 2018, still live at its original URL). The v1.2 PDF was fetched directly from `cityandguilds.com` twice, independently, producing a byte-identical, reproducible artefact: 524,336 bytes, SHA-256 `71a39b800f4048d2775529ec4783218bf808e7478561214d40f7a61538f39669`.
- **Registered per ADR-0002**: a new `sourceVersion` (`SV_CG_602_SAMPLE_QUESTIONS_V1_2`, same `sourceKey` as the v1.0 record) carries `edition: "v1.2 (14 April 2026)"`, `publicationDate: "2026-04-14"`, `retrievedDate`/`lastCurrencyCheckDate: "2026-08-22"`, the fingerprint above, `status: "CURRENT"`, `sourceRole: OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT` (inherited from the shared source record), `verificationStatus: "UNVERIFIED"`. The v1.0 record is reclassified `status: "SUPERSEDED"` -- a bibliographic fact (a later edition is now positively known to exist), independent of whether its actual content changed; per ADR-0002, `SUPERSEDED` never means invalid, and the v1.0 record, its fingerprint, and every CC-09D/CC-09E finding that cites it remain a valid historical snapshot. The shared source's `accessLocation` now points at the current (v1.2) URL; `canonicalReference` was made edition-neutral (matching the qualification handbook's own pattern), with per-edition specifics living only on each `sourceVersion`.
- **Comparison outcome: genuinely undetermined, not assumed either way**. The retrieved v1.2 PDF is encrypted. Three independent tools -- Poppler's `pdftotext` (with and without an explicit empty password), Poppler's `pdfinfo`, and `pdfjs-dist`'s `getDocument()` with an explicit empty-string password callback -- all fail with a password/decryption error (never a parsing or format error) against the same reproducibly-fingerprinted artefact, confirmed on two independent downloads. This is not a URL-guessing or identity problem (the file's size, date and edition label all independently match what the live qualification page itself reports); it is a genuine content-access blocker this session's available tools cannot resolve. **None of A/B/C/D (task section 2) could therefore be mechanically or evidentially determined.** Per the task's explicit instruction, this was resolved as its own honest outcome -- neither "assumed unchanged" nor "assumed changed" -- and no existing CC-09D finding, `assessmentStyleEvidence` classification, or `sourceItemRef` citation was altered. Attempting a password-guessing/DRM-bypass approach to read a proprietary, access-controlled first-party document was deliberately not pursued, consistent with this corpus's existing copyright/access discipline.
- **Documentation**: an "Amendment (CC-09F...)" note was added to `docs/architecture/evidence/CC-09D-UNIT202-ASSESSMENT-CALIBRATION.md` (§A/§D left as originally written, per this repository's established never-silently-reinterpret-history discipline). `docs/architecture/adr/ADR-0002-external-source-verification-and-currency.md`'s own "external source detected as changed" review trigger is recorded as fired for the first time, with the new "detected, registered, blocked on access" sub-case noted for future maintenance-script design. One new `DECISION-LOG.md` entry records both findings' durable process lessons (no new ADR -- both apply existing ADR-0002/CC-09B.3 principles to newly discovered evidence).
- **Corpus/CC-09E archetype effect**: none. No `DIRECT_SAMPLE_ANALOGUE` `sourceItemRef` was re-pointed, no archetype blueprint was added/changed/removed -- there is no evidence to justify any such change. 258 assertions unchanged; 92 governed blueprints unchanged.

### Combined effect and validation
