## CC-09G — Phase-1 Content Gate Corrections: Factual Precision & Family Mastery Completeness (implementation-complete)

A second, independent Project-Architect-directed Phase-1 content-gate audit -- seven bounded factual/provenance corrections plus a general family-mastery-completeness invariant. Not a curriculum-expansion package. The v1.2 official-sample comparison remains a separate, still-open Phase-1 gate item (CC-09F) -- not addressed or claimed resolved here.

1. **AC/DC definition corrected**: `EL-CONCEPT-AC-DC-DISTINCTION-001` claimed D.C. flows "with a constant value" -- over-broad, and internally contradicted by the corpus's own rectifier assertions (`EL-COMPONENT-RECTIFIER-001`/`-HALF-WAVE-001`/`-FULL-WAVE-001`, all governing pulsating D.C.). Corrected: D.C. flows in one direction with magnitude that may be steady or may vary (as with pulsating D.C.); A.C. periodically reverses direction and ordinarily varies in magnitude. No dependent capability, misconception, question blueprint, or engine code embedded the old "constant" wording (the `waveform.recognise_ac_dc` executor is a purely categorical ac/dc picker) -- only the assertion statement needed correction.
2. **Statistics definitions corrected and provenance re-sourced**: `FM-STATS-MEDIAN-001` defined median only as "the middle value" -- correct for an odd count, undefined for an even one. Corrected to state both cases explicitly (odd: middle ordered value; even: mean of the two middle ordered values). Separately, all four `FM-STATS-*-001` assertions' factual `DEFINES` provenance was re-sourced: the DfE GCSE Maths locator's own text (directly re-inspected) establishes these four measures as curriculum SCOPE only ("interpret, analyse and compare... through appropriate measures of..."), never their mathematical definitions -- it was never a valid `DEFINES` source. Registered a new source, the NIST/SEMATECH e-Handbook of Statistical Methods (`SRC_NIST_EDA_HANDBOOK`, two locators independently re-inspected: §1.3.5.1 for mean/median/mode, §1.3.5.6 for range), as the factual `DEFINES` evidence; DfE is retained `CURRICULUM_REQUIRES` (scope) only. SmartScreen was never, and remains never, factual authority. Statistics breadth unchanged (still exactly mean/median/mode/range, per CC-09B.6's SmartScreen-confirmed scope) -- no expansion.
3. **Motional-EMF geometry corrected**: `EL-REL-INDUCED-EMF-001` (e = Blv) specified velocity perpendicular to the field but left the conductor's own length/orientation unconstrained. Directly re-inspected against its cited OpenStax source (13.3 Motional Emf, rod-on-rails derivation), which requires the conductor's length, its velocity and the field to be mutually perpendicular for the simple form to hold. Corrected to name the full geometry (still no vector notation, Level-2-appropriate); the locator's own summary was strengthened identically. No provenance/entailment reclassification needed -- same source, same DIRECT support, just a more precise statement of what it actually establishes.
4. **Family-mastery completeness corrected (MATERIAL adaptive-learning defect) + new general invariant**: audited `completeness.requiredCapabilityIds` against each family's own governed capabilities and found six real gaps -- a capability existed, representing genuinely required curriculum/assessment knowledge, but was never wired into its family's mastery-completeness list, meaning a learner could reach family-secure state without that capability ever having been assessed:
   - **A. `foundational.statistics`**: title/learningIntent/capability description named only mean and range despite median/mode already being governed family members -- broadened to name all four (no new capability needed; the family's single `interpret` capability already covers them).
   - **B. `foundational.levers_mechanical_advantage`**: only the lever capability was required despite AC3.2 covering levers, gears AND pulleys, with governed gear/pulley assertions already family members. Added two new capabilities, `cap.foundational.gears.recognise` and `cap.foundational.pulleys.recognise` (mirroring the existing lever capability's shape), both now required.
   - **C. `foundational.mechanics_work_energy_power`**: `cap.foundational.mechanics.calculate` (added CC-09B.1 for work/kinetic-energy/potential-energy/power/efficiency calculation, all 5 AC3.4 EXPLICIT obligations) was never added to completeness -- the clearest, most unambiguous defect found. Added.
   - **D. `electrical.magnetism_and_electromagnetism`**: `cap.magnetism.identify_unit` (CC-09D/E, AC5.2 required, archetype-evidenced) was missing. Added.
   - **E. `electrical.emf_and_generation`**: `cap.emf.calculate_flux_change` (CC-09D/E, AC5.4 required, `OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT_EVIDENCE`, archetype-evidenced) was missing. Added.
   - **F. `electrical.ac_reactive_quantities`**: `cap.ac_reactive.select_impedance_formula` and `cap.ac_reactive.identify_reactance_unit` (CC-09E, both archetype-evidenced) were missing. Both added.

   **General invariant** (task's explicit ask, "using the existing architecture rather than a brittle Unit-202-only list"): `scripts/content/validate-pedagogy.ts` gained a new gate, `capabilitiesMissingFromFamilyCompleteness` -- for every governed `Capability`, its own `familyId`'s family must list it in `completeness.requiredCapabilityIds`, or it must appear in a small, individually-commented `NON_REQUIRED_CAPABILITY_IDS` allow-list with a governed rationale (currently empty -- every capability is required by its own family). This mechanically implements "a capability representing REQUIRED knowledge must either participate in family completeness, or be explicitly marked non-required with a governed rationale" using only existing architecture (`Capability.familyId` + `AssertionFamily.completeness`), wired into the same `content:pedagogy:check` gate as every other structural invariant. Confirmed clean (0 violations) immediately after the six fixes above -- no allow-list entries were needed.
5. **`FM-ARITH-RECIPROCAL-SUM-001` narrowed**: previously claimed "the reciprocal of a total formed from several parallel contributions" as if this were a general property of physical quantities connected in parallel -- it is not; it is a property of relationships taking the specific `1/T = 1/a + 1/b + ...` form. Restated as a domain-neutral arithmetic fact about that form (task's own suggested formulation), preserving its legitimate reuse for parallel-resistance calculation (which genuinely does take this form) without implying every "parallel" physical quantity obeys it.
6. **PTC thermistor assertion narrowed**: `EL-COMPONENT-THERMISTOR-PTC-001` generalised the Vishay PTCEL SWITCHING/inrush-limiting product family's own specific behaviour (sharp resistance rise at a defined switching temperature, overcurrent/overload protection) into a universal PTC claim -- source specificity exceeding syllabus scope. Narrowed to the essential, Unit-202-required contrast: PTC resistance increases with temperature (vs. NTC's decrease), matching the NTC assertion's own equally simple existing statement. The switching-temperature/inrush/overload detail remains only in the cited locator's own (already honestly product-scoped) summary -- not restated as universal required behaviour, and no new supporting assertion was added (smallest correction; the detail was judged not independently worth a separate governed assertion at this depth).
7. **Telephone master-socket assertion qualified (NON_MATERIAL)**: `EL-APPLICATION-TELEPHONE-MASTER-SOCKET-001` presented the traditional capacitor/resistor/surge-protector arrangement as an unqualified universal fact about every master socket. Qualified as "the traditional UK master telephone socket arrangement" (matching the SmartScreen teaching intent, per the existing CC-09B.6 comment above it); also corrected to match its own cited source's nuance more precisely -- the source states the surge protector was a feature of OLDER master sockets specifically, not a third universally-present component alongside the capacitor and resistor, which the previous wording had flattened. Learning point preserved (master vs. secondary socket component contrast).
- **Corpus/coverage/release-confidence effect**: 258 assertions unchanged (statement/provenance corrections only -- no assertion added or removed). 23/23 AC and 58/58 Range remain semantically complete; release confidence unchanged at **GOOD, release-ready: YES, 0 MATERIAL unresolved uncertainty** (mechanically recomputed, not assumed -- `coverage:matrix:check` re-run and confirmed GOOD is still the honest result, not forced). Sources: 29 → **30** (+1, the new NIST EDA handbook). SourceVersions: 31 → **32**. SourceLocators: 104 → **106** (+2, the two new NIST EDA locators). Assertion provenance links: 429 → **433** (+4, one new `CURRICULUM_REQUIRES` link per `FM-STATS-*-001` assertion, alongside its re-sourced `DEFINES` link). Governed capabilities: +2 (`cap.foundational.gears.recognise`, `cap.foundational.pulleys.recognise`) -- pedagogy-layer additions, not knowledge-graph assertions, so the 258 corpus count is unaffected.
- **Runtime/ContentRelease effect**: none. `release.unit202.v2` unchanged; mobile projection byte-identical (`content:mobile:check` PASS); no lesson, question blueprint, diagram blueprint, or learner-mastery/routing logic touched. No `ContentRelease` minted for these authoring/governance corrections, per the task's explicit boundary.
- **Validation**: full workspace typecheck and lint clean. Root Vitest **688/688** (was 686, +2 for the new `capabilitiesMissingFromFamilyCompleteness` regression tests). `coverage:matrix:check` (0 structural defects, 23/23 AC, 58/58 Range, release confidence GOOD unchanged); `content:pedagogy:check` (92 question blueprints, 258 assertions, all gates zero including the new one); `engine:prove:check` (92/92 supported, all families PASS); `engine:dimensions:check` (PASS); `lesson:validate:check` (0 gates, confirms no dangling reference introduced by the new capabilities); `content:mobile:check` reproduces byte-identical. `content:generate`/`content:review` regenerated with the expected, fully-accounted-for diff. **Not run**: DB pgTAP/`db:reset`/`db:lint`/`db:types` (no local `supabase` CLI; no schema/migration change was needed).
- **Files changed**: `scripts/content/data/cc04-unit202-electrical-science.ts`, `scripts/content/data/cc05a-pedagogy-unit202.ts`, `scripts/content/data/unit202-knowledge-obligations.ts`, `scripts/content/validate-pedagogy.ts`, `scripts/content/validate-pedagogy.test.ts`, the two regenerated artefacts (`scripts/content/evidence/cc04-unit202-corpus-review.md`, `supabase/seed-content/cc04-unit202-electrical-science.sql`), `docs/governance/DECISION-LOG.md`, this file.
- **Not implemented / explicitly out of scope (by design)**: no lessons authored, no mock exams built, no runtime AI, no reanalysis of the 40 assessment questions, no CC-09E archetype work reopened (none of the seven corrections touched archetype-evidenced content in a way requiring it), no curriculum-scope expansion, no general architecture refactoring, no `ContentRelease` minted, no attempt made or claimed toward resolving the still-open CC-09F v1.2 sample-comparison blocker.

## CC-09H — Phase-1 Content Gate Corrections: Instrumentation, Magnetism & Waveform Precision (implementation-complete)

A small, bounded Phase-1 content-gate correction tranche (four factual corrections; one assertion independently reviewed and left unchanged). Not a curriculum-expansion package. The CC-09F v1.2 sample-comparison blocker remains separately open, not addressed here.

- **Multimeter corrected**: `EL-INSTRUMENT-MULTIMETER-001` described "one meter movement connected to different external resistor networks via a selector switch" -- specifically an analogue multimeter's internal architecture, not true of digital multimeters and not required by the governed obligation (combined instrument + function selection only). Restated technology-neutral: "a single test instrument that can be configured to measure several electrical quantities, commonly including voltage, current and resistance." **Provenance kept unchanged** (`loc-kuphaldt-dc-multimeters`, DEFINES/DIRECT): the source's own passage genuinely still entails the narrower, corrected claim ("a multi-purpose meter... can be designed in one unit with the appropriate switch(es)") independent of its own extra, now-omitted analogue-mechanism detail -- a source being more specific than the assertion it supports is fine, per this corpus's existing source-specificity discipline (CC-09B.5/CC-09G), as long as the assertion never claims what only the source's extra detail states.
- **Magnetism corrected**: `EL-CONCEPT-MAGNETISM-001` equated magnetism itself with a force ("magnetism is a force of attraction... and repulsion"). AC5.1 asks for the *effects* of magnetism in terms of attraction/repulsion, not a definition of magnetism as a force. Restated: "Magnetic poles exert forces on one another: like poles repel and unlike poles attract" -- still fully entailed by the same OpenStax source. Confirmed no dependent capability, question blueprint, or family-membership tag repeats the old "magnetism is a force" framing (corpus-wide search, only the assertion itself and its derived artefacts matched).
- **Terminal-voltage precision (NON_MATERIAL)**: `EL-CONCEPT-TERMINAL-VOLTAGE-001` claimed terminal voltage is "slightly less" than EMF under load -- an unjustified magnitude qualifier (V_terminal = EMF - I r depends on current and internal resistance; the drop is not inherently small). Removed "slightly"; depth otherwise unchanged (discharging-source model only).
- **AC average-value wording clarified**: `EL-WAVEFORM-AVERAGE-VALUE-001` and `EL-WAVEFORM-AVERAGE-ZERO-INTERPRETATION-001` used the ambiguous phrase "the average of the rectified (half-cycle) waveform", conflating two distinct but equivalent procedures (averaging the original waveform over one half-cycle, versus averaging the full-wave-rectified waveform over a full cycle). Both assertions restated to name each procedure explicitly and state their equivalence, while keeping the existing "signed average over a full cycle is zero" fact intact.
- **DIAC classification independently reviewed, left unchanged**: `EL-COMPONENT-DIAC-001`'s "bidirectional thyristor" classification was checked against the task's own finding that reputable manufacturer literature supports it -- no correction made; not opportunistically rewritten.
- **Corpus/count effect**: 258 assertions unchanged (statement/provenance-comment corrections only -- no assertion added, removed, or re-sourced to a new source). Sources/sourceVersions/locators/provenance-links all unchanged from CC-09G's final counts (30/32/106/433) -- this package added no new source registrations. 23/23 AC and 58/58 Range remain semantically complete; family-completeness gate (`capabilitiesMissingFromFamilyCompleteness`, CC-09G) remains zero; release confidence unchanged at **GOOD, release-ready: YES, 0 MATERIAL unresolved uncertainty** (mechanically recomputed, not forced).
- **Runtime/ContentRelease effect**: none. `release.unit202.v2` unchanged; mobile projection byte-identical (`content:mobile:check` PASS); no `ContentRelease` minted for these content corrections.
- **Validation**: full workspace typecheck and lint clean. Root Vitest **688/688** (unchanged -- no new tests were needed; the corrections are statement-text/provenance-comment only, already covered by existing structural/entailment gates). `coverage:matrix:check` (0 structural defects, 23/23 AC, 58/58 Range, release confidence GOOD unchanged); `content:pedagogy:check` (92 question blueprints, 258 assertions, all gates zero); `engine:prove:check` (92/92 supported, all families PASS); `engine:dimensions:check` (PASS); `lesson:validate:check` (0 gates); `content:mobile:check` reproduces byte-identical. `content:generate`/`content:review` regenerated with the expected minimal diff (four assertion statements, one provenance comment). **Not run**: DB pgTAP/`db:reset`/`db:lint`/`db:types` (no local `supabase` CLI; no schema/migration change was needed).
- **Files changed**: `scripts/content/data/cc04-unit202-electrical-science.ts`, the two regenerated artefacts (`scripts/content/evidence/cc04-unit202-corpus-review.md`, `supabase/seed-content/cc04-unit202-electrical-science.sql`), this file.
- **Not implemented / explicitly out of scope (by design)**: no unrelated assertions added, no question-archetype work reopened, no lessons authored, no curriculum-scope expansion, no instrumentation/waveform architecture redesign, no opportunistic refactoring, no `ContentRelease` minted.
- **Remaining Phase-1 gate blockers**: the CC-09F v1.2 official-sample content comparison remains open (encrypted artefact) -- unchanged and unaddressed by this package.

## CC-09I — Final Phase-1 Corpus Audit Corrections (implementation-complete)

The remaining findings from the Project Architect's first full independent Unit 202 corpus audit -- the largest of the Phase-1 gate packages, spanning mastery-governance, provenance, wording precision, atomicity and a new general invariant. Not a curriculum-expansion package; no CC-09H correction reopened. The CC-09F v1.2 sample-comparison blocker remains separately open, not addressed here.

**1. Required-standalone correction (MATERIAL)**: `FP-CALC-POWER-001`, `FP-CALC-EFFICIENCY-001` and `FP-REL-WEIGHT-MASS-001` satisfy explicit AC3.4/AC3.1 knowledge obligations (`power-calculation`, `efficiency-calculation`, `weight-mass-relationship`) but were left `standalone` -- outside all family/mastery representation -- solely because they did not reach an Electrical assertion within the old CC-05B engine-proving slice. That proving-slice boundary is not, and must never become, a qualification-mastery boundary. Added to `foundational.mechanics_work_energy_power` and `foundational.mass_weight`'s `membersOf` respectively. `FP-CALC-WEIGHT-001` was separately adjudicated: it is not named by any Unit 202 obligation, so it correctly remains `standalone` -- not promoted to required merely to remove the entry.

**2. Family/mastery changes**:
- `cap.foundational.mechanics.calculate`'s description corrected to genuinely cover all 5 AC3.4 calculation obligations (work, kinetic energy, gravitational potential energy, **power, efficiency** -- the last two were previously omitted despite CC-09G's own comments already saying this capability represented them).
- `cap.foundational.mass_weight.recognise`'s description strengthened to name W = mg explicitly.
- `cap.foundational.gears.recognise` narrowed to the genuinely REQUIRED "gears" obligation (gear principle + gear-ratio mechanical advantage) only -- direction reversal and the idler gear's role (SUPPORTS-only curriculum mappings, confirmed by direct inspection) were previously named in this REQUIRED capability's own description, silently implying they were mandatory mastery. Pinned by a new regression (`report-coverage-matrix.test.ts`).
- A broad generic "no REQUIRED capability's blueprint evidence may cite a SUPPORTS-only assertion" check was attempted for `validate-pedagogy.ts` and found **not to hold** against the real corpus -- it flagged 41 long-standing, genuinely correct question blueprints that legitimately draw on SUPPORTS-mapped assertions as part of testing an integrated skill (e.g. `series.predict_open_circuit_effect`). Reverted; the actual defect (a capability *description* naming SUPPORTS-only content) is addressed directly at the source and via a targeted regression instead of an over-broad structural check.

**3. Prerequisite corrections**: `EL-CONCEPT-AC-DC-DISTINCTION-001` no longer requires `EL-CONCEPT-SINE-WAVE-001` (backwards for root-cause purposes -- a learner can and normally should understand AC/DC before sinusoidal generation; current/charge context kept). `EL-CONCEPT-EMF-001` no longer requires `EL-CONCEPT-MAGNETIC-FLUX-001` (EMF is a general source concept; flux is prerequisite to *induction* EMF specifically, and `EL-CONCEPT-ELECTROMAGNETIC-INDUCTION-001`/`EL-CONCEPT-AC-GENERATOR-001` already independently declare that dependency). `EL-INSTRUMENT-SELECT-001` gained the missing `EL-INSTRUMENT-MULTIMETER-001` dependency (its own statement explicitly names selecting among all four instruments, including multimeter).

**4. Electron-theory scope corrected**: `EL-CONCEPT-ELECTRON-THEORY-001`/`EL-CONCEPT-CONDUCTOR-001`/`EL-CONCEPT-INSULATOR-001` qualified to the metallic-conductor context (Level 2 electrical-installation materials) rather than presenting free-electron conduction as universal to all electrical conductors -- this same corpus separately, correctly governs electrolytic current as a chemical/ionic effect (`EL-CURRENT-CHEMICAL-EFFECT-001`). No ionic/semiconductor charge-carrier physics introduced. New regression confirms internal consistency.

**5. Ohmmeter correction**: `EL-INSTRUMENT-OHMMETER-001`'s "must be used on a component that is isolated and de-energised" overstated its own OpenStax source, which establishes only that an ohmmeter must never be connected to an *energised* circuit. Corrected to the smallest truthful statement, with isolation named only as a conditional need for measuring an individual component accurately.

**6. Foundational Maths corrections**: `FM-ALG-PROPORTION-DIRECT-001`/`FM-ALG-PROPORTION-INVERSE-001` restated with exact "multiply/divide by k" definitions (the previous "increases in the same ratio" wording was mathematically ambiguous). `FM-NUM-STANDARD-FORM-001` corrected so the coefficient's *magnitude* (not the coefficient itself) satisfies 1-to-10, allowing negative numbers. `FM-ARITH-RECIPROCAL-SUM-001` corrected to state precisely that summing reciprocals gives the reciprocal of the total, never the total itself (a further step, `FM-ARITH-RECIPROCAL-INVERT-001`, is needed).

**7. Mechanical-advantage/pulley correction**: `FP-CONCEPT-MECHANICAL-ADVANTAGE-001`'s circular "changing the relationship between effort and load" restated with its genuine Level-2 meaning (ratio of output/load effect to input/effort effect; force ratio for levers/pulleys, torque ratio for gears). Provenance corrected: the sole-cited lever-specific locator does not itself cover gears/pulleys generally -- added the existing generic simple-machines locator as the genuine `DEFINES` source. `FP-CONCEPT-PULLEY-001` restated to cover direction-change **and/or** mechanical advantage, not direction-change alone.

**8. Diode (NON_MATERIAL) and UK-supply corrections**: `EL-COMPONENT-DIODE-001`'s "junction narrows/widens" corrected to "depletion layer narrows/widens" (matching its own source). `EL-CIRCUIT-AC-SUPPLY-RECOGNITION-001`'s UK 230V/50Hz claim was previously supported only by a generic OpenStax locator -- registered the actual first-party UK regulatory source (The Electricity Safety, Quality and Continuity Regulations 2002, SI 2002/2665, Regulation 27, directly re-inspected) as `DEFINES` evidence. The unsourced "(for example 230 V)" illustrative parenthetical on `EL-CONCEPT-PEAK-VS-RMS-SUPPLY-INTERPRETATION-001` was removed rather than separately sourced (it was never that assertion's own testable claim). Deliberately bounded to this one fact -- no wiring-regulations package added.

**9. Atomicity adjudication (task section 12)**: `FM-NUM-INDICES-LAWS-001`, `FM-GEOM-TRIG-RATIOS-001`, `EL-APPLICATION-TELEPHONE-MASTER-SOCKET-001` and `EL-COMPONENT-TRANSISTOR-001` were each inspected against the "one learner response must not falsely establish mastery of unrelated clauses" principle. **Adjudication: no split, for all four, at this time.** None currently has any question blueprint at all (confirmed by direct inspection) -- the harm the principle guards against (one graded response conflating independent clauses) cannot materialise without a blueprint to do the conflating. Splitting now would be corpus growth with no current functional consumer, exactly the kind of premature restructuring the package's own boundaries exclude ("do not perform unrelated refactoring", "do not explode the corpus into micro-facts"). This adjudication should be revisited by whichever future package first authors a question blueprint against any of these four assertions.

**10. Duplication adjudication (NON_MATERIAL)**: `EL-SERIES-POWER-DISTRIBUTION-001`/`EL-PARALLEL-POWER-DISTRIBUTION-001` substantially duplicate their `...-DOMINANT-RESISTOR-001` siblings' "dissipates the most power" conclusion. Both pairs kept (never merged -- each maps to a genuinely distinct AC: 4.4 vs 4.6), but the duplication is now made explicit and traceable via `derivedFrom`/`LOGICAL_DEFINITIONAL` rather than left as an unexplained independent restatement.

**11. ## CC-10 — Complete Unit 202 Course Production Build (implementation-complete for its authored scope)

A deliberate phase change from Phase-1's "audit every small step" cadence to "build big, validate, audit at gates": the first package to turn the governed CC-04/CC-05A knowledge/pedagogy corpus into substantial learner-facing lesson content at scale, rather than correcting it. The 258-assertion knowledge corpus was treated as **frozen** for ordinary authoring (task section 22) -- `cc04-unit202-electrical-science.ts` was not touched by this package at all; every change lives in the pedagogy/presentation/lesson/release layers.

**1. Lessons authored (11 new)**: `lesson.electrical.core-quantities`, `si-units`, `instrumentation` (completing LO2's three assessable ACs, zero new blueprints needed), and `charge-and-current`, `conductors-and-insulators`, `thermal-and-chemical-effects`, `resistivity`, `power`, `energy-and-efficiency`, `fault-conditions-protection`, `series-vs-parallel-comparison` (eight LO4 families, the last a deliberate capstone requiring both series and parallel circuits as prerequisites). Each lesson follows the full `LessonPlan` contract: orientation, concept teaching, guided/independent/transfer practice, retrieval, exit completion, misconception-targeted diagnostics where the family has a governed misconception, and worked examples where a blueprint existed.

**2. Release**: `release.unit202.v3` minted (task section 30 -- CC-08A's release-immutability rule forbids growing `release.unit202.v2`'s membership, so a new release was the only compliant path for a genuinely complete, validated content batch). v3 carries all 15 lessons: the 11 new ones plus release-scoped re-entries of the 4 pre-existing `release.unit202.v2` lessons (Ohm's Law, formula rearrangement, series/parallel resistors), reusing those lesson objects unmutated. `MOBILE_BUNDLED_RELEASE_ID` switched from v2 to v3 -- a deliberate publication decision, since these are complete, fully-validated lessons, not intermediate authoring. `release.unit202.v1`/`v2`'s own membership arrays remain byte-identical, untouched.

**3. Presentation-layer gap closed (mechanically discovered, not anticipated)**: bundling 39 previously-unbundled question blueprints for the first time surfaced that `presentation.promptLines` -- required for a blueprint to render on mobile, but never enforced by `engine:prove:check`/`content:pedagogy:check` -- was missing on 68 of the corpus's 92 blueprints (a pre-existing gap, not introduced here). Authored `promptLines` for all 39 blueprints this package's lessons actually use. Two blueprints (`charge.calculate`, `power.calculate_total`) generate a variable/branching parameter shape that a single static template cannot safely reference (an undefined placeholder throws, by `presentation.ts`'s own deliberate design) -- fixed with small, additive engine changes (`given_summary`, `component_count`/`summary`) that compute a safe always-present summary string, mirroring the existing `series.solve_missing_component` precedent of templating only always-present aggregate values. Seven blueprints (`resistivity.recognise`, `resistivity.compare_materials`, `thermal_chemical.recognise_effect/recognise_application`, `conductors.classify_material`, `instrumentation.recognise_purpose`, `fault.recognise_condition`) were found to generate a parameter *identical to their own correct answer* -- templating it directly would have handed the learner the answer. Each was fixed with a small additive executor change (a governed-statement-derived clue/label parameter with the answer word withheld, or a genuine numeric-comparison redesign for `compare_materials`) rather than templating the leaking value. All fixes are presentation-layer only (`packages/calculation-engine/src/families/*.ts`, `cc05a-pedagogy-unit202.ts`'s `presentation` field) -- no assertion, capability, or blueprint answer/grading logic changed. `engine:prove:check` (92/92 supported, all families PASS, including full generate+grade proving) and `engine:dimensions:check` confirm no behavioural regression.

**4. Scope decision (explicit, not silent)**: LO1/LO2/LO4 plus the series-vs-parallel capstone are now learner-ready; **LO5 (magnetism/electromagnetism, EMF/generation, AC-DC waveforms, AC reactive quantities) and LO6 (electronic components) were deliberately deferred**, authoring zero new lessons there, per the task's own explicit permission to prioritise coherent completed Learning Outcomes over scattered partial coverage when the full unit cannot fit in one package. This was a reasoned scope choice, not a discovered blocker -- both LOs' knowledge/pedagogy corpus (assertions, capabilities, and for LO5, question blueprints) already exist and are untouched; only lesson authoring remains.

**5. Runtime gaps identified, deliberately not fixed this package** (task section 16's "author missing content, fix only bounded/safe runtime gaps" -- these three are genuine product/UX features, not bounded bugs): (a) `LessonStepView.tsx` never renders `representation.diagramBlueprintId` -- a pre-existing gap (the original `lesson-resistors-series.ts` already has it) now also inherited by `lesson-instrumentation.ts` and `lesson-series-vs-parallel-comparison.ts`, documented in both files' headers; (b) no learner-facing "browse Unit 202 lessons" screen exists yet -- `apps/mobile/src/app/(app)/learn/index.tsx` only ever surfaces one diagnostic-chosen activity, so Learning Mode's "pick a lesson directly" requirement (task section 15) is content-ready but not yet reachable through the current navigation; (c) `FeedbackPanel.tsx` has one feedback depth, not the "MAKE ME PASS" vs "MAKE ME UNDERSTAND" toggle (task section 7) -- content is authored so this can be added later without a knowledge-model rewrite, but the toggle itself does not exist yet.

**6. Course-production manifest (task section 19)**: no new parallel report was built. `report-coverage-matrix.ts` (`coverage:matrix:check`) already computes, per AC, whether a lesson/masteryGate/questionBlueprint exists -- entirely dynamically from `lessons.ts`/`cc05a-pedagogy-unit202.ts` -- and already reflects all 11 new lessons with zero code change; combined with `validate-lesson-plan.ts` (lesson/step governance) and `validate-pedagogy.ts` (blueprint/capability/mastery coverage), these three existing, extended-for-free tools jointly satisfy the manifest requirement. Per-LO exam-readiness as mechanically reported: LO1 READY, LO2 READY, LO3 NOT READY (2/4 AC lesson-covered, pre-existing, untouched by this package), LO4 READY (8/8 AC), LO5 NOT READY (0/5 AC, deferred per §4), LO6 NOT READY (0/2 AC, deferred, pre-existing gap).

**7. Counts**: lessons 5 → **20** (11 new + 4 v3-scoped re-entries of pre-existing lessons; `release.unit202.v3` carries 15 distinct lessons). Lesson steps: ~60 → **217** across all 20 lesson entries. `release.unit202.v3` bundles **63** question blueprints (up from v2's 23) -- all with governed `presentation.promptLines`. Question blueprints, capabilities, assertions, formula families: unchanged (92/all/258/12) -- this package authored presentation and lesson content against the existing corpus, never new knowledge. Worked-example steps used: 6 (`worked.resistivity.calculate_resistance`, `worked.power.calculate_from_vi`/`calculate_from_ir`, `worked.energy.calculate_energy`, `worked.efficiency.calculate`; `worked.charge.calculate_current` exists but was not used, a minor missed opportunity).

**8. Validation**: full workspace `typecheck` and `lint` clean. Root Vitest **693/693**. `coverage:matrix:check` (0 structural defects, 23/23 AC referentially and semantically covered, release confidence **GOOD** unchanged). `content:pedagogy:check` (92 blueprints, 258 assertions, all four gates zero). `engine:prove:check` (92/92 supported, all families PASS). `engine:dimensions:check` (PASS). `lesson:validate:check` (20 lessons, 217 steps, all 19 governance gates zero). `content:mobile:check` regenerates cleanly against the working tree (byte-identical currency confirmed by `check-mobile-projection.test.ts`'s own regeneration-equality test); its `git diff`-based check will show the expected new-content diff until this package's changes are committed.

**9. Files changed**: 11 new `scripts/content/data/lesson-*.ts` files; `scripts/content/data/lessons.ts`, `content-releases.ts`, `cc05a-pedagogy-unit202.ts` (presentation only); 7 `packages/calculation-engine/src/families/*.ts` executors (additive safe-parameter fixes); `scripts/content/check-mobile-projection.test.ts`, `validate-lesson-plan.test.ts`, `packages/calculation-engine/src/families/new-families.test.ts` (expectation updates for the new counts/parameter shapes); the regenerated `apps/mobile/src/lib/lesson-content/generated/mobile-content-projection.ts`; this file. `cc04-unit202-electrical-science.ts` (the knowledge-graph corpus) was **not modified** -- the knowledge-freeze constraint was honoured with zero exceptions invoked.

**10. Not implemented / explicitly deferred (by design)**: LO5 and LO6 lesson authoring (§4); the three runtime gaps in §5; the CC-09F v1.2 sample-comparison blocker (unchanged, unaddressed); a mixed-practice/mock-exam generator (architecture not newly designed this package -- existing blueprint/family structure already supports building one, no ambiguity introduced).

**Next package**: author LO5 and LO6 lesson content to complete the Unit 202 learner journey (mirroring this package's pattern); then implement the three deferred runtime gaps (diagram rendering, lesson-browse screen, feedback-depth toggle) as their own product-facing package(s).

## CC-11 — Complete Unit 202 Course + Instructional Visuals (implementation-complete)

Two inseparable objectives, both closed: (A) complete the remaining Unit 202 learner-facing course content (LO3 remainder, LO5, LO6), and (B) make instructional visuals actually render in the real Lesson Player, with technical/pedagogical correctness reviewed. Baseline: `main` @ `6ccd7a1` (clean, confirmed before editing). Build-big cadence continued from CC-10: five parallel workstreams (three content-authoring, one visual-inventory audit, one integration/runtime-fix agent), reconciled and integrated by the main agent, then globally validated.

**1. Parallel workstreams.** Workstream A (LO3 remainder), B (LO5), and C (LO6) each worked in the same working directory concurrently, restricted to non-overlapping new files plus a written integration spec for the two genuinely shared files (`cc05a-pedagogy-unit202.ts`, `packages/calculation-engine/src/engine.ts`) rather than editing them directly — git-worktree isolation was attempted first but is unusable in this environment (pre-existing long filenames under `reports/instructional-visuals/renders/` exceed the Windows path limit the moment a worktree checks out `HEAD`, a pre-existing repository condition unrelated to this package). Workstream D (visual inventory) ran read-only, in parallel. The main agent applied every integration spec, wired the shared registries, fixed cross-cutting defects, and ran every validation gate.

**2. LO3 remainder (Workstream A).** AC3.1 (mass/weight) and AC3.2 (levers/gears/pulleys) had zero lesson or question-blueprint coverage at all. AC3.3/AC3.4 (force/work/energy/power/efficiency) showed as nominally covered in CC-10's own coverage report, but this was a **false-green artefact**: the only lesson touching those ACs' capabilities was `lesson-electrical-energy-efficiency.ts`, via one shared capability (`EL-CONCEPT-ELECTRICAL-EFFICIENCY-001`) — the actual general-mechanics content was never taught anywhere. Closing this required a deliberate, explicit pedagogy-layer decision: reclassifying three Foundational Physics families (`foundational.mass_weight`, `foundational.levers_mechanical_advantage`, `foundational.mechanics_work_energy_power`) from `teaching_only` to `assessable` — "reusable horizontal Foundational Physics knowledge" does not make curriculum-required Unit 202 knowledge optional to assess. Added: 1 new capability (`cap.foundational.levers.calculate`, closing AC3.2's own governed lever-calculation obligation), 6 new `FormulaFamily` objects (lever balance, work, kinetic energy, potential energy, mechanics power, mechanics efficiency — all routed through the pre-existing generic structured-expression evaluator, zero engine-core changes needed), 6 worked examples, 11 new question blueprints, a new engine executor file (`foundational-mechanics.ts`), and 3 new lessons (`lesson-mass-and-weight.ts`, `lesson-simple-machines.ts`, `lesson-mechanics-force-work-energy-power.ts`). `cc04-unit202-electrical-science.ts` untouched.

**3. LO5 (Workstream B).** All 5 ACs had zero lesson coverage but the full knowledge/pedagogy corpus (16 question blueprints across `electrical.magnetism_and_electromagnetism`, `electrical.emf_and_generation`, `electrical.ac_dc_waveforms`) already existed — pure lesson-authoring, no new blueprints. Split into 4 lessons rather than the suggested 3 (`lesson-magnetism-fundamentals.ts`, `lesson-magnetic-effects-of-current.ts`, `lesson-ac-generation-principles.ts`, `lesson-ac-dc-and-sine-wave-quantities.ts`) after determining the suggested single AC5.4+AC5.5 lesson would need 9 target capabilities in one lesson, well past the ~13-step/6-capability precedent. Two real, honestly-flagged knowledge gaps found (not silently worked around): AC5.1's own core "attraction/repulsion" obligation (`EL-CONCEPT-MAGNETISM-001`) has no dedicated question blueprint anywhere in the corpus (taught, not directly assessed); AC5.3's two calculation obligations (F = BIl, e = Blv — Fleming's left/right-hand rules) have no formula family, worked example, blueprint, or capability anywhere in the corpus at all (taught conceptually, cannot be practised numerically). Both are knowledge-layer gaps, out of a lesson-authoring workstream's scope, and are the most material remaining content gap this package did not close — see §12 below.

**4. LO6 (Workstream C).** AC6.1/AC6.2 had genuinely zero pedagogy-layer authoring — not even blueprints — despite real governed knowledge existing since CC-09B (11 electronic components: capacitors, rectifiers, diodes, Zener/LED/photodiode, thermistor, DIAC/TRIAC/thyristor/transistor, inverter). Reclassified `electrical.electronic_components` from `teaching_only` to `assessable`; authored 6 new categorical (no-calculation, matching the task brief's explicit "do not turn this into semiconductor engineering") question blueprints, a new engine executor file (`electronic-components.ts`), and 2 new lessons (`lesson-electronic-components-passive.ts`, `lesson-electronic-components-switching-control.ts`). Atomicity (task §21, CC-09I precedent) was deliberately honoured: compound governed assertions covering two independent facts (`EL-APPLICATION-SECURITY-ALARM-TRANSISTOR-THYRISTOR-001`, `EL-APPLICATION-TELEPHONE-MASTER-SOCKET-001`) were split into separate scenario keys so no single correct answer falsely certifies two independent claims.

**5. The Lesson Player diagram runtime gap — fixed generically (task §7).** CC-10 identified but did not fix: `LessonStepView.tsx` never rendered `representation.diagramBlueprintId`, even though `ContentLookup` never carried `diagramBlueprints` to resolve against. Fixed as a single shared registry (`apps/mobile/src/components/diagrams/DiagramRenderer.tsx`) — `DiagramBlueprint.id → component`, used by both the Lesson Player and (via a newly-shared `resolveDiagramComponent` primitive) the pre-existing CC-05D render-capture/QA-catalogue pipeline, which previously hand-rolled its own second dispatch switch statement (exactly the anti-pattern task §7 warns against) and has now been refactored onto the same registry. `resolve-lesson-step.ts`'s `ContentLookup`/`RenderableLessonStep` gained `diagramBlueprints`/`diagram`; `local-content-registry.ts` threads the projection's real diagram blueprints through. Unregistered blueprint ids throw `UnsupportedDiagramBlueprintError` (fail loudly, never silently disappear) rather than rendering nothing. **Reveal timing**: for the two magnetism diagrams with an assessed directional answer, the reveal is now withheld until feedback (`evaluation && revealCorrectAnswer`), mirroring exactly how the correct-answer text itself is already gated (CC-06D Correction G) — a pure teaching step (no generated question instance) renders the diagram via a new generic `buildTeachingDiagramInstance(blueprint)` helper (first-declared enum value, `true` for booleans, `min` for number ranges) with no reveal, which is safe by construction and more consistent with the governed DO→RESPOND→FEEDBACK→NEXT step contract than the older `learn/[family]/` screens' "always reveal in teaching mode" pattern Workstream D's audit found.

**6. Renderer coverage: 4/7 → 7/7.** Built the three renderers CC-05D tracked as a known, non-fatal gap and left unbuilt: `SeriesParallelMixedCircuitDiagram.tsx` (both `series_of_parallel`/`parallel_of_series` topologies, rectilinear wiring only, symbolic R{n} labels), `WaveformSineDiagram.tsx` (zero-axis-referenced peak/RMS/period reference lines; RMS line drawn at exactly `1/sqrt(2)` of the peak's height, never coincident with peak or zero; no "average" line ever drawn, since a symmetric full-cycle AC average is genuinely zero), `InstrumentConnectionDiagram.tsx` (voltmeter always drawn as an unbroken-main-path side branch; ammeter always spliced into the current path; ohmmeter always isolated with no source in a closed loop regardless of the requested `connection_style`, plus an explicit on-diagram "(standard connection)"/"(NOT the standard connection)" caption so no combination is ever silently endorsed as correct). Workstream D's audit found these were the two most urgent (already referenced by real, previously-shipped `release.unit202.v3` lesson content that the diagram runtime gap had been silently swallowing) rather than merely theoretical.

**7. Visual defects found and corrected (task §6/§10, Workstream D's audit).** (a) `MagneticForceDiagram.tsx`/`RightHandGripRuleDiagram.tsx` never disambiguated "current" as *conventional* current, risking reinforcement of the real, governed, already-targeted `MIS-EL-ELECTRON-CURRENT-DIRECTION-CONFUSION-001` misconception — fixed in both accessibility text and on-canvas captions. (b) `ParallelCircuitDiagram.tsx`'s accessibility label never mentioned its own branch current-arrows even when shown, an accessibility-parity gap against its sibling `SeriesCircuitDiagram.tsx` — fixed. (c) The capture-renders second-switch-statement duplication (§5 above). All existing rendered SVG evidence for the 4 pre-CC-11 diagrams was regenerated and now reflects the corrected wording.

**8. Visual semantic contracts: 4/7 → 7/7.** New `VisualSemanticContract` entries for all 3 newly-rendered blueprints (`visual-contract.series-parallel-mixed-topology`, `visual-contract.ac-waveform-sine`, `visual-contract.instrument-measurement-connection`), each with explicit `mustShow`/`mustNotShow`/`invariantExpectations`/`answerDisclosure` and an honestly-recorded `knownAmbiguity` where a genuine open design question exists (e.g. `instrument.measurement_connection`'s own always-shown correctness caption would leak the answer if that diagram is ever wired into `instrumentation.recognise_connection`'s own assessment presentation — flagged for the next package, not silently resolved). Canonical audit variants: 18 → 31 (2 mixed-topology + 6 waveform + 5 instrument-connection, each a pedagogically distinct parameter combination, never arbitrary permutation). The mechanical gate (`npm run visuals:check:strict`) and the full pipeline (`npm run visuals:all`) both pass clean; the HTML/JSON catalogue (`reports/instructional-visuals/index.html`) regenerated with all 31 variant cards.

**9. Answer-leakage fixes surfaced by bundling LO5 for the first time (same failure class CC-10 found and fixed 7 times for its own 39 blueprints).** Bundling `magnetism`/`emf`/`waveform` question blueprints into a real content release for the first time required authoring their `presentation.promptLines` (a pre-existing gap: CC-10 only authored promptLines for blueprints its own lessons used) — and doing so surfaced that several generated parameters were themselves the blueprint's own correct answer. Fixed with the same additive, governed-statement-derived clue-parameter pattern CC-10 established (never templating the leaking value directly): `magnetism.recognise_concept`/`compare_permanent_electromagnet`/`compare_motor_generator` and `waveform.recognise_ac_dc` each gained a small clue lookup table; `emf.calculate_flux_change`/`waveform.calculate_rms_from_peak`/`waveform.calculate_frequency_from_period` each gained an engine-computed `given_summary` string for their branching known-variable shape (identical fix pattern to CC-10's `charge.calculate`/`power.calculate_total`). 16 blueprints total gained `presentation.promptLines`. All changes are additive to existing executors — no blueprint answer/grading/generation logic changed.

**10. Content release.** `release.unit202.v3`'s own membership is left exactly as CC-10 declared it (immutable, per this file's own documented rule). A new release, `release.unit202.v4`, carries all 24 lessons: the 15 pre-existing v3 lessons (re-addressed via the same release-scoped-membership pattern v2/v3 themselves established, never mutated) plus 9 genuinely new LO3/LO5/LO6 lessons. `MOBILE_BUNDLED_RELEASE_ID` switched from v3 to v4. The generated mobile projection now bundles all 7 diagram blueprints (previously fewer were ever reachable, since no lesson referenced the 3 unrendered ones) and 96 question blueprints (up from 63).

**11. Validation — full battery, all green.** `npm run typecheck` (all workspaces + all scripts tsconfigs): clean. `npm run lint`: clean (one real `react-hooks/static-components` finding in `DiagramRenderer.tsx`, fixed by invoking the resolved component as a plain function rather than in JSX position, since every registry entry is a stateless presentational component with nothing to lose across re-renders). Root `npx vitest run`: **749/749**. `apps/mobile` `npx jest`: **265/265** (6 pre-existing failures found and fixed along the way — stale `release.unit202.v2` hardcoded expectations across 3 test files, dating from CC-10's own v2→v3 bundled-release switch and never updated; confirmed pre-existing via a stash-isolated baseline check against `main` before this package's own edits, not introduced by this package). `npm run content:pedagogy:check`: 258 assertions, 109 question blueprints, all four coverage gates zero. `npm run engine:prove:check`: 109/109 supported, all 22 families PASS (up from 20). `npm run engine:dimensions:check`: PASS (11 blueprints/13 dimension entries, up from 10/12). `npm run lesson:validate:check`: 44 lessons, 478 steps, all 19 governance gates zero (including a real, mechanically-caught defect this package introduced and fixed: 6 new concept-teaching steps initially had neither `capabilityIds` nor a `representation` reference, tripping the "teaching step with no governed reference" gate — fixed by adding the relevant capability id to each, matching the established `lesson-conductors-and-insulators.ts` precedent for concept-only, no-formula teaching steps). `npm run coverage:matrix:check`: **0 structural defects, LO1–LO6 all READY, 23/23 ACs referentially ASSESSABLE and semantically COMPLETE_PENDING_VERIFICATION, 58/58 Range items covered, release confidence GOOD**. `npm run content:mobile:check`/`visuals:check:strict`/`visuals:all`: clean (the mobile-projection check's own git-diff-based staleness comparison will show the expected new-content diff until this package's changes are committed, exactly as CC-10's own report noted for the same reason).

**12. Not implemented / explicitly deferred (by design, all honestly flagged, none hidden by a green top-line metric).** LO5's two knowledge-layer gaps (§3): AC5.1's missing dedicated attraction/repulsion blueprint; AC5.3's missing Fleming's-law (F=BIl, e=Blv) formula family/blueprint — both require new governed knowledge-corpus authoring, out of this package's pedagogy-layer scope, and are the most material remaining content gap. Real AI/vision semantic visual review: still not run (no credential available in this environment — the existing mock-provider pipeline reviewed all 31 variants as **SIMULATED, not real**, unambiguously labelled as such per CC-05D's own truthfulness-correction precedent). `instrument.measurement_connection`'s always-shown correctness caption vs. a hypothetical future assessment-mode pairing with `instrumentation.recognise_connection` (§8's `knownAmbiguity`) — an open design question, not silently resolved. Workstream D's recommended LO3/LO6 diagram candidates (lever-class, gear-pair, pulley-arrangement, diode-symbol, rectifier-circuit) were deliberately NOT built this package — LO3/LO6 content instead uses formula/concept-card representation, which the task brief explicitly permits ("not every concept should be an illustration") and which avoided a five-new-diagram-blueprint scope expansion inside an already-large package; each remains a well-scoped candidate for a future visual-polish package. ## CC-11.1 — Unit 202 Content & Visual Gate Closeout (implementation-complete)

Closes exactly the material gap CC-11 §12 flagged and left open, plus prepares real pixel-level visual review — nothing else. Baseline: `main` @ `46d0263`.

**1. The false-green problem, closed generically.** CC-11's own coverage report could say "23/23 ACs LEARNER-READY" while AC5.1's core attraction/repulsion obligation was taught but never assessed, because the gate only checked "does this AC have *some* blueprint," never "does *every* required obligation have an evidence route." Fixed with a new, non-hardcoded dimension in `report-coverage-matrix.ts`: **OBLIGATION-LEVEL LEARNER READINESS**, computed generically from `KnowledgeObligation.basis` (only `EXPLICIT`/`OFFICIAL_TEACHING_INTERPRETATION`/`OFFICIAL_ASSESSMENT_EVIDENCE` obligations require their own evidence route — `RANGE`/`NECESSARY_PREREQUISITE` never do) cross-referenced against real `QuestionBlueprint.evidence.assertionIdentifiers` citations, never a per-AC or per-unit hardcoded list. A small, explicit, always-reported `TRACKED_OBLIGATION_EVIDENCE_EXCEPTIONS` allowlist (one entry: `AC4.1:electron-theory-of-current`, a genuine but LO4/out-of-scope gap) mirrors the CC-05D `ungovernedNonPilotBlueprints` pattern. `isReportClean()` now also gates on zero *untracked* gaps — this specific false-green class cannot recur. A regression test (`buildReport({ pedagogy: tamperedPedagogy })`, injecting the exact CC-11 baseline defect) proves the gate actually fires, not just that real content happens to pass.

## CC-11.2 — Final Unit 202 Content-Gate Closeout (implementation-complete)

Closes the one remaining tracked exception CC-11.1 left open. Baseline: `main` @ `9012b40`.

**1. The gap.** AC4.1's own "electron-theory-of-current" obligation (`EL-CONCEPT-ELECTRON-THEORY-001`, basis EXPLICIT) is taught in `lesson.electrical.conductors-and-insulators` but was never directly assessed — the family's two pre-existing blueprints (`conductors.classify_material`, `conductors.recognise_breakdown`) test conductor/insulator classification and insulation breakdown, neither of which exercises the electron-theory proposition itself.

**2. Closed with the minimum path.** New capability `cap.conductors.recognise_electron_theory` on the existing `electrical.conductors_and_insulators` family; one new deterministic question blueprint, `conductors.recognise_electron_theory` (multiple-choice, "what actually moves to create current in a metallic conductor" — `free_electrons` vs. `protons`); one new engine executor (`recogniseElectronTheory` in the existing `thermal-and-conductors.ts` file, no new engine file). The distractor is `protons`, not an ionic/semiconductor charge carrier — both the correct answer and the wrong one draw only on content this family's own prerequisite already teaches (`EL-CONCEPT-ATOMIC-CHARGE-STRUCTURE-001`: protons fixed in the nucleus, only electrons free to move), so no new teaching content was introduced to support the question, matching the task brief's explicit "do not create unnecessary semiconductor/ionic-conduction teaching."

**3. Lesson/mastery integration.** One new `guided_interaction` step, `guided_recognise_electron_theory`, added to `lesson.electrical.conductors-and-insulators` immediately after the existing `concept_electron_theory` step (before `concept_conductor_insulator`) — the same "practice immediately after the concept that teaches it" placement CC-11.1 established. `targetCapabilityIds`, `completionCriteria.requiredStepIds`/`requiredCapabilityEvidence` updated; the recap step's `reinforces` extended to include the electron-theory assertion it was already summarising in prose but not citing.

**4. Exception governance correction (task §3).** CC-11.1's own tracked-exception reasoning for this gap — "genuinely uncovered, but out of THIS package's task-brief scope" — was wrong and is documented as such at the mechanism itself (`TRACKED_OBLIGATION_EVIDENCE_EXCEPTIONS`'s own doc comment in `report-coverage-matrix.ts`): which internal package happened to schedule a fix is never, on its own, a valid reason to defer a required-obligation evidence gap for a real qualification. The allowlist mechanism itself is unchanged and still real (a genuinely justified deferred exception — e.g. content this repository does not yet govern at all, or a deliberate product scoping decision — still has somewhere to go), it is simply **empty** as of this package: `TRACKED_OBLIGATION_EVIDENCE_EXCEPTIONS = {}`.

**5. Release.** `release.unit202.v5`'s own declared membership is left byte-for-byte immutable. `lesson.electrical.conductors-and-insulators` is single-sourced and its content genuinely changed, so — exactly the same pattern v5 was itself created by — a new release, `release.unit202.v6`, carries the correction; `MOBILE_BUNDLED_RELEASE_ID` moved v5 → v6. Because this one lesson was, until now, resolved identically (same live object) by v3 (natively), v4 and v5 (both via release-scoped spread overrides), correcting it in place would have retroactively mutated three already-immutable releases at once. Fixed by capturing the lesson's exact pre-CC-11.2 step content (sourced via `git show 9012b40`) into a new frozen file, `lesson-conductors-and-insulators-v3-v5-historical-snapshot.ts`, referenced from v3/v4/v5's own membership blocks in `lessons.ts` in place of the (now v6-tagged) live object — restoring full v3/v4/v5 resolvability without mutating any of them, mirroring CC-11.1's own `lesson-lo5-v4-historical-snapshot.ts` precedent exactly, just one release-generation deeper.

## CC-11.3 — Full Unit 202 Instructional Visual Coverage + Correction Gate (implementation-complete)

Closes the whole-course instructional-visual layer: every lesson/capability that should carry imagery now has one, and every existing visual (old and new) was pixel-reviewed and corrected. Baseline: `main` @ `1c31aae`. Content/knowledge gate stayed closed throughout — no factual audit reopened, no new question banks, no unrelated lesson rewrites; the one exception (§11 below) was a genuine visual-implementation-exposed defect, reported as such.

**1. Discovery and matrix.** 8 parallel discovery workstreams (LO1 maths, LO2 measurement, LO3 mechanics, LO4 DC electrical, LO5 magnetism/AC, LO6 electronics, existing-defect review, reusable infrastructure) audited all 24 current lessons and classified visual need as REQUIRED/USEFUL/NOT_NEEDED from lesson/capability/objective → need. Recorded as a discrete artefact this time: `reports/instructional-visuals/visual-needs-matrix.md` — 12 REQUIRED (all satisfied), 9 NOT_NEEDED, 3 USEFUL-deferred (conductors-and-insulators, fault-conditions-protection, thermal-and-chemical-effects — factual-recall objectives a diagram would restate, not clarify), 1 genuine content-freeze contradiction recorded honestly (LO1 trigonometry has no lesson to attach a visual to, and building one would violate the explicit freeze).

**2. Nine new diagram families built**, each with a native SVG renderer, `VisualSemanticContract`, and canonical-variant coverage: `mechanical.lever_arrangement` (class I/II/III), `mechanical.gear_mesh` (driver/driven ratio), `mechanical.pulley_arrangement` (fixed vs movable), `mechanical.resistivity_dimensions` (length/area comparison), `magnetic.pole_interaction` (like/unlike poles), `magnetic.flux_field_lines`, `emf.motional_emf_geometry` (mutually-perpendicular B/l/v), `generator.rotating_loop` (single-loop AC generator), `electronics.component_symbol_card` (13-symbol governed set, BS EN 60617/IEC 60617). New `symbolStandard: "UK_IEC"` field on `VisualSemanticContract`; new `diagramParameters` field on lesson steps so one shared multi-value blueprint (e.g. the symbol card) can be parameterised per teaching step without a generated question driving it.

**3. Five real defects found and fixed during implementation** (not scope creep — each was a genuine correctness/contract problem the visual-completeness work exposed): (a) `instrument.measurement_connection`'s teaching-error variants were tagged for both teaching AND assessment with a leaking caption — refactored with a `mode` prop so deliberate errors stay legitimate teaching comparisons that never leak into assessment; (b) the same contract's absolute `mustNotShow` contradicted its own deliberate-error variants — resolved by governing the exception explicitly rather than normalising it away; (c) the ohmmeter connection taught a false universal isolation requirement — corrected to "de-energised circuit; individual-component isolation MAY be required where parallel paths affect the reading," and its `connection_style` metadata fixed from misleading `series` to a real new `isolated` value; (d) the right-hand-grip-rule thumb pointed diagonally and didn't actually communicate current direction — replaced with a tapered wedge plus an into/out-of-page glyph at the tip; (e) `lesson.emf.ac-generation-principles` pointed 2 steps at the wrong blueprint (the static motor-force diagram, not a rotating loop) — fixed to `generator.rotating_loop`, its actual governed visual.

**4. Real pixel-level review caught a sixth defect, this time in the review tooling itself.** Reading the regenerated PDF review package page-by-page (task brief §30's mandatory step — Claude may run mechanical checks but must never self-approve imagery) surfaced near-invisible text and strokes across every diagram, old and new: labels, gear teeth, capacitor symbols all washed out to the point of unreadability. Root cause was not the diagrams — every one of them correctly uses the app's real governed dark theme (`apps/mobile/src/lib/tokens.ts`: background `#0B0D12`, text `#F2F4F8`) — but `generate-review-package.ts`'s phone-mockup CSS rendered them inside a **white** `.phone-screen`, a pre-existing mismatch (present since CC-11.1, affecting all 66 variants, not something this session introduced) that made near-white text and strokes nearly invisible against a background the app never actually uses. Fixed by changing the mockup to the app's real `#0B0D12` background; every previously-illegible page (gear teeth, lever labels, pole-interaction boxes, component-symbol icons, right-hand-grip-rule fingers/thumb) is now clearly readable, confirmed by re-reading the same sample pages after the fix.

**5. New generic visual-completeness gate (§27).** `check-visual-governance.ts` proves every *blueprint that has a renderer* also has a contract/variants/no-leakage — but never looks at lesson content, so it could go green even if a lesson's declared visual were dangling. New `scripts/visual-governance/check-visual-completeness.ts` (`npm run visuals:completeness:check`, wired into `visuals:all`) walks every current lesson step's `representation.diagramBlueprintId` and mechanically verifies the governed asset exists, a renderer exists, a contract exists, and (where the blueprint is a `component_symbol`) an approved UK/IEC convention is declared. Result: **49/49 REQUIRED-visual declarations across 24 lessons, 0 gaps in all 4 dimensions.** (One real bug surfaced and fixed while building this: a naive "highest lesson `version` wins" dedup silently selected frozen historical snapshots over live content, because `version` tracks structural schema revisions, not release history, and both stayed at `1`. Fixed to "last array occurrence wins," matching the corpus's actual chronological-append structure.)

**6. Release.** Same immutability pattern as CC-11.1/CC-11.2, at larger scale: 7 lessons changed (`lesson-simple-machines`, `lesson-resistivity`, `lesson-magnetism-fundamentals`, `lesson-magnetic-effects-of-current`, `lesson-ac-generation-principles`, `lesson-electronic-components-passive`, `lesson-electronic-components-switching-control`), spanning v3/v4/v5/v6 native releases — each got a frozen historical snapshot (`lesson-cc11-3-historical-snapshot.ts`, one export per lesson) preserving every past release it used to resolve identically, before retagging the live object to `release.unit202.v7`. `MOBILE_BUNDLED_RELEASE_ID` moved v6 → v7. v6 and earlier remain byte-for-byte resolvable and untouched.

**7. Validation — full battery, all green.** `npm run typecheck`: clean. `npm run lint`: clean (0 errors; 6 pre-existing unrelated warnings). `npm run visuals:check:strict`: PASS, 16 blueprints/16 renderers/16 contracts, 66 canonical variants, 0 answer-leakage failures. `npm run visuals:completeness:check`: PASS, per §5. `npm run lesson:validate:check`: PASS, 116 lessons, all 19 governance gates zero. `npm run coverage:matrix:check`: **6/6 LOs, 23/23 ACs learner-ready, 0 untracked gaps, 0 tracked exceptions** — unchanged from CC-11.2, as expected (no obligation-evidence work in scope). `npm run content:pedagogy:check`: 258 assertions, 113 question blueprints, all coverage gates zero, 16 diagram blueprints, 0 unresolved required-diagram references. `npm run engine:prove:check`: 113/113 (fixed one Representation-contract failure this session — `recogniseAttractionRepulsion` declared a required diagram but never attached one; added `poleDiagram()`/`requireDiagramBlueprint` wiring). `npm run engine:dimensions:check`: PASS. Root `npm run test:unit`: **764/764** (753 baseline + 11 new for `check-visual-completeness.test.ts`). `apps/mobile` `npm run test`: **331/331**, 62 suites (unchanged — no diagram-count or bundled-blueprint regression). `npm run content:mobile:generate`: regenerated clean, release.unit202.v7, 100 bundled question blueprints, 16 diagram blueprints.

**8. Counts.** Diagram blueprints: 7 → **16**. Diagram renderers: 7 → **16**. Visual semantic contracts: 7 → **16**. Canonical variants: 31 → **66**. Lessons (all releases): 92 → **116** (24 new v7 members: 17 unchanged re-addressed + 7 corrected). Lesson-plan steps (all releases): 1013 → **1,281**. Question blueprints: 113 → 113 (unchanged — this package is visual, not assessment-content, scope). Root Vitest: 753 → **764**. Mobile Jest: 331/331 unchanged. Content release: v6 → **v7**. UK/IEC symbol-convention coverage: **1/1** contracts requiring it declare it (`electronics.component_symbol_card`); 0 unapproved/unstated conventions anywhere in the corpus.

**9. Real pixel-level review status (task brief §30 — Claude does not self-approve imagery).** MECHANICAL VISUAL GOVERNANCE: **PASS**. VISUAL COMPLETENESS: **PASS** (49/49 required declarations satisfied). RENDERER COVERAGE: **16/16**. SEMANTIC-CONTRACT COVERAGE: **16/16**. UK/IEC SYMBOL-CONVENTION COVERAGE: **1/1** applicable. MOCK/SIMULATED SEMANTIC PIPELINE: informational only, not a substitute for human review. Claude read the full 67-page regenerated `unit202-instructional-visuals-review.pdf` sample-by-sample this session (pages spanning every new family plus 3 pre-existing families) and found/fixed the phone-mockup contrast defect in §4 — that is genuine defect-finding, not approval. **REAL PIXEL-LEVEL PRODUCT OWNER/PROJECT ARCHITECT REVIEW: PENDING** — still requires a human (or vision-capable reviewer with authority) to open `reports/instructional-visuals/review-package/unit202-instructional-visuals-review.pdf` and sign off.

**10. Files changed.** 9 new diagram components + tests (`apps/mobile/src/components/diagrams/{Lever,Gear,Pulley,ResistivityDimensions,MagneticPole,MagneticFlux,MotionalEmf,ACGenerator}Diagram.tsx`, `ComponentSymbolCard.tsx`/`ComponentSymbols.tsx`); schema additions (`pedagogy.ts` diagram types, `visual-governance.ts` `symbolStandard`, `lesson-plan.ts` `diagramParameters`); `DiagramRenderer.tsx`/`LessonStepView.tsx` registry + overrides wiring; 5 defect fixes across `InstrumentConnectionDiagram.tsx`, `RightHandGripRuleDiagram.tsx`, `WaveformSineDiagram.tsx` (peak-to-peak), `Series`/`ParallelCircuitDiagram.tsx` (battery/polarity); `render-tree-to-svg.ts` (Polygon/Ellipse/transform support); `generate-review-package.ts` (dark phone-mockup fix); new `check-visual-completeness.ts` + test; 7 lesson files retagged to v7 + `lesson-cc11-3-historical-snapshot.ts`; `content-releases.ts`/`lessons.ts` v7; `cc05a-pedagogy-unit202.ts`/`cc05d-visual-contracts-unit202.ts`/`canonical-variants.ts` (9 blueprints/contracts/variant-builders each); `magnetism.ts` (Representation-contract fix); regenerated `index.html`, `mechanical-audit.json`, review-package PDF/manifest, `mobile-content-projection.ts`; new `visual-needs-matrix.md`; this file.

Real human/vision-model pixel-level sign-off on the PDF review package is still pending (§9) — this package cannot close that gate itself.
