## CC-11.5 — ALP Local Instructional Visual Production Studio (implementation-complete, production tooling only)

A local, localhost-only development tool for producing and approving premium instructional artwork under the reference-first pipeline [`docs/architecture/PREMIUM-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-PRODUCTION-PIPELINE.md`](docs/architecture/PREMIUM-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-PRODUCTION-PIPELINE.md) and [`ADR-0004`](docs/architecture/adr/ADR-0004-deterministic-authority-over-generated-instructional-imagery.md) already govern. Baseline: `main` @ `adc687b`. **Not learner-facing product functionality** — no product code, content, or artwork was changed; the Unit 202 knowledge/content gate was not reopened; CC-12 not started.

**1. What it is.** `tools/visual-production-studio/` (`npm run visuals:studio`) — a plain `node:http` server plus a static, unbundled HTML/CSS/JS page, zero new npm dependencies. Removes the manual orchestration burden the Product Owner previously carried when running a separate ChatGPT browser session as the art producer: filenames, directories, prompts and provenance records are now generated/handled by the tool, never hand-typed.

**2. Structured production catalogue, not hand-authored prompt strings.** `catalogue.ts` seeds all 24 Unit 202 visual-production entries from the task brief (hand-rule mnemonics, generator/motor-effect, levers/pulleys/gears, magnet field/poles, deterministic-family style references, physical-component companions, and 3 explicitly `REFERENCE_NOT_READY`-blocked candidates), each carrying its reference source/licence/quality grade, immutable technical facts, creative freedoms, deterministic-overlay responsibilities, prohibited changes and governed output location. `prompt-builder.ts` builds every asset's exact copyable prompt deterministically from that data (two calls, same entry, byte-identical output) — never 30 hand-pasted prompt strings in HTML. Every generated production prompt carries task-brief §11's CRITICAL RULE verbatim (inspect actual geometry/arrowheads against the reference; never trust a caption's own claim of correctness).

**3. Safe local save.** `paths.ts` is the sole function permitted to turn an (outputSubfolder, filename) pair into a real filesystem path — closed enum + strict filename regex validated before resolution, then the resolved path is re-checked to still be a descendant of the governed asset root after resolution (defense in depth, not a single check). `approval.ts`'s APPROVE + SAVE orchestration never silently overwrites an existing approved version: with no explicit versioning choice it returns a conflict (HTTP 409) the UI surfaces as CANCEL / SAVE AS NEW VERSION / REPLACE WITH EXPLICIT CONFIRMATION, exactly per task brief §7.

**4. Versioning/provenance.** Approved artwork is frozen per version (`{stem}-v{N}.{ext}`, SHA-256 hashed) and recorded in an **append-only** manifest, `reports/instructional-visuals/premium-artwork/unit202-artwork-manifest.json` — mirroring this repository's existing immutable-content-release discipline (never mutate a past approved record; a REPLACE writes a new record at the same version number, resolved by `approvedAt` as tiebreaker, not by overwriting the old one).

**5. Image paste/drop workflow.** Ctrl+V paste, drag-and-drop, and a file-picker fallback all route through one staging upload; PNG/WEBP/JPEG are sniffed from magic bytes/headers (`image-utils.ts`, zero image-processing dependency), reporting detected dimensions, format, approximate size and (where determinable) transparency before approval.

**6. Status persistence and next-asset ranking.** `state-store.ts` persists per-asset status (`REFERENCE_NOT_READY` → `READY_TO_PROMPT` → `IN_ART_SESSION`/`IMAGE_PASTED` → `NEEDS_REVIEW`/`APPROVED`/`SAVED`/`SUPERSEDED`/`BLOCKED`) to a local JSON file — no database — surviving a Studio restart. `next-asset.ts` ranks by priority, then reference readiness, then status, powering the page's NEXT RECOMMENDED ASSET panel and its `N`-key shortcut.

**7. Review contact sheet.** `contact-sheet.ts` generates a single local HTML page of every currently-approved asset (id, name, thumbnail, reference, status, version) for whole-family review without opening every file individually, per task brief §18.

**8. Repo wiring.** `package.json`: `visuals:studio` script; `typecheck`/`lint` extended to cover the new project. `vitest.config.ts`: `tools/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}` added to test discovery. `eslint.config.mjs`: a scoped browser-globals override for the tool's own unbundled client JS (it runs in a browser, not Node, and is outside every TypeScript project). `.gitignore`: `tools/visual-production-studio/data/` (ephemeral local WIP state/staging — the governed manifest under `reports/` is committed, this working state is not). New governed asset tree: `apps/mobile/src/assets/instructional/unit202/{teaching,conceptual,hybrid,physical-components,deterministic-polish}/` with its own `README.md` (added specifically so the intended folder structure survives in git — empty directories otherwise are not tracked at all).

**9. Validation.** `npm run typecheck` (scoped project): clean. `npm run lint` (scoped): clean, 0 errors. **71/71** new tests pass across 8 files covering exactly the task brief's §19 list: deterministic prompt generation, correct output path per asset, path-traversal rejection, status persistence across a simulated restart, image-save behaviour, existing-file protection (conflict/new-version/replace), manifest append-only update + hashing, catalogue id/sequence/filename-stem uniqueness, invalid-reference-ready-state detection, and Copy Prompt text generation. A live end-to-end smoke test (server started locally, real HTTP requests against `/`, `/api/catalogue`, `/api/master-prompt`, `/api/prompt/:id`, `/api/state`, `/api/next`, `/api/paste/:id`, `/api/approve/:id` including the 409 conflict path, `/api/contact-sheet`) confirmed the full paste → approve → conflict → contact-sheet flow against the real governed asset root; the synthetic fixture image and manifest entry that smoke test produced were deleted before committing — no fake artwork or provenance record shipped in this commit.

**10. Documentation.** `docs/architecture/PREMIUM-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-PRODUCTION-PIPELINE.md` now points to this tool as the manual-assisted production implementation of its §3 reference-first workflow, and its §13 status record now honestly notes: the tool exists, but **zero premium/hybrid artwork has actually been produced or approved through it yet** — the tool's existence is not evidence the artwork does.

**11. Counts.** New files: 10 tool modules (`catalogue.ts`, `master-prompt.ts`, `prompt-builder.ts`, `paths.ts`, `image-utils.ts`, `state-store.ts`, `approval.ts`, `next-asset.ts`, `contact-sheet.ts`, `server.ts`) + 8 test files (71 tests) + 3 static client files (`index.html`/`studio.css`/`studio.js`) + `tsconfig.json` + 2 `README.md` (tool + governed asset tree). Catalogue entries: **24** (8 P0, 10 P1, 6 P2 including 3 `REFERENCE_NOT_READY`-blocked). Approved artwork: **0** (tooling-only package). Repo config files touched: 5 (`package.json`, `vitest.config.ts`, `eslint.config.mjs`, `.gitignore`, plus the pipeline doc).

**12. Remaining / current limitations.** No artwork produced yet — this is production tooling only, exactly as scoped. `OPEN SAVED FILE` and the post-launch browser auto-open are best-effort platform commands (silently no-op on a machine with no GUI). Reference thumbnails are never fetched/cached (rights-respecting by design — each card links to its source page instead). WEBP dimension/alpha detection covers VP8X/VP8L/VP8 container variants only.

**Next package**: use the Studio to actually produce and approve the first P0 assets (hand-rule mnemonics, generator/motor-effect, levers/pulleys/gears, bar magnet) once the Product Owner is ready to run ChatGPT sessions against it. **CC-12 not started, per explicit instruction.**

## CC-11.6 — Variable-Size Visual Families + Studio Two-Prompt Model (implementation-complete, production tooling only)

Restructures the Studio's flat 24-entry catalogue into a **VisualFamily → VisualAsset[]** model where visual count follows pedagogical need, not a fixed template, plus three Product-Owner corrections that arrived mid-package and were integrated into the same restructuring rather than deferred. Baseline: `main` @ `547d15e`. **Not learner-facing product functionality** — no product code, learner content, or artwork was changed; no assertion/question blueprint touched; the Unit 202 knowledge/content gate was not reopened; CC-12 not started.

**1. Visual Family architecture.** `catalogue.ts` now exports `FAMILIES: VisualFamily[]` (each holding an ordered `assets: VisualAsset[]`) instead of a flat list. **20 families, 29 individual assets, 25 currently promptable** (3 blocked on a reference; 1 — the governed UK/IEC symbol system — explicitly has no image-generation deliverable at all, `promptable: false`). 8 families are genuinely multi-asset, 12 are single-asset — both are first-class outcomes, never templated. New `VisualAssetRole` enum (`PHENOMENON`/`MNEMONIC`/`CONFIGURATION`/`SEQUENCE_STATE`/`COMPARISON`/`PHYSICAL_RECOGNITION`/`TECHNICAL_DIAGRAM`/`ASSESSMENT_SUPPORT`/`FEEDBACK_SUPPORT`), never required to be fully used by any family.

**2. Right-hand-grip family, built exactly as specified.** New `unit202.current-conductor.magnetic-field` (PHENOMENON) asset added alongside the existing `unit202.right-hand-grip.teaching` (MNEMONIC) — two assets, not one, so the electromagnetic phenomenon a learner must recognise is never conflated with the mnemonic used to predict it. The same phenomenon+mnemonic pattern was extended by direct analogy to both Fleming families (`unit202.family.fleming-left-hand-motor` = `unit202.motor.effect` + `unit202.fleming-left-hand.teaching`; `unit202.family.fleming-right-hand-generator` = `unit202.generator.rotating-loop` + `unit202.fleming-right-hand.teaching`) — no new assets invented there, purely a regrouping of what already existed.

**3. Pulley family: evidence-based, not assumed.** Before finalising the family, a dedicated research pass reviewed the real governed Unit 202 corpus (`FP-CONCEPT-PULLEY-001`, `FP-PULLEY-FIXED-VS-MOVABLE-001`, `FP-REL-PULLEY-MECHANICAL-ADVANTAGE-001`, `FP-REL-PULLEY-FORCE-DISTANCE-TRADEOFF-001`, `cap.foundational.pulleys.recognise`, the calculation engine's `PULLEY_SCENARIOS`, and every existing scope-boundary comment in `PulleyDiagram.tsx`/the visual-semantic-contract). Verdict: the corpus teaches fixed-vs-movable and the qualitative more/fewer-supporting-segments relationship only; multiple governed artefacts explicitly declare multi-pulley/block-and-tackle content out of scope. Result: `unit202.family.pulleys` stays at exactly two assets (fixed; movable) — confirmed, not assumed, and not expanded merely because the tooling now supports more than one asset per family.

**4. Levers and resistivity split into genuinely distinct assets.** `unit202.levers.classes` (one entry, implicitly asking for three images from one prompt) became three real CONFIGURATION assets (`unit202.levers.class-1/2/3`) in `unit202.family.levers`. `unit202.resistivity.dimensions` likewise split into `unit202.resistivity.length-comparison` / `unit202.resistivity.area-comparison` (both COMPARISON role) — length and area are two independently-varying quantities a single image cannot cleanly compare at once. Magnetism (`unit202.magnet.field` + `unit202.magnet.poles`) and electronic components (`unit202.components.symbols` + `unit202.components.physical`) were regrouped into families without inventing or splitting further, per the explicit "only split where a distinct image is genuinely required" instruction.

**5. Prompt granularity never reduced by grouping.** A VisualFamily is an organisational display grouping only. Every individual asset — even one of three lever classes or two pulley configurations — gets its own fully independent, deterministically-generated ASSET-SPECIFIC PROMPT (`buildAssetPrompt(asset, family)`), each ending with an explicit "Produce ONLY this asset — do not automatically create the other members of the visual family" line. The Studio's two prompt layers remain exactly two: PROMPT 1 (`master-prompt.ts`'s MASTER ART SESSION PROMPT, used once per session) and PROMPT 2 (the per-asset prompt, used once per asset, never once per family) — confirmed by a dedicated test proving every one of the 29 assets produces byte-unique prompt text.

**6. ANNOTATION FOLLOWS PEDAGOGICAL STATE (Product Owner correction, integrated mid-package).** The original "premium artwork should not bake in labels" default was wrong and is now corrected: teaching artwork MAY and often SHOULD carry explanatory labels (THUMB = CURRENT, FINGERS = MAGNETIC FIELD, EFFORT/LOAD/FULCRUM, N/S, etc.) — an aesthetically clean but pedagogically incomplete image is a failed deliverable, exactly like a technically wrong one. New `AnnotationPolicy` field (`TEACHING_EXPLANATORY | ASSESSMENT_NON_REVEALING | FEEDBACK_EXPLANATORY | NONE`) plus `requiredLabels: string[]` on every asset; every generated prompt now states explicitly whether labels are REQUIRED (and names them), PERMITTED-but-non-revealing, or OMITTED — never left implicit. `docs/architecture/PREMIUM-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-PRODUCTION-PIPELINE.md` §8 rewritten to record the corrected rule as the durable architecture position, not just a Studio implementation detail.

**7. Default premium surface (second Product Owner correction, integrated mid-package).** Premium illustrated teaching artwork now defaults to a muted, medium-dark slate/blue-grey background with a subtle gradient — softer and cooler than pure black — rather than defaulting to the app's own `#0B0D12` UI black. Recorded as a new architecture principle (pipeline doc, "Default premium surface" under §11) and automatically injected into every HYBRID/PREMIUM_CONCEPTUAL asset's generated prompt (`prompt-builder.ts`'s `backgroundInstruction()`) unless that specific asset sets `backgroundStyleOverride`; DETERMINISTIC_TECHNICAL assets (schematics/graphs/symbols/assessment tiles, which have no illustrated background at all) are automatically excluded, never touched per-asset by the Product Owner.

**8. Studio family-grouped UI.** `public/index.html`/`studio.js`/`studio.css` restructured: the catalogue renders as collapsible family sections (`<details>`, native, no extra JS state machine), each showing the family's display name, asset count, and a live `[x/N approved]` progress badge; every individual asset card inside remains fully independent (its own COPY PROMPT/VIEW PROMPT/paste-drop/approve-save/status controls, unchanged from CC-11.5). Filters now hide a whole family section only when none of its member assets match. A new header line reports the three-way prompt accounting (family count / individual asset count / promptable artwork asset count) so this distinction is visible, not just internally tracked.

**9. Contact sheet grouped by family.** `contact-sheet.ts` now groups approved assets under their family's display name in family declaration order, with a per-family `[x/N approved]` badge, so visual consistency and pedagogical progression across related images can be reviewed together — a family with zero approvals gets no section at all (no empty clutter).

**10. Validation.** `npm run typecheck` (scoped project): clean. `npm run lint` (scoped, including the browser-side `studio.js`): clean, 0 errors. **112/112** tests pass across the 8 existing test files (up from 71 — 41 new/rewritten tests), covering every item in task-brief §18: variable-size families, single- and multi-asset families, family/asset id uniqueness, ordered family display, per-asset deterministic prompt generation, master-prompt separation, family-aware next-recommended logic (including a synthetic fixture proving a REFERENCE_NOT_READY sibling never blocks an actionable co-member), grouped contact-sheet output, plus full regression of every CC-11.5 safe-save/versioning/status/hashing behaviour. A live end-to-end smoke test against the family-shaped `/api/catalogue`/`/api/next`/`/api/prompt`/`/api/contact-sheet` endpoints confirmed correct nested family/asset JSON shape and prompt content; full repo battery (`typecheck`/`lint`/`test:unit`) re-run clean at **876/876** root tests.

**11. Counts.** Families: 24 flat entries → **20 families / 29 assets** (+5 net: +1 new phenomenon asset, +2 from the lever split, +1 from the resistivity split, +1 net from other regrouping/no change). Promptable artwork assets: **25** of 29. Multi-asset families: **8**. Single-asset families: **12**. Root Vitest: 835 → **876** (+41, all in `tools/visual-production-studio`). Approved artwork: still **0** — this remains tooling-only. Documentation: `PREMIUM-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-PRODUCTION-PIPELINE.md` gained §14 (Visual families), §15 (Studio prompt architecture), a rewritten §8 (annotation policy), a "default premium surface" principle under §11, and an explicit restated §12 (visual completeness is neither "every lesson has an image" nor "every concept has N images").

**12. Remaining / current limitations.** Still zero artwork produced — production tooling only, exactly as scoped. The pulley family's evidence-based two-asset conclusion is recorded but not independently re-verified by a second reviewer. `needsScopeConfirmation`/`SCOPE_CONFIRMATION_NEEDED` machinery exists and is tested but currently has no real occupant in the live catalogue (pulleys resolved cleanly; no other family hit genuine ambiguity this pass) — it is ready for the first future case that does.

**Next package**: use the Studio to actually produce and approve the first P0 family assets. **CC-12 not started, per explicit instruction.**

## CC-11.7 — Full Unit 202 Visual Pedagogy Audit and Comprehensive Production Catalogue (audit + catalogue + governance, no artwork)

A full-corpus audit treating the existing 66 canonical variants as **a baseline to reconcile, not the target visual count** — every current lesson, assertion, capability, formula family, misconception, question blueprint, DiagramBlueprint, VisualSemanticContract and canonical-variant builder was re-examined, both LO-by-LO and independently assertion-by-assertion, and the catalogue restructured into a third hierarchy level. Baseline: `main` @ `8568f76`. **Audit, catalogue, Studio and governance only** — no assertion/capability/question-blueprint/lesson content changed, no artwork generated, CC-12 not started.

**1. Three-level hierarchy.** `catalogue.ts`'s `VisualAsset` now carries a required `canonicalStates: CanonicalState[]` array — VISUAL FAMILY → PRODUCTION/BASE ASSET → CANONICAL LEARNER-VISIBLE STATE. A base asset may support several canonical states (all 8 motor pole/current/force combinations on one base; both TEACHING and ASSESSMENT presentations of one phenomenon); states are never collapsed merely because they share a base. Each `CanonicalState` declares its own `pedagogicalState` (`TEACHING`/`PRACTICE`/`ASSESSMENT`/`FEEDBACK`/`MULTI_STATE`) and `annotationPolicy`, so ANNOTATION FOLLOWS PEDAGOGICAL STATE applies per state, not just per asset.

**2. Zero existing canonical variants silently lost — mechanically verified.** New `reconciledVariantId()` reproduces the real CC-05D `stableVariantId` algorithm exactly (parameters merged with `mode` *before* sorting keys, not appended after — a bug caught and fixed via cross-check during this package) so every `CanonicalState.existingCanonicalVariantId` is computed from real inputs, never hand-transcribed. All **66 of 66** real canonical variants (recomputed live from `canonical-variants.ts`/`cc05d-visual-contracts-unit202.ts`, never trusted from the catalogue's own claims) are reconciled onto states across the 32 assets; `audit.ts`'s own gate reports **0 unmapped**. None superseded — all 66 retained.

**3. Genuine new REQUIRED needs found beyond the 66.** Three brand-new assets added to `unit202.family.electronic-components`: `unit202.diode.bias-direction` (HYBRID, forward/reverse-bias states, targets `MIS-EL-DIODE-DIRECTION-CONFUSION-001`), `unit202.rectification.waveforms` (DETERMINISTIC_TECHNICAL, half-wave/full-wave/inverter states), `unit202.capacitor.transient` (DETERMINISTIC_TECHNICAL, charge/discharge states). A fourth finding — `unit202.electrolysis` — was upgraded in priority on existing-corpus corroboration rather than added as a new asset. Two further genuine gaps (`unit202.components.physical` gaining 6 REQUIRED per-component states; the instrumentation lesson's ohmmeter diagram never actually being shown by any lesson step despite the underlying deterministic asset being correct) were found and are documented as corrections, the latter flagged for a future content-integration package rather than silently fixed here.

**4. Corrections to the prior session's own reasoning.** Two earlier audit conclusions were independently re-verified, not re-stated: the mass-and-weight NOT_NEEDED call still holds but its original justification ("the formula triangle already carries it") was factually wrong — no such formula family exists — and is now recorded on corrected grounds; the ohmmeter-coverage gap above was found by re-checking claimed vs. actual lesson steps rather than trusting the earlier readiness report.

**5. Classification is now explicit and derived, not asserted.** New `VisualNeedClassification` (`REQUIRED`/`USEFUL`/`NOT_NEEDED`/`DEFERRED_SCOPE`/`BLOCKED_REFERENCE`) and `visualNeedClassificationFor(asset)`, computed mechanically from `referenceReadiness`/`needsScopeConfirmation`. Live counts: **28 REQUIRED**, **3 BLOCKED_REFERENCE** (heating-effect, conductor-insulator, protective-devices — never removed for lacking a reference, tracked until one is supplied), **1 DEFERRED_SCOPE** (trigonometry). USEFUL findings (**10**, e.g. clamp-meter/oscilloscope recognition, electron-flow-vs-conventional-current, 5 specialist component types) are deliberately tracked but *not* materialised as catalogue assets this pass, per the brief's explicit instruction not to over-produce; **9 lessons** confirmed NOT_NEEDED at lesson granularity, each with recorded reasoning.

**6. Comprehensive machine- and human-readable catalogue.** New `generate-matrix.ts` (`npm run visuals:studio:matrix`) writes `reports/instructional-visuals/unit202-visual-coverage-matrix.json` (51 rows: every catalogued asset plus the USEFUL/NOT_NEEDED findings) derived live from the catalogue, never hand-typed. New `reports/instructional-visuals/unit202-comprehensive-visual-audit.md` is the full human-readable audit report: 66-variant reconciliation table, lesson-by-lesson corrections, new REQUIRED needs, USEFUL/NOT_NEEDED findings, Studio changes, final honest counts.

**7. Deterministic completeness gate.** New `audit.ts` (`npm run visuals:studio:audit`) fails on: an existing canonical variant left unmapped, a REQUIRED need with no owning family, a REQUIRED premium/hybrid asset with no working prompt, a REQUIRED premium/hybrid asset with no reference or BLOCKED_REFERENCE status, a family referencing a nonexistent blueprint, a canonical state with no pedagogical state, an ASSESSMENT state leaking a known answer-bearing mnemonic dependency, or any duplicate family/asset/state id. Live run: **PASS, all zero.** Tested with intentionally-injected-gap cases (state removal, blanked reference, duplicated id, leaked mnemonic) to prove each check actually fires.

**8. Dashboard replaces the single misleading total.** New `dashboard.ts` computes 12 distinct counts mechanically from the live catalogue + Studio status data (families / production assets / canonical states / REQUIRED / USEFUL-tracked / deterministic-only / premium-hybrid art jobs / approved / outstanding / BLOCKED_REFERENCE / DEFERRED_SCOPE / SUPERSEDED) — no single "X of Y" figure is shown anywhere. Wired into a new `GET /api/dashboard` endpoint and a new dashboard section in `public/index.html`/`studio.js`/`studio.css`.

**9. Prompts enumerate every state a base asset must support.** New `canonicalStatesInstruction(asset)` in `prompt-builder.ts` lists each canonical state (pedagogical state, display name, required labels, notes) inside that asset's single ASSET-SPECIFIC PROMPT — still exactly two prompt layers per the CC-11.6 model (master once per session, one prompt per asset, never per family or per state). Deterministic-only states are never given an art prompt at all.

**10. Validation.** Full repo battery re-run clean after every CC-11.7 change: `npm run typecheck` (root + every workspace + all 5 scoped script/tool projects) — clean. `npm run lint` (root + every workspace + scoped projects) — clean, only 6 pre-existing unrelated `import/first` warnings in `apps/mobile` test files. `npm run test:unit` — **897/897** root Vitest tests pass (up from 876, +21 new/extended in `tools/visual-production-studio` covering the CC-11.7 §18/§27 checklist: reconciliation correctness, hierarchy containment, dashboard math, audit-gate true positives/negatives, matrix generation). `npm run test --workspace apps/mobile` — **331/331** Jest tests pass, unchanged (no mobile app code touched). `npm run visuals:studio:audit` — clean.

**11. Counts.** Families: **20** (unchanged). Production/base assets: 29 → **32** (+3). Canonical learner-visible states: **87** total, of which **66** reconcile an existing CC-05D variant (0 lost, 0 superseded) and **21** are newly declared. Promptable artwork assets: 25 → **26**. Premium/hybrid art-production jobs: **23** assets. Deterministic-only states (no art prompt ever generated): **38**. REQUIRED: **28**. BLOCKED_REFERENCE: **3**. DEFERRED_SCOPE: **1**. USEFUL tracked-not-catalogued: **10**. NOT_NEEDED lessons: **9**. Approved artwork: still **0** — this remains audit/catalogue/tooling only. Root Vitest: 876 → **897** (+21).

The 3 BLOCKED_REFERENCE assets and 1 DEFERRED_SCOPE asset (trigonometry) remain genuinely blocked/deferred, not silently dropped — they surface in the dashboard's own counts every time the Studio runs. The instrumentation lesson's ohmmeter-diagram non-display gap is documented but intentionally left unfixed (content-integration work is out of scope for an audit-only package). The 10 USEFUL findings are tracked in the matrix/report but have no catalogue asset yet — a future package would need to explicitly decide to materialise any of them, not assume this audit already did.

## CC-11.7A — Materialise the Complete Visual Production Queue + One Art Prompt Per Distinct Image Job (Studio tooling only, no re-audit)

A focused follow-up to CC-11.7, not a re-audit: the CC-11.7 audit's conclusions are accepted unchanged. Fixes two production-tooling gaps so the Studio becomes the *complete* Unit 202 visual production queue (REQUIRED + USEFUL), not merely the minimum-REQUIRED one. Baseline: `main` @ `f22195c`. **Studio/catalogue/governance only** — no assertion/capability/question-blueprint/lesson content changed, no artwork generated, no CC-11.7 REQUIRED finding reopened or weakened, CC-12 not started.

**1. All 10 CC-11.7 USEFUL findings materialised.** Every finding CC-11.7 §4 tracked but deliberately left documentation-only now has a live `catalogue.ts` asset: family assignment, `CanonicalState`(s), and a new `needOverride: "USEFUL"` field — a distinct, permanent marker kept separate from `VisualNeedClassification`'s REQUIRED-by-default derivation, so a materialised USEFUL asset can never silently become REQUIRED merely because it is now visible. Clamp meter and oscilloscope recognition joined the `instrument-connections` family; the conventional-current-vs-electron-flow diagram got its own new family (`unit202.family.current-direction`, DETERMINISTIC_TECHNICAL — a dual-arrow geometric fact, not an artistic subject); the magnet-vs-electromagnet comparison joined `magnetism`; the gear rotation-direction/idler finding joined `gears` as a DETERMINISTIC annotation overlay on the *existing* `unit202.gears` base artwork (no new art job); the 5 specialist component recognition findings (zener diode, photodiode, DIAC, TRIAC, thyristor/SCR) became 5 independent `electronic-components` assets. 8 of the 10 are honestly `BLOCKED_REFERENCE` (no individually-sourced reference approved yet) — tracked, never fabricated, never removed for lacking one.

**2. `unit202.components.physical` split from 1 asset into 6.** The pre-existing asset carried 6 `CanonicalState`s for 6 genuinely different physical objects (resistor/capacitor/diode/LED/thermistor/transistor) behind one prompt/filename/approval slot — a real "ONE ART PROMPT PER DISTINCT IMAGE JOB" violation, not a stylistic choice (task brief's own "INCORRECT EXAMPLE — ELECTRONIC COMPONENTS"). Split into 6 independent `ProductionAsset`s (`unit202.components.physical.resistor`/`.capacitor`/`.diode`/`.led`/`.thermistor`/`.transistor`), each with its own prompt/filename/save-slot/approval state. REQUIRED classification and READY reference status left exactly as previously accepted — only structural granularity changed, per the explicit "do not reopen or weaken the accepted REQUIRED findings" constraint. The 5 new specialist USEFUL physical-recognition assets (item 1) were built directly in this corrected shape from the start.

**3. Structural gate: `validateCatalogue()` now enforces the split mechanically.** A `PHYSICAL_RECOGNITION`-role asset may never carry more than one `CanonicalState` — a `PHENOMENON`/`CONFIGURATION`/`COMPARISON`/etc. asset legitimately may (deterministic annotation on one shared base), but a physical-recognition asset depicts one specific real object and must always be its own independent art job.

**4. REQUIRED and USEFUL tracked as fully independent buckets, everywhere.** `dashboard.ts` rewritten: `required`/`useful` asset counts, `requiredPremiumHybridArtJobs`/`usefulPremiumHybridArtJobs`/total, `approvedRequired`/`outstandingRequired`/`approvedUseful`/`outstandingUseful`, plus `blockedReferenceRequired`/`blockedReferenceUseful` — USEFUL progress can never make REQUIRED completeness look incomplete, or vice versa (verified by dedicated dashboard tests approving one of each independently). Studio UI gained a prominent "REQUIRED ART x/y APPROVED" / "OPTIONAL (USEFUL) ART x/y APPROVED" block above the existing 12-stat dashboard grid (now expanded to split REQUIRED/USEFUL art-job counts), a REQUIRED/USEFUL badge on every asset card, and new filters (Required / Useful / Blocked / Premium-Hybrid / Deterministic, alongside the pre-existing priority/output/approval filters). `next-asset.ts`'s NEXT RECOMMENDED ASSET ranking now sorts REQUIRED before USEFUL ahead of priority, so a USEFUL P0 item is never recommended ahead of an actionable REQUIRED one. `contact-sheet.ts` shows a REQUIRED/USEFUL badge on every approved-asset card.

**5. `audit.ts` extended with two new FATAL gates.** `usefulFindingsMissingFromCatalogue` — fails if any of the 10 CC-11.7 findings (`EXPECTED_USEFUL_FINDING_ASSET_IDS`, mechanically enumerated from the audit report, not invented) is missing from the live catalogue or has lost its `needOverride`. `catalogueStructuralProblems` — folds in `validateCatalogue()`'s own checks (including the new PHYSICAL_RECOGNITION-single-state rule, and the pre-existing duplicate-filenameBase/stateId/assetId checks) as FATAL audit failures, not merely a separate, easy-to-skip check. Both proven with intentionally-injected-gap tests (removing a finding's asset, stripping its `needOverride`, giving a physical-recognition asset a second state, duplicating a filenameBase).

**6. Validation.** Full repo battery re-run clean: `npm run typecheck` — clean. `npm run lint` — clean, same 6 pre-existing unrelated `import/first` warnings in `apps/mobile` test files, 0 errors. `npm run test:unit` — **918/918** root Vitest tests pass (up from 897, +21 new/extended in `tools/visual-production-studio`: catalogue split/materialisation tests, dashboard REQUIRED/USEFUL independence tests, audit gap-detection tests, prompt-uniqueness and needClassification tests, contact-sheet badge test, next-asset REQUIRED-before-USEFUL test). `npm run test --workspace apps/mobile` — **331/331** unchanged (no mobile app code touched). `npm run visuals:studio:audit` — clean, all gates zero, including the two new ones. Live server smoke test of `/api/dashboard` and `/api/catalogue` confirmed correct REQUIRED/USEFUL split accounting end-to-end.

**7. Counts.** Visual families: 20 → **21** (+1: `unit202.family.current-direction`). Production/base assets: 32 → **47** (+15: +5 from the physical-component split, +10 materialised USEFUL). Canonical states: 87 → **98** (+11). REQUIRED assets: 28 → **33** (+5, entirely from the split — no REQUIRED finding added/removed). USEFUL assets (materialised): **10** (2 DETERMINISTIC_TECHNICAL/READY with no art job; 8 PREMIUM_CONCEPTUAL/HYBRID currently `BLOCKED_REFERENCE`). BLOCKED_REFERENCE: 3 → **11** (+8, all USEFUL). REQUIRED premium/hybrid art jobs: **25**. USEFUL premium/hybrid art jobs currently live (unblocked): **0** (honest — none of the 8 blocked USEFUL findings has a sourced reference yet). `usefulTrackedNotCatalogued`: 10 → **0**. All 66 historical CC-05D canonical variants remain reconciled (0 lost, 0 superseded) — unaffected by this package. Root Vitest: 897 → **918** (+21).

**8. Remaining / current limitations.** Still zero artwork produced. 8 of the 10 materialised USEFUL findings remain reference-blocked — this package deliberately did not fabricate references to make them appear promptable; sourcing real references for any of them is a future step. The gear rotation-direction/idler USEFUL finding is annotation-only on the existing `unit202.gears` base artwork by design (no new art job), consistent with the audit report's own "cheap addition if commissioned" framing.

**Next package**: source references for the 8 blocked USEFUL findings if the Product Owner chooses to commission them, and/or use the Studio to produce and approve the first REQUIRED family assets. **CC-12 not started, per explicit instruction.**

## CC-11.7B — Final Pre-Production Visual Asset Audit, Shared-Base Validation, Completeness Accounting, and Product-Owner PDF Review Pack

The FINAL pre-production audit before premium artwork production begins. Answers one question mechanically: have the 98 canonical learner-visible states been compressed into the *correct* number of actual production images? Not "internally consistent" — genuinely, individually audited. Baseline: `main` @ `288bd2a`. **Audit, catalogue, Studio, and review-pack generation only** — no assertion/capability/question-blueprint/lesson content changed, no artwork generated, no REQUIRED finding's underlying pedagogical need reopened, CC-12 not started.

**1. Every multi-state ProductionAsset individually audited.** All 18 multi-state assets (CC-11.7A baseline) reviewed against: does the asset's own `exactDeliverable` text already ask for more than one image; do the grouped states differ only by deterministic overlay (label/arrow/dimension/reveal-hide), or by a genuine pose/configuration/scene change; is a declared `deterministicOverlayResponsibilities` entry actually sufficient to cover the difference. New `SharedBaseAudit` type/field (`catalogue.ts`) records `classification` (`SAFE_SHARED_BASE`/`SHARED_BASE_WITH_CONDITIONS`/`SEPARATE_ARTWORK_REQUIRED`), `action` (`KEEP`/`KEEP_WITH_CONDITIONS`/`SPLIT`), `rationale`, and (for a split result) `splitFrom`/`splitSiblings`. 100% coverage mechanically enforced by both `validateCatalogue()` and a new `audit.ts` gate.

**2. Two real composite-prompt defects found and fixed.** `unit202.magnet.poles` and `unit202.diode.bias-direction` each had `exactDeliverable` text that literally asked for "Two premium illustrations" inside one prompt/asset/filename/approval-slot — the exact violation CC-11.7A itself named but had only fixed for `PHYSICAL_RECOGNITION`-role assets, not `COMPARISON`/`PHENOMENON`-role ones. Both split.

**3. Five assets split, one kept-with-corrected-conditions, one role corrected.** `unit202.motor.effect` (8 states) → `.horizontal-poles`/`.vertical-poles` (pole orientation is a genuine apparatus-layout change; current direction within one orientation stays a shared overlay concern). `unit202.generator.rotating-loop` (2 states) → `.horizontal`/`.vertical` (face-on vs edge-on loop pose is a materially different 3D silhouette, no overlay can rotate it). `unit202.magnet.poles` (4 states) → `.like`/`.unlike` (attracting/repelling magnets are conventionally composed with different spacing/field behaviour). `unit202.gears` (3 states) → `.driven-larger`/`.driven-smaller`/`.equal` (driven-gear size is a genuine physical size change to a rendered object; no overlay mechanism can resize a gear). `unit202.diode.bias-direction` (2 states) → `.forward`/`.reverse` (conducting vs blocked current is a genuine presence/absence scene difference). `unit202.current-conductor.magnetic-field` (4 states) kept, corrected: direction indicators moved from "baked into artwork" (an internal inconsistency — the pre-audit config asked for a direction-specific image while needing to serve both directions) to explicit `deterministicOverlayResponsibilities`, matching the reasoning the sibling MNEMONIC hand asset already used. `unit202.protective-devices` role corrected `PHYSICAL_RECOGNITION` → `COMPARISON` (a two-object comparison was mismatched to a single-object-recognition role); explicitly confirmed as one intentional fuse-vs-breaker composite per the brief's own accepted-composite example.

**4. Zero states lost, zero historical variants disturbed.** 98 canonical states before this package, 98 after (every state redistributed onto its split asset's new home, none dropped). All 66 historical CC-05D variants remain reconciled — re-verified mechanically after every split, since a split moving `existingCanonicalVariantId` values to new parent assets is exactly the kind of change that could silently orphan one if done carelessly.

**5. REQUIRED/USEFUL vs BLOCKED_REFERENCE conflation fixed.** `visualNeedClassificationFor()` previously returned `"BLOCKED_REFERENCE"` for any `NOT_READY` asset, silently overriding its REQUIRED/USEFUL classification and excluding it from the REQUIRED/USEFUL totals everywhere downstream — exactly the bug the task brief's own §12 named. Corrected: `VisualNeedClassification` no longer includes `BLOCKED_REFERENCE` at all (now `REQUIRED`/`USEFUL`/`NOT_NEEDED`/`DEFERRED_SCOPE` only); a new `isReferenceBlocked()` reads production readiness as a fully independent dimension. `dashboard.ts` rewritten around this: `requiredTotal`/`requiredReady`/`requiredBlocked`, `usefulTotal`/`usefulReady`/`usefulBlocked`, `requiredArtJobsTotal/Ready/Blocked/Approved`, `usefulArtJobsTotal/Ready/Blocked/Approved`, and a new `requiredVisualProductionComplete` boolean (true only when every REQUIRED art job is approved AND `requiredBlocked` is exactly 0 — USEFUL status never affects it). `generate-matrix.ts`'s `gapStatus` now reports both dimensions together (e.g. `REQUIRED_BLOCKED`) instead of one silently overwriting the other. `audit.ts` gained a regression-proof gate (`requiredBlockedExcludedFromRequiredTotal`) and Studio UI gained a prominent REQUIRED/OPTIONAL completion banner plus per-bucket dashboard stats.

**6. Real, previously-undetected bug found and fixed: `paths.ts`'s `REPO_ROOT`.** While building this package's own test suite (which, unlike any prior test, actually reads real files from `reports/instructional-visuals/` via a `REPO_ROOT`-derived path), discovered that `repoRoot()`'s `new URL(".", import.meta.url).pathname` computation silently resolved to `D:\` (drive root) under this repo's Vitest config (`environment: "jsdom"`) — jsdom's bundled WHATWG URL polyfill mishandles a Windows `file:///D:/...` URL's drive letter. Every `REPO_ROOT`-derived constant (`MANIFEST_PATH`, `STAGING_ROOT`, `APPROVED_ASSET_ROOT`, `STATE_PATH`, `CONTACT_SHEET_PATH`) was silently wrong under Vitest, undetected because no prior test exercised the literal resolved value against a real file. Fixed by computing `repoRoot()` via `fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)` + `dirname()` (a Node builtin operating on the raw URL string, unaffected by jsdom's `URL` polyfill) instead of manual `URL`/`pathname` parsing.

**7. The Product Owner's pre-production PDF review pack.** New `tools/visual-production-studio/generate-final-review.ts` (`npm run visuals:studio:final-review`) generates `reports/instructional-visuals/unit202-final-visual-production-review.{pdf,json}` entirely from live catalogue/dashboard/audit data (Playwright chromium `page.pdf()`, the same zero-new-dependency approach `scripts/visual-governance/generate-review-package.ts` already uses). 53-page PDF: cover with the mandatory "PRE-PRODUCTION REVIEW — NO ARTWORK GENERATION SHOULD BEGIN UNTIL APPROVED" statement, executive summary, how-to-read glossary, full catalogue by LO/lesson (real embedded deterministic SVG previews where governed renders already exist -- verified by direct visual inspection of the rendered PDF; honest "ARTWORK NOT YET PRODUCED" placeholder everywhere else, never a fabricated mockup), multi-state sharing review table (all 30 audited decisions), the actual 42-row image-generation job list, deterministic visual list, blocked-references section (split REQUIRED/USEFUL), right-hand/Fleming/motor/generator directional-safety section, component-recognition section (every physical-recognition job shown individually), final production-readiness table (every art job explicitly READY TO GENERATE or BLOCKED, never ambiguous), and a final mechanical audit statement.

**8. Validation.** Full repo battery re-run clean: `npm run typecheck` — clean. `npm run lint` — clean, same 6 pre-existing unrelated warnings, 0 errors. `npm run test:unit` — **948/948** root Vitest tests pass (up from 918, +30: new `generate-final-review.test.ts` (20 tests, including the §27 "PDF source model matches live catalogue exactly" equality checks), 3 new CC-11.7B gap-detection tests in `audit.test.ts`, plus extended `catalogue.test.ts`/`dashboard.test.ts`/`next-asset.test.ts` coverage for the splits and the REQUIRED/blocked correction). `npm run test --workspace apps/mobile` — **331/331** unchanged. `npm run visuals:studio:audit` — clean, all 14 gates zero (3 new: multi-state shared-base coverage, split-decision traceability, REQUIRED-blocked-exclusion regression proof). PDF visually inspected page-by-page (cover, executive summary, embedded-preview pages, multi-state sharing table, art-job list, blocked-references, directional-safety, production-readiness, final-audit) — confirmed legible, correctly badged, no fabricated artwork, and (after the `artPromptRequired` bug fix in item 9) correctly shows every blocked asset in both the Blocked References and Final Production Readiness sections.

**9. Bug found and fixed during this package's own build: `artPromptRequired` gated on `isPromptable()`.** The review-pack generator's first draft computed `artPromptRequired: isPromptable(asset) && ...`, but `isPromptable()` itself requires `referenceReadiness === "READY"` — meaning every blocked REQUIRED/USEFUL asset silently reported `artPromptRequired: false`, making the Blocked References section always empty and the Final Production Readiness table silently omit every blocked row. Caught by direct visual inspection of the rendered PDF (not by an automated test, since the test suite was written against the same flawed premise) and fixed by decoupling `artPromptRequired` from readiness (`productionClass !== "DETERMINISTIC_TECHNICAL" && promptable !== false`, independent of `referenceReadiness`) — re-verified by re-inspecting the regenerated PDF.

**10. Counts.** Visual families: **21** (unchanged). Production/base assets: 47 → **53** (+6 net from 5 splits). Canonical states: **98** (unchanged — zero lost). REQUIRED: 33 → **42** total (**39 ready, 3 blocked** — the increase is the accounting correction from item 5, not a new pedagogical need). USEFUL: **10** total (**2 ready, 8 blocked**). Deterministic-only: **11**. DEFERRED_SCOPE: **1** (trigonometry). Shared-base decisions audited: **30** (18 multi-state + 12 single-state/composite) — **18 KEPT** (SAFE_SHARED_BASE), **3 KEPT WITH CONDITIONS**, **9 SPLIT** (SEPARATE_ARTWORK_REQUIRED). Total art jobs: **42** (**34 REQUIRED** — 31 ready, 3 blocked; **8 USEFUL** — 0 ready, 8 blocked). `REQUIRED VISUAL PRODUCTION COMPLETE`: **false** (0 of 34 REQUIRED art jobs approved; 3 remain blocked). Root Vitest: 918 → **948** (+30).

**11. Remaining / current limitations.** Still zero artwork produced. The 3 REQUIRED and 8 USEFUL blocked assets remain genuinely blocked pending a sourced reference — this package did not source any (out of scope; audit/catalogue/tooling only). `requiredVisualProductionComplete` is `false` and will remain so until a human actually produces and approves the 34 REQUIRED art jobs through the Studio.

**Next package**: **the Product Owner must review and approve `reports/instructional-visuals/unit202-final-visual-production-review.pdf` before any artwork generation begins.** Once approved, use the Studio to produce and approve the 34 REQUIRED art jobs (sourcing references for the 3 currently blocked). **CC-12 not started, per explicit instruction.**

## CC-11.7C — Tighten Visual Production Readiness

A focused correction pass on the CC-11.7B catalogue, found while beginning CC-11.8's real production-pipeline work: 15 assets carried `referenceReadiness: "READY"` with placeholder/non-specific reference text rather than a genuinely sourced, verifiable reference — meaning the Studio would have treated them as promptable when no real reference actually backed them. Corrected to `NOT_READY` with the placeholder replaced by an explicit `NOT_READY_REF` marker. `isPromptable()` also corrected to exclude `DETERMINISTIC_TECHNICAL` assets (vector-authoritative, never an art-generation job) from the promptable/art-job flow, and the Studio UI updated to show a vector-authoritative badge with no COPY/VIEW PROMPT buttons and no raster output format claimed for those assets. The three hand-rule assets (`unit202.right-hand-grip.teaching`, `unit202.fleming-left-hand.teaching`, `unit202.fleming-right-hand.teaching`) gained an explicit `"DO NOT MIRROR OR HORIZONTALLY FLIP HAND-RULE ARTWORK"` prohibition (a mirrored hand rule teaches the wrong rule, and is not a "legitimate deterministic transform" — corrected wording that had incorrectly implied otherwise).

**Commit:** `34318a2`, pushed to `origin/main`.

**Next**: proceed to the automated production pipeline itself (CC-11.8).

## CC-11.8 — Automated Visual-Production Architecture, Two-Asset Gemini Proof & Project Dashboards

Establishes the canonical automated instructional-visual production architecture (not yet run at scale) and proves it end-to-end against the live Gemini API on 2 real Unit 202 assets. Also builds a local project-visibility dashboard (roadmap + platform-flow). **Does not** process the full Unit 202 visual production queue, does not source new references beyond the 2 assets' own already-approved ones, and does not start CC-12.

**1. Division of responsibility, locked.** `docs/design/ALP-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-STYLE-GUIDE.md` (new) and `docs/architecture/PREMIUM-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-PRODUCTION-PIPELINE.md` §20 (new) record: Claude/Claude Code determines visual need from the governed corpus, creates/maintains visual asset contracts, classifies production method, orchestrates the pipeline, acquires and caches APPROVED references, sends reference + prompt + style to Gemini, performs the independent technical/pedagogical audit, runs bounded automatic retries, and produces the human-readable review pack — **never silently promoting an uncertain image**. ChatGPT / Product Owner performs reference research (verifying a candidate reference supports the required geometry; recording provenance/licensing) — Claude may identify that a reference is missing but must not independently substitute an unapproved one. Gemini (`gemini-3.1-flash-image`, official `@google/genai` SDK) is an **implementation renderer only**: it receives the actual approved reference image file (never merely a URL), the asset's production contract, and the canonical ALP style guide; it redraws/re-illustrates; it is never technical authority. Product Owner remains final approval authority. `ADR-0004`'s Consequences section and `ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md`'s content-publication-boundary paragraph both updated to point at this.

**2. Canonical style guide.** `docs/design/ALP-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-STYLE-GUIDE.md` consolidates already-established direction (not new invention): product character (industrial precision + premium adult/vocational learning product — explicitly not children's art, white textbook diagrams, corporate clip-art, cyberpunk/neon, or advertising-dramatic); a governed background colour table sourced directly from the app's own real tokens (`apps/mobile/src/lib/tokens.ts`'s `color.surface = #151821` / `color.border = #262B38`, a muted dark slate/blue-grey gradient — explicitly distinct from the app's near-black UI chrome `color.background = #0B0D12`, never used for illustration backgrounds); raster-vs-vector responsibility (raster = appearance/material/lighting; vector/deterministic = labels/arrows/dimensions/exact geometry/waveforms/assessment-state); a default label policy (generate clean base art, do not bake ordinary labels unless the asset contract explicitly justifies it); the technical-reference-vs-style-reference distinction (technical truth always wins); and a family-consistency note. `docs/design/visual-style-references/README.md` created as the style-reference asset location — structure/metadata only, since no canonical style-board image exists yet to approve (deliberately not fabricated).

**3. Real pipeline code, zero new image-processing dependencies.** `tools/visual-production-studio/gemini-client.ts` (official `@google/genai` SDK, `ai.models.generateContent()` with `Part.inlineData` base64 attachments — verified directly against the installed SDK's own `.d.ts` types, not trusted from an unreliable web search result). `tools/visual-production-studio/reference-acquisition.ts` (downloads and SHA-256-hashes a reference, verifies it is real image/SVG data and not an HTML error page — raises `REFERENCE_UNSUITABLE` rather than silently accepting a bad file; SVG rasterisation via the same Playwright screenshot approach already used for PDF generation elsewhere in this repo, not a new library). `tools/visual-production-studio/visual-proof/` (`run-proof.ts` orchestration CLI, `prompt-builder-gemini.ts`, `derivative.ts`, `generate-proof-review.ts` review-pack generator — same Playwright `page.pdf()` approach as the existing final-review generator).

**4. The 2-asset proof, run for real against the live Gemini API.**

- **`unit202.magnet.field`** (bar magnet + external field pattern). Reference: Wikimedia Commons `DipolMagnet.svg` (public domain), downloaded and SHA-256-verified. Attempt 1: **PASS** on first generation — topologically faithful concentric field-line geometry, self-consistent circulation, governed-style-compliant background, no baked labels. 1 Gemini call.
- **`unit202.levers.class-1`** (Class I lever arrangement). Reference: Wikimedia Commons `Lever_(PSF).svg` (public domain — the originally-planned `unit202.pulleys.fixed` reference was inspected and found genuinely `REFERENCE_UNSUITABLE`, a compound block-and-tackle system contradicting that asset's own prohibited-changes list, so the proof used a different already-READY asset instead of fabricating a replacement reference). Attempt 1: **RETRY** — Gemini reproduced the reference's entire 3-diagram composite (one of the extra schematics shows the fulcrum at the far end, a Class II/III arrangement) and baked every text label onto the image. A bounded correction attempt (attempt 2, with an explicit strengthened instruction) was run: same two defects persisted. Per the task brief's own "do not burn tokens or money trying endless fixes" instruction, this asset is recorded as **HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED** rather than retried a third time — its 1-initial-+-1-correction budget is exhausted. 2 Gemini calls.
- **Total: 3 of the 4 maximum permitted Gemini calls used.** Every immutable fact and prohibited change was checked fact-by-fact (PASS/FAIL/UNCERTAIN) against the actual generated pixels, not against labels, per the brief's own audit standard.

**5. Human-readable review pack.** `reports/instructional-visuals/unit202-visual-production-proof-review.{pdf,json}` (`npm run visuals:proof:review`) — one page per asset, reference image and finished generated image side by side, audit verdict and fact-level findings, retry count, master/derivative paths. Visually inspected page-by-page via a rendered screenshot pass: confirmed legible, both images present, accurate summary counts. **This is the document the Product Owner sends to ChatGPT for independent review before any wider automated production run** — it is explicitly not a bulk-production approval, and does not constitute Product Owner sign-off of either candidate image.

Project dashboard (`npm run project:dashboard`, `http://127.0.0.1:4757/`).** A local, zero-new-server-dependency `node:http` tool (same architecture as the Visual Production Studio) with two tabs: **Roadmap**, rendered live from the version-controlled `tools/project-dashboard/roadmap-data.ts` (Phase 0 → Phase 2, all WP1.1–WP1.11, all CC-00 → CC-16 stages, with real status/dependencies/gates/evidence transcribed from `docs/roadmap/ROADMAP.md` and this file — CC-11.8 marked as the current `IN_PROGRESS` position, CC-12 explicitly `NOT_STARTED`); and **Platform Flow**, rendered live from `tools/project-dashboard/platform-flow.mmd` (Mermaid, loaded client-side from a CDN), a full course-factory + learner-platform architectural/process map (course selection → research → ingestion → knowledge model → lesson creation → visual determination → image research → visual production detail (contract → route → reference acquisition → Gemini redraw → deterministic overlays → audit → retry/human-review → review pack → master/derivative) → assessment creation → content release → learner engine (shared learner model, diagnostic loop, MAKE ME PASS / MAKE ME UNDERSTAND, spaced retrieval, misconception evidence) → analytics feeding back into course research/knowledge-model/visual-need). Both tabs visually verified rendering correctly via a real browser (Playwright screenshot pass) — including catching and fixing a real bug (Mermaid failing to lay out into a `display:none` tab panel at page load; fixed by rendering the diagram lazily on first tab activation). **Architectural rule, recorded directly in both source files' own header comments: material roadmap or platform-flow changes must update the relevant data source; the dashboard never hardcodes prose of its own.**

## CC-11.9 — External Reference Handover Implementation, White/Light Style Correction, Four-Asset Pilot & Full Unit 202 Production

Implements a Product Owner-supplied external reference-research handover into the production pipeline, corrects the visual style default, runs the required four-asset pilot, and -- following a corrected pilot verdict -- completes the full Unit 202 generative production run. **Full production is generated and awaiting Product Owner / ChatGPT visual review; nothing has entered the shipped asset tree or the approved-artwork manifest.**

**1. Handover ingested and cross-checked.** Stored verbatim at `reports/instructional-visuals/reference-research/unit202/` (README, summary, and the full 53-asset `unit202-reference-implementation-checklist.json` -- the actual per-asset authority this package consumed; the separately-supplied `.csv`/`.md` per-asset renderings were not additionally re-stored byte-for-byte, being fully redundant with the checklist JSON already preserved). Cross-check: **53/53 assetIds matched** against the live catalogue, zero unknown, zero omitted; research-decision distribution matched exactly (21 APPROVED_PRODUCTION_REFERENCE / 19 APPROVED_WITH_PREPARATION / 10 DETERMINISTIC_INTERNAL_AUTHORITY / 3 CONTRACT_CORRECTION_REQUIRED).

**2. Catalogue corrected via an additive overlay, not an in-place rewrite.** An automated text-block rewrite of `catalogue.ts` was attempted first and found unsafe: several asset families (`unit202.motor.effect.*`, `unit202.generator.*`, `unit202.magnet.poles.*`, `unit202.gears.*`, `unit202.components.physical.*`, `unit202.diode.bias-direction.*`) are generated programmatically from shared loop/template data tables, not one hand-written block per assetId, so there is no unique per-asset text region to safely replace. Reverted cleanly (git) before any corruption, then rebuilt as `tools/visual-production-studio/reference-corrections.ts`: a `REFERENCE_CORRECTIONS` map keyed by assetId, with `effectivePrimaryReference()` / `effectiveSecondaryReference()` / `effectiveReferenceReadiness()` accessors every downstream consumer must call instead of reading `asset.primaryReference` directly. Verified: **42/42 generative assets now resolve to a real reference** (0 missing) via this overlay.

**3. Reference preparation tooling built.** `tools/visual-production-studio/reference-preparation.ts`: `cropReference()` (fractional-bounding-box crop via a Playwright screenshot, same technique as the existing SVG rasteriser), `composeReferenceSheet()` (plain side-by-side composition with labels/caption, deliberately boring), `extractPdfPage()` (via the system `pdftoppm`/Poppler binary already installed on this machine -- not a new npm dependency). Used for real: cropped the three lever-class subfigures out of the composite `Lever_(PSF).svg` (Class I crop directly verified by visual inspection -- clean isolation, no ghost siblings); located the correct DOE AC-generator topology figure by running `pdftotext` against the actual downloaded 127-page PDF and searching for "slip ring" (found on page 21, "Figure 1 Simple AC Generator" -- exactly the elementary one-loop/two-slip-ring topology this asset needs), extracted it via `pdftoppm`, and cropped to just the diagram box.

**4. Reference Gate.** `tools/visual-production-studio/generate-reference-readiness-final.ts` -- `reports/instructional-visuals/unit202-reference-readiness-final.{json,md}`. As of this package: 0 missing references, 0 unresolved placeholders, 5 composites requiring preparation before use (the 3 lever-class crops -- prepared; `unit202.components.physical.thyristor-scr` and `unit202.emf.motional` -- not yet prepared, out of pilot scope). **Real reference-file acquisition: 17/42 generative assets' actual bytes downloaded and hashed** at the time this package concluded (not a false 42/42) -- the remaining 25 hit a live Wikimedia Commons rate limit (HTTP 429) triggered by the acquisition burst; a polite `User-Agent` header and inter-request delay were added, and a bounded background retry ran for several minutes but the rate limit had not fully cleared by the time this package's budget was reached (it did ease enough for 2 further assets to succeed on a final direct check). This is an external, transient condition, not a defect in the acquisition code (canonicalising Commons "File:" page URLs to the `Special:FilePath` raw-file endpoint, added this package, is confirmed working -- every successful download used it).

**5. Style corrected to white/near-white.** `docs/design/ALP-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-STYLE-GUIDE.md` §2 rewritten (new canonical values `#FBFBFA` -> `#F0F1F3`, the dark-slate default retained only as a historical record and a narrow per-asset override for an inherently dark device screen). Both active prompt builders updated (`prompt-builder.ts`'s `DEFAULT_BACKGROUND_INSTRUCTION`; `prompt-builder-gemini.ts` reads the style guide live, so it inherited the change automatically). §8 added recording the reusable-canonical-visual-asset model (see item 8 below).

**6. Required technical semantics corrected to be part of acceptance.** `prompt-builder-gemini.ts`'s clean-base-art default reversed: an asset's `requiredLabels` are now stated explicitly and required to be present *and correct* in the generated image, not deferred to "add it later as an overlay" (`ADR-0004`'s Consequences section updated to record this correction).

**7. Prior `unit202.magnet.field` PASS reclassified REJECTED.** Independent re-audit of the actual CC-11.8 master image (not merely restated on the Product Owner's authority) confirmed: no N/S label of any kind present; the arc-loop arrows and the horizontal-ray arrows imply *opposite* pole assignments -- a genuine, confirmable self-contradiction. Recorded in `unit202.magnet.field-audit-v1.json`'s new `verdictHistory` field; the original master file is retained on disk as failed evidence only.

**8. Reusable canonical visual assets (mid-task amendment, minimal implementation).** Recorded as a data-model intent, not built as a new subsystem: a commissioning course is not a generated visual's permanent owner; conceptually `CanonicalVisualAsset -> approved master/state/version -> UsageBinding(s)`, technical approval reusable across compatible future bindings, pedagogical suitability re-checked per binding. See style guide §8 and pipeline doc §21. No existing assetId was renamed; no cross-course asset-management subsystem was built.

Reference acquisition and preparation completed; Hard Reference Gate green.** The remaining generative assets' real reference bytes were acquired once the Wikimedia rate limit eased (via the thumbnail-fallback fix plus patient retrying, `npm run visuals:acquire-all`), and all known composites were prepared (`npm run visuals:reference-gate`). Final gate result: **GENERATIVE ASSETS READY: 42/42, MISSING REFERENCES: 0, UNRESOLVED PLACEHOLDERS: 0, UNPREPARED COMPOSITES: 0 -- Gate: PASS.** Report: `reports/instructional-visuals/unit202-reference-readiness-final.{json,md}`.

**10. `unit202.magnet.field` corrected: the earlier HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED verdict was itself a misread, not a real defect.** The Product Owner queried the v3 audit's conclusion that the S-side horizontal field-line arrowhead pointed outward (away from the magnet). Rather than accept the correction on authority, it was independently re-verified with two fresh, tightly-cropped zoom screenshots of just the left (S-side) and right (N-side) arrowheads: on direct pixel inspection, the left arrow genuinely points rightward/inward, toward S -- the original v3 audit had misread the triangle's orientation. `audit-v3.json` was updated with a `correctionNote`/`supersededBy` field documenting this as an independently re-verified correction, not a rubber-stamp of the Product Owner's say-so. The only real remaining defect was cosmetic: faint ghosted/phantom duplicate field-curve lines. A bounded cleanup pass (v4) removed only those, preserving every intended line, arrowhead, label and the white-style background unchanged -- confirmed **PASS**.

**11. Four-asset pilot -- corrected result: 4/4 PASS.**

- **`unit202.magnet.field`**: v4 (cleanup pass) -- **PASS**. N/S labels correct, field-line pattern complete, all arrow directions confirmed correct on independent re-verification (see item 10), phantom lines removed, white background.

Corrected pilot review pack regenerated: `reports/instructional-visuals/unit202-four-asset-pilot-review.{pdf,json}`, visually verified via a real Playwright screenshot showing `{attempted:4, pass:4, retry:0, humanReviewRequired:0, notFound:0, allPass:true}` and the "ALL FOUR PASS -- AUTOMATIC GO INTO FULL PRODUCTION" banner.

**12. Pilot verdict: GO. Full Unit 202 production run completed automatically**, per the task brief's own rule that a clean pilot PASS proceeds directly into bulk generation without a further confirmation prompt. All 42 generative assets in the governed catalogue (REQUIRED + USEFUL; no `DEFERRED_SCOPE` asset activated; no `DETERMINISTIC_TECHNICAL` asset sent to Gemini) were processed: prepared/approved reference -> generation -> audit -> bounded retry (max 1 correction) -> master + derivative + metadata.

## CC-11.10 — Bounded Remediation of the Unit 202 Visual Production Package

A targeted correction pass fixing specific issues an external (Product Owner / ChatGPT) review found in the CC-11.9 production package. Explicitly bounded: no re-opened architecture, no redone reference research, no re-audit of the curriculum, no blind full-catalogue re-run.

**1. Direction-sensitive assets: missing state-specific outputs generated.** Three assets had only ever produced their shared direction-neutral base image, never the direction-specific final learner-visible states their own `canonicalStates` already require: `unit202.current-conductor.magnetic-field` (4 states), `unit202.motor.effect.horizontal-poles` (4 states), `unit202.motor.effect.vertical-poles` (4 states) -- 12 states total, all now generated. The handover's own `reference-corrections.ts` overlay had already recorded this exact correction (`contractCorrections: ["...commission state-specific final imagery so the visible arrows/current marker are correct in the image itself"]`) but it had never been executed. `run-production.ts` was minimally extended with a `--state <stateId>` flag (`RunProductionOptions.stateId`, `forceCleanBaseArtOverride`) that generates one specific `canonicalStates` entry's final output instead of the shared base, writing files under the owning asset's directory named by the full `stateId`; `prompt-builder-gemini.ts` gained an additive `stateRequirement` prompt section. Direction per state was independently re-derived from F = BIL / Fleming's-left-hand-rule and the right-hand grip rule (not copied from the catalogue's own internal `force`/`field_rotation` bookkeeping fields, which are reconciliation-hash inputs only, not prompt-driving data) and cross-checked against the acquired external reference image. For `unit202.motor.effect.horizontal-poles`, a new `rotateReference()` helper was added to `reference-preparation.ts` (same Playwright-screenshot technique as the existing `cropReference()`) to rigidly rotate the generic Lorentz-force reference 90° for verification purposes. Each of the 3 assets' own already-PASS-audited base master was reused as the technical reference for its state generations (consistent geometry, lower risk than re-deriving from the raw external source each time). Result: **11/12 states passed on first attempt; 1 needed one bounded correction** (`unit202.current-conductor.magnetic-field.state.into-page-teaching`: attempt 1 used a 3D receding-perspective composition and produced one contradictory arrowhead among four checked, caught via two independent zoomed crops of the ring's left/right edges before concluding it was a real defect; attempt 2 switched to a flat, unambiguous 2D cross-sectional composition -- a permitted compositional freedom, not an immutable-fact change -- and passed with all four cardinal arrowheads independently verified). All 4 assessment-state images correctly show the given directional stimulus while withholding the assessed-answer indicator (no force arrow / no circulation arrowheads).

**2. `unit202.magnet.permanent-vs-electromagnet` -- unmistakable power source added.** The prior image's wire loop connected to an ambiguous striped/banded component that read as a resistor, not a battery -- the original CC-11.9 audit had incorrectly PASSed this as an acceptable "source/component" (documented transparently in the new `-audit-v2.json`'s `correctionNote`, with the v1 audit retained unmodified as the record of that miss). Corrected via one generation call using the existing master as reference: the ambiguous component is replaced with a standard, unmistakable two-line battery/cell symbol with a switch, verified via a zoomed crop of the source area. Permanent-magnet panel, field lines and labels unchanged.

**3. `unit202.electrolysis` -- regression to raw-reference instrumentation reversed.** Attempt 1 had reproduced the external reference's full textbook instrumentation (voltmeter, ammeter, and a redundant separate bench power-supply box) with no ion movement at all -- not the intended simplified teaching model. Corrected via one generation call: voltmeter and ammeter verified absent, source reduced to a simple two-line battery/cell symbol with +/- terminals, and ion movement added as circled-plus (cations) and circled-minus (anions) symbols correctly migrating toward the cathode and anode respectively -- cross-checked as physically correct for an electrolytic cell (cathode wired to the battery's negative terminal, matching the direction of ion migration shown).

**4. `unit202.levers.class-2` -- duplicate FULCRUM label: still unresolved, correctly left HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED.** Two further correction attempts were made this package. Attempt 3 omitted the prepared Class-II-only reference crop and fell back to the raw multi-class composite reference, regressing badly (reproduced all three lever classes in a flat black-and-white icon style, nothing like the established premium style) -- caught immediately and not treated as a real candidate. Attempt 4 used the correct prepared reference and restored the correct premium style and geometry, but the FULCRUM-label duplication was not fixed and in fact worsened to a triplicate (three instances of the word, plus a stray extra triangle icon). Per this package's own bounded-retry discipline (no endless stylistic-variant attempts), this was not retried a third time. The underlying lever geometry has been correct since attempt 2 -- only the automated text-label placement is unreliable for this specific asset across four attempts total. Recommended follow-up (not actioned here, out of this package's bounded scope): a deterministic text-overlay fix rather than a further generative attempt.

**5. Low-risk optional cleanups.** `unit202.magnet.poles.unlike`: the unnecessary crossed-out "repel" icon was removed in one clean correction call, physics and all other content unchanged -- PASS. `unit202.levers.class-3`: assessed and **skipped** -- the existing image mixes a real excavator-bucket photo composite with abstract lever iconography in a way that would need a genuine compositional redesign to match the Class I/II family's simple abstract style, not a quick bounded cleanup; left for a future package per this task's own "do not block the package" instruction for optional items.

**6. Review-package stale-reference display bug fixed.** `generate-production-review.ts` and `generate-pilot-review.ts` both rendered `asset.primaryReference.sourceName` -- the raw catalogue field -- for the "REFERENCE (as sent to Gemini)" caption, which still reads `"PRIMARY REFERENCE STILL TO BE APPROVED"` for any asset whose real reference was supplied only through the `reference-corrections.ts` overlay (e.g. both motor-effect assets). Fixed to call `effectivePrimaryReference(asset)` instead. While regenerating, the production review was also extended to render one dedicated page per generated `canonicalState` output (via a new `highestAuditedAttemptFor(ownerAssetId, outputId)` helper and `ProductionReviewEntry.stateEntries`) rather than only ever showing an asset's shared base -- the three direction-sensitive assets now show all 4 of their state pages each, and the summary counts (`summaryCounts`) flatten state entries into the tally so the reported totals reflect actual required learner-visible outputs, not assetId count. Both packages regenerated and visually verified via real Playwright screenshots (cover page counts, and one state-specific page confirmed showing the corrected reference caption).

**7. Net production tally after this package.** 51 generative outputs attempted (39 assets with a single shared output + 12 individual canonicalState outputs across the 3 direction-sensitive assets), **50 PASS, 1 HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED** (`unit202.levers.class-2`, unchanged), **66 total Gemini calls** (51 carried over from CC-11.9, +13 for the 12 new states -- one needed a correction, +2 for the electromagnet/electrolysis single-attempt corrections, +2 net for levers.class-2's two further attempts, +1 for the magnet.poles.unlike cleanup, and the 3 direction-sensitive assets' old single-base tallies replaced by their new 4-state tallies). Hard reference gate unaffected (all corrections reused already-acquired/approved references) -- still 42/42 READY.

**8. Validation.** `npx tsc --noEmit -p tools/visual-production-studio/tsconfig.json` clean after every code change (`run-production.ts`, `prompt-builder-gemini.ts`, `reference-preparation.ts`, `generate-production-review.ts`, `generate-pilot-review.ts`). No new automated test suite -- real generated visual evidence (16 new/corrected images) plus honest per-image audits, consistent with this whole package's own established practice. Full monorepo test suite not re-run (no learner-runtime/DB/mobile code touched).

**9. Status unchanged: FULL PRODUCTION GENERATED — AWAITING PRODUCT OWNER / CHATGPT VISUAL REVIEW.** Not called "approved" -- `unit202.levers.class-2` remains HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED and nothing has moved into the shipped `apps/mobile` asset tree or the approval manifest.

**Next package**: Product Owner / ChatGPT review of the regenerated `unit202-visual-production-review.pdf`; deterministic-overlay resolution of `unit202.levers.class-2`'s label duplication; `unit202.levers.class-3`'s deferred compositional cleanup. **CC-12 not started, per explicit instruction.**

## CC-11.11 — Complete Unit 202 Canonical Visual-State Suite

The final Unit 202 visual-completeness/reconciliation pass. Explicitly not a re-run: no architecture change, no redone reference research, no blind regeneration of already-good imagery.

**1. Live catalogue mechanically re-counted, not assumed.** `allAssets()` over the live `catalogue.ts`: **53 ProductionAssets, 98 canonicalStates** -- matches the historical figure exactly, confirmed programmatically rather than trusted from memory. Breakdown by productionClass: HYBRID 26 assets/41 states, DETERMINISTIC_TECHNICAL 11 assets/41 states, PREMIUM_CONCEPTUAL 16 assets/16 states.

**2. State-level completeness matrix built.** New `tools/visual-production-studio/generate-final-state-completeness.ts` classifies every one of the 98 states into exactly one `ResolutionType` (GENERATED / DETERMINISTIC / REUSED_CANONICAL / SHARED_BASE_VALID / DEFERRED_SCOPE / UNRESOLVED), never conflating ProductionAsset count with CanonicalState count with generated-file count. Output: `reports/instructional-visuals/unit202-final-state-completeness.{json,md}`. Result:

| resolutionType | count |
|---|---|
| GENERATED | 47 |
| DETERMINISTIC | 42 |
| SHARED_BASE_VALID | 8 |
| DEFERRED_SCOPE | 1 |
| REUSED_CANONICAL | 0 |
| **UNRESOLVED** | **0** |

The hard completeness gate (UNRESOLVED states of REQUIRED need must equal 0) **passes**. The 1 DEFERRED_SCOPE state (`unit202.trigonometry.state.teaching`) is governed as deferred by `catalogue.ts`'s own `visualNeedClassificationFor()` -- no lesson exists yet to host it.

**3. Three real deterministic-renderer gaps closed.** Mechanical cross-check against `apps/mobile/src/components/diagrams/DiagramRenderer.tsx`'s registry found three `DETERMINISTIC_TECHNICAL` catalogue assets with a governed production class but literally no renderer registered anywhere: `unit202.rectification.waveforms` (half-wave/full-wave/inverter, 3 states), `unit202.capacitor.transient` (charge/discharge, 2 states), `unit202.current-direction.electron-flow-vs-conventional` (1 state, USEFUL not REQUIRED). Investigated whether the underlying `DiagramBlueprint`/lesson wiring already existed (it did not -- both rectifier and capacitor questions are real and live in the governed corpus, but neither has a diagram attached: the rectifier question has none at all, the capacitor question points at the generic static component-symbol card as a deliberate stand-in). Built three new components (`RectificationWaveformDiagram.tsx`, `CapacitorTransientDiagram.tsx`, `ElectronFlowVsConventionalDiagram.tsx`), each enforcing its governed immutable facts structurally (e.g. rectification never draws a smooth sine for any of the three shapes; the capacitor curve is genuinely exponential, never a ramp or step; the two current-direction arrows are always opposite, always on the same wire). Registered under three new blueprint ids in `DiagramRenderer.tsx`; `DiagramRenderer.test.tsx` extended (11/11 passing, up from 8/8) including exact-match assertions for the new `SUPPORTED_DIAGRAM_BLUEPRINT_IDS` list (19, up from 16). **Deliberately not wired into any lesson step's `representation.diagramBlueprintId`** -- doing so would mean editing governed lesson-content data (`scripts/content/data/lesson-electronic-components-passive.ts`), which is curriculum/content-mapping work this task brief explicitly excludes. The renderers are real, tested and production-ready; the content-layer wiring is left for the next LESSON/ACTIVITY INTEGRATION workstream.
