## CC-11.13 — Visual Production Pipeline Hardening (Post-Unit-202 Correction Package)

An architecture/tooling/governance hardening package. Explicitly not another Unit 202 image-production run -- no bulk regeneration, no reopened curriculum mapping, no lesson/activity integration.

**1. Product Owner decisions encoded as permanent governance (all future modules/courses/qualifications, not Unit-202-only).** (a) Simply vectorising/tracing a reference is not an acceptable final production method -- a technically suitable reference is used as the technical authority and **redrawn as an original ALP-styled teaching diagram**, no artistic licence over the technical relationships it establishes. (b) Governed teaching diagrams default to **clean 2D/flat/diagrammatic** treatment, not 3D -- perspective/depth is opt-in only where it materially improves comprehension of a genuinely spatial relationship (the AC generator's loop/shaft/slip-ring topology is the one clear example so far). Recorded in `docs/design/ALP-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-STYLE-GUIDE.md` §10, `docs/architecture/PREMIUM-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-PRODUCTION-PIPELINE.md` §23, ADR-0004's Consequences, and `ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md`.

**2. Governed production-mode model.** New `tools/visual-production-studio/production-mode.ts`: `ProductionMode` = `DETERMINISTIC_TECHNICAL` | `ORIGINAL_REDRAW_FROM_REFERENCE` (the default for most teaching diagrams) | `HYBRID_REDRAW_PLUS_DETERMINISTIC_OVERLAY` | `GENERATIVE_CONCEPTUAL` | `DEFERRED_POLISH` (a status, not a creation method). `PRODUCTION_MODE` classifies all 53 live ProductionAssets (mechanically verified: 0 missing), keyed per-asset or per-state for the 3 direction-sensitive assets. Generation via an image model is recorded as a production *option*, never assumed mandatory. Also exports the platform-wide `NO_GENERATIVE_INFERENCE_RELATIONSHIPS` list -- right-hand-grip field rotation, Fleming correspondences, motor/generator current-field-force-motion, motional-EMF B/l/v geometry, lever-class ordering, circuit topology, diode polarity, electrolysis ion direction, IEC/switching symbol geometry -- relationships that must always come from deterministic geometry or a reference-constrained redraw, never generative invention, regardless of mode.

**3. Asset-lifecycle / gating model.** New `tools/visual-production-studio/asset-lifecycle.ts`: seven gates (`REFERENCE_APPROVED` → `SEMANTIC_COMPOSITION_APPROVED` → `TECHNICAL_MASTER_APPROVED` → `PEDAGOGICAL_MASTER_APPROVED` → `POLISH_PENDING` → `POLISHED` → `PRODUCT_OWNER_APPROVED`), explicitly separating technical correctness from pedagogical clarity from visual polish from real Product Owner sign-off. Key rule, enforced structurally by `isDevelopmentUsable()`: an asset may be development-usable while `POLISH_PENDING` (full polish is never required before internal product-development use) -- but an asset in `BLOCKING_CORRECTNESS` is never usable regardless of how aesthetically acceptable it looks, checked *before* any polish/approval state is consulted.

**4. PRESERVE / ADD / REMOVE / REPLACE remediation contract.** `asset-lifecycle.ts`'s `RemediationContract` (`{ preserve, add, remove, replace }`) is the schema every future remediation package's per-asset instruction should use, making preserve-lists first-class. Directly targets the named failure class: a correction package silently deleting a previously-approved element (a correct symbol, a correct label) when the actual instruction only asked for something else to be added or fixed elsewhere. `preserve` is never optional -- an empty list is a deliberate full-redraw choice, not an oversight.

**5. CC-11.12 audit mistake corrected.** `unit202.right-hand-grip.teaching` was marked PASS on all three CC-11.12 verdicts. Fresh, more specific Product Owner review identified the actual defect precisely: the field-circulation cue is in the wrong *plane* -- it must wrap around the copper conductor in the same geometric sense as the curled fingers, following the curl from finger-base toward fingertip, not float as a detached or differently-projected arrow. This is exactly the class of directional relationship `NO_GENERATIVE_INFERENCE_RELATIONSHIPS` now names explicitly. Reclassified `TECHNICAL_MASTER_APPROVED` / `BLOCKING_CORRECTNESS` in the lifecycle model -- not development-usable as a correct teaching asset until fixed. Recorded, per explicit instruction, as a **targeted future bounded fix**, not generated now: the recommended target production mode is `HYBRID_REDRAW_PLUS_DETERMINISTIC_OVERLAY` (keep the reliably-correct hand/thumb/conductor redraw as the base, add the wrap-around circulation cue as a deterministic overlay -- a governed curved-arrow primitive following the finger-curl geometry, never a freehand generative stroke).

**6. Class III lever reclassified.** `unit202.levers.class-3`'s CC-11.12 outcome (excavator contamination removed, correct geometry) is **not accepted as a successful improvement merely because it is technically interpretable** -- the redraw is pedagogically worse than the reference (2 extra inherited labels create confusion the reference itself does not have), and the one correction attempt regressed the beam geometry rather than fixing it. Reclassified `BLOCKING_CORRECTNESS` (pedagogical clarity failure, not a polish gap). Recommended future mode is unchanged (`ORIGINAL_REDRAW_FROM_REFERENCE`) -- the fix is a more carefully constrained redraw pass, not a different production method, and direct evidence that this mode should not require forced 3D flourish or continued retrying of a poor re-interpretation. Not generated now.

**7. Component-symbol family note recorded, not expanded.** The long-term platform rule (physical-recognition image + separate deterministic IEC/BS symbol companion) is documented; Unit 202 does not get further component-family time in this package. The failure mode to guard against -- a correction package deleting previously-useful symbol content when the real need was family-wide completion -- is exactly what item 4's `RemediationContract` now structurally prevents. A future governed industry-standard switch/component symbol SVG library (one-way switch, one-way two-pole switch, two-way two-pole switch, etc.) is recorded as a distinct future platform requirement, not built here.

**8. Unit 202 visual-debt register.** New `tools/visual-production-studio/generate-visual-debt-register.ts` → `reports/instructional-visuals/unit202-visual-debt-register.{json,md}`. Deliberately lean, not padded: **3 `BLOCKING_CORRECTNESS`** (`unit202.right-hand-grip.teaching`, `unit202.emf.motional` -- retained blocking pending one more confirmation pass on its CC-11.12-corrected board, `unit202.levers.class-3`), **1 `DEVELOPMENT_USABLE_POLISH_PENDING`** (`unit202.electrolysis` -- a real, specific, already-noted duplicated "current path" label, nothing more invented), **1 `DEFERRED_SCOPE`** (`unit202.components.physical.resistor`'s deterministic colour-band overlay, CC-11.12's own KEEP_WITH_ANNOTATION finding). The remaining 46 of 51 generative outputs carry three independent PASS verdicts each and no individually-identified debt -- not listed merely to look thorough.

**9. Documentation and dashboard.** Style guide §10, pipeline doc §23, ADR-0004, `ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md` updated (also fixed two stale CC-11.12-era doc references that named the wrong file for the semantic-QA overlay). `tools/project-dashboard/roadmap-data.ts` and `platform-flow.mmd` updated to show production-mode selection, technical/pedagogical approval, and polish/Product-Owner-approval as distinct visible stages -- see below.

**10. Validation.** `npx tsc --noEmit -p tools/visual-production-studio/tsconfig.json` clean. Targeted `vitest run tools/visual-production-studio tools/project-dashboard` re-run clean, no regressions. No image generation, so no pixel-level verification was needed this package -- verification effort went into confirming all 53 assets resolve a production mode and the platform-flow diagram still renders without error (real Playwright screenshot).

**11. No broad regeneration occurred.** Zero Gemini calls made in this package. Two specific known-blocking assets recorded as targeted future bounded-fix candidates (§5, §6 above), not generated now.

**Next package (recommended, small and bounded)**: ~~a single targeted fix pass covering exactly `unit202.right-hand-grip.teaching` ... and `unit202.levers.class-3` ...~~ **CORRECTED in §CC-11.13A below -- this recommendation accidentally omitted `unit202.emf.motional`; the real next bounded package is three assets, not two.** **CC-12 not started, per explicit instruction.**

## CC-11.13A — Correct Visual Pipeline State Records

A small metadata/governance correction to CC-11.13. No images generated or modified; CC-11.13's architecture (production-mode model, lifecycle gates, remediation contract, semantic-QA gate, 2D-diagrammatic default) is unchanged and accepted -- this package fixes four specific errors in CC-11.13's own output data and prose.

**1. Lifecycle-gate contradiction fixed.** CC-11.13 left `unit202.right-hand-grip.teaching` and `unit202.emf.motional` at the `TECHNICAL_MASTER_APPROVED` gate in `asset-lifecycle.ts` while simultaneously classifying both `BLOCKING_CORRECTNESS` in the same record -- self-contradictory, since `TECHNICAL_MASTER_APPROVED` specifically asserts a passed technical audit. Both corrected to `SEMANTIC_COMPOSITION_APPROVED` (the reference/prepared frame is locked and correct; the generated master built from it is not): `unit202.right-hand-grip.teaching`'s field-circulation cue does not reliably wrap the conductor in the correct plane across two generation attempts (CC-11.9, CC-11.12); `unit202.emf.motional`'s CC-11.12-corrected board places `l` along the wrong geometric dimension (see §2). Both remain `BLOCKING_CORRECTNESS` -- the blocking status was not weakened to fit the lifecycle; the lifecycle was corrected to fit the (already-correct) blocking status. `unit202.levers.class-3` reviewed for the same class of error and found honest as recorded: its `TECHNICAL_MASTER_APPROVED` gate is accurate (the technical verdict genuinely passed, excavator contamination removed, correct Class III geometry achieved) -- but it remains capped there, not advanced to `PEDAGOGICAL_MASTER_APPROVED` or any Product-Owner gate, since no Product Owner approval of any kind has been given and the pedagogical-clarity defect (2 extra inherited labels beyond the governed EFFORT/LOAD/FULCRUM set, one regressed correction attempt) is real and unresolved.

**2. Motional EMF restored to the next bounded correctness package, plus a new named geometry finding.** CC-11.13's own final-report recommendation named only `unit202.right-hand-grip.teaching` and `unit202.levers.class-3` as the next targeted fix pass, omitting `unit202.emf.motional` even though it was correctly recorded `BLOCKING_CORRECTNESS` in the same package's own data -- an inconsistency between the data and the prose. The recommended next bounded package is corrected to exactly three assets: `unit202.right-hand-grip.teaching`, `unit202.emf.motional`, `unit202.levers.class-3` (§CC-11.13 line struck through and pointed here). Product Owner review additionally identified a genuine technical defect in `unit202.emf.motional`'s CC-11.12-corrected board: the learner-visible diagram places `l` along the wrong geometric dimension. Governed relationship for the future fix, now also named explicitly in `production-mode.ts`'s `NO_GENERATIVE_INFERENCE_RELATIONSHIPS`: `l` = active conductor length; `v` (velocity) is perpendicular to `l`; `B` (field) is perpendicular to both `l` and `v`. All three relationships must be established deterministically/reference-first (a corrected hand-authored board via `renderTechnicalBoard()`) before any redraw -- never re-derived by the generator. CC-11.12's photoreal-3D-pipe clarity defect remains genuinely fixed (the board is correctly flat/2D); the `l`-dimension defect is a separate, newly-identified, still-open technical finding.

**3. Component-family decision corrected.** CC-11.12 removed `unit202.components.physical.capacitor`'s baked UK/IEC schematic symbol in an attempt to achieve family consistency with the other `components.physical.*` assets (which had no symbol). That was **not** the desired product decision. The correct long-term teaching grammar, now recorded in `asset-lifecycle.ts` and `production-mode.ts`: **PHYSICAL COMPONENT RECOGNITION IMAGE + DETERMINISTIC UK/IEC SYMBOL COMPANION**, for every component, capacitor included. The correct direction was to make the *other* component teaching states capable of showing their deterministic symbol companion consistently, not to remove the capacitor's already-useful symbol teaching. No image regenerated or modified -- `unit202.components.physical.capacitor` reclassified `DEFERRED_SCOPE` with a new `asset-lifecycle.ts` entry documenting the corrected rule for future course production; Unit 202 component-family cleanup (giving every `components.physical.*` asset a deterministic symbol companion, not a baked-in raster element) remains explicitly deferred, unchanged from CC-11.13's own scope decision.

**4. Global Unit 202 polish debt made visible.** CC-11.13's visual-debt register implied, by omission, that the 46 (now 45, after the capacitor entry above) generative outputs with no individually-recorded defect were therefore visually finished. They are not. New `UNIT202_GENERATIVE_SUITE_STATUS` constant in `asset-lifecycle.ts` (`GLOBAL_POLISH_PENDING`), rendered by `generate-visual-debt-register.ts` as a suite-level section ahead of the per-class breakdown in both `unit202-visual-debt-register.md` and `.json`: not a blocker for ongoing product development (these assets remain development-usable now); not Product Owner final visual approval (no asset in the suite has that yet, regardless of polish state); a controlled polish pass (sharpening/crispness, line quality, consistent label treatment, family consistency, removal of generative softness, final ALP style normalisation) is recorded as needed, to be addressed only after the `BLOCKING_CORRECTNESS` items above are closed. Deliberately one suite-level status, not 45 individually-padded debt records, per the register's own "use careful judgement, do not bloat the list" discipline.

**5. CC-11.13 preserved.** No change to the production-mode architecture, the original-redraw principle, the 2D-diagrammatic default, semantic reference QA, the no-generative-inference rules, the remediation contract, or the general seven-gate lifecycle architecture. This package is data/status correction only.

**6. Validation.** `npx tsc --noEmit -p tools/visual-production-studio/tsconfig.json` clean. Targeted `vitest run tools/visual-production-studio tools/project-dashboard` re-run clean, no regressions (no runtime logic changed, only overlay data). Confirmed directly: (a) all 53 assets still resolve a production mode via `productionModeFor()`; (b) the three next correctness-fix candidates (`unit202.right-hand-grip.teaching`, `unit202.emf.motional`, `unit202.levers.class-3`) are recorded `BLOCKING_CORRECTNESS` in `ASSET_LIFECYCLE`; (c) no known technically-incorrect asset is marked `TECHNICAL_MASTER_APPROVED` or later inappropriately -- right-hand-grip and emf.motional now cap at `SEMANTIC_COMPOSITION_APPROVED`, levers.class-3's `TECHNICAL_MASTER_APPROVED` is legitimately earned and does not advance further; (d) component-family documentation expresses physical-image-plus-deterministic-symbol-companion in `production-mode.ts`, `asset-lifecycle.ts`, and this section; (e) global Unit 202 polish debt is visible via `UNIT202_GENERATIVE_SUITE_STATUS`; (f) zero image-generation calls occurred -- no `run-production.ts` invocation, no Gemini calls, no new generated PNG/audit/metadata files in `git status`.

**Next**: the three-asset bounded correctness package (`unit202.right-hand-grip.teaching`, `unit202.emf.motional`, `unit202.levers.class-3`) is recorded and ready, but **not started in this package**, per explicit instruction. **CC-12 not started.**

## CC-11.14 — Three-Asset Correctness Proof (Bounded Validation of the Hardened Visual Pipeline)

Deliberately narrow: the CC-11.13/13A production-mode/lifecycle/semantic-QA/remediation-contract architecture is accepted and unchanged. This package proves the hardened pipeline can correctly repair exactly the three assets CC-11.13A named as the next bounded-fix candidates, without degrading them or triggering another broad production cycle.

**1. Remediation contracts authored before any generation call.** New `tools/visual-production-studio/cc-11.14-remediation-contracts.ts` (`CC_11_14_REMEDIATION_CONTRACTS`, using `asset-lifecycle.ts`'s own `RemediationContract` type) -- explicit PRESERVE/ADD/REMOVE/REPLACE for all three assets, written and reviewed before the first Gemini call, per the task brief's own §3/§8 requirement.

**2. Right-hand grip -- fixed via HYBRID_REDRAW_PLUS_DETERMINISTIC_OVERLAY, exactly as CC-11.13 recommended.** Root-caused the v2 defect by direct pixel inspection (not prose): two conflicting arrows -- a copper crescent with contradictory arrowheads at both ends, and a separate dark arrow near the wrist that read as a detached hand gesture rather than a field cue. Fix in two steps: (a) one Gemini self-referential cleanup call (master v3) -- v2 fed back as its own prepared reference with an explicit "remove both arrows, change nothing else" instruction, verified pixel-preserving the hand/thumb/conductor/both text labels; (b) a DETERMINISTIC wrap-around ellipse composited programmatically on top (master v4, zero Gemini calls) -- its geometry is not invented: the Product-Owner-approved Wikimedia `Right-hand_grip_rule.svg` reference's own loop path (`M85 338a68 28 23 1 0 -41-17`, sweep-flag=0) was extracted, and rigidly rotated -22° about its own current-arrow's start point (the angle that straightens that reference's tilted current arrow to vertical) -- preserving the reference's own counterclockwise-on-screen sense, which independently matches this asset's visible finger curl (base near the palm on the right, fingertips on the left). Verified against all 5 checks in the brief's §10 special-verification list (wraps the conductor; correct rotational plane; agrees with finger curl; progresses base-to-tip; cannot be read as a separate gesture) -- see `unit202.right-hand-grip.teaching-audit-v4.json`.

**3. Motional EMF -- fixed via a corrected prepared technical board, established before generation.** Root-caused v2's defect by pixel measurement: the `l` bracket was horizontal, spanning a rail-length distance at the bottom of the diagram, not the conductor's own length. Built a new hand-authored prepared board (`reference-cache/prepared/unit202.emf.motional.cc-11.14-corrected-l-dimension.prepared.png`) with `l` already drawn VERTICALLY, along the active conductor between the two rails, before Gemini saw it -- the governed B/l/v geometry was established deterministically first, exactly as the brief requires, never left to the generator to infer. One Gemini call (master v3), first attempt, restyled the board into the asset's existing premium illustration look without altering the geometry. Verified against all 6 checks in the brief's §11 special-verification list (l along the conductor; v⊥l; B⊥l; B⊥v; one conductor; no misleading extra dimension) -- see `unit202.emf.motional-audit-v3.json`.

**4. Class III lever -- fixed via a corrected prepared board, exactly matching the reference's own composition skeleton.** Root-caused v2's defect: the raw reference itself carries `RESISTANCE` and `MOTION` labels (a general lever-teaching aid, not scoped to the governed FULCRUM/EFFORT/LOAD set), and the prior redraws inherited both plus drifted into a photoreal 3D metallic beam/wedge/cube rendering the brief explicitly rules out for this asset. Built a new hand-authored prepared board (`reference-cache/prepared/unit202.levers.class-3.cc-11.14-corrected-labels.prepared.png`) -- the reference's own flat line-art composition with `RESISTANCE` removed, `MOTION` (and its arrow) removed, and the resistance glyph relabelled `LOAD`, preserving the governed Class III ordering (catalogue.ts's own immutableFact: "effort positioned between the fulcrum and the load point" -- verified by pixel measurement on the final candidate, no additional relative-distance requirement exists or was assumed). One Gemini call (master v4), first attempt, redrawn as clean flat 2D original ALP artwork -- no 3D, no decorative contamination, exactly three labels. Verified against all 6 checks in the brief's §12 special-verification list -- see `unit202.levers.class-3-audit-v4.json`. Noted, explicitly out-of-scope-for-this-package: Class I/II remain the pre-CC-11.13 soft-3D metallic-beam style; true cross-class family consistency is a separate future decision, not authorised here.

**5. Bounded retry discipline honoured.** All three assets succeeded on the first attempt within this package (3 Gemini calls total, one per asset) -- no correction attempt was needed for any of them, well within the brief's "initial attempt + one targeted correction" maximum. No asset was retried blindly; none required a second call.

**6. Audits performed by direct pixel inspection, not self-reported prose.** Each of the three new audit JSONs (`*-audit-v4.json` / `*-audit-v3.json`) records TECHNICAL / PEDAGOGICAL_CLARITY / VISUAL_PRODUCT_QUALITY verdicts derived from measuring the actual candidate image (finger-curl x-positions, l-bracket orientation, FULCRUM/EFFORT/LOAD x-positions) -- deliberately not repeating the CC-11.12 mistake (right-hand-grip's own v2 audit) of trusting a plausible-sounding self-reported PASS.

JSON stays lightweight (~8 KB, paths/verdicts only, no embedded image data). Visually verified via real rendered pages (Playwright/`pdftoppm`).

## CC-12 — Unit 202 Learner-Facing Vertical Slice (Magnetism / Effects of Current)

The visual-production correctness phase (CC-11.13/13A/14) is closed for development purposes and not reopened here. This package proves the governed knowledge, visuals, questions, misconceptions, feedback and learner-state machinery can operate together as a real learner-facing adaptive experience — the first genuine end-to-end product vertical slice, not another isolated infrastructure package.

**1. Content scope: reused, not remapped.** Investigated the live repository first (four parallel research passes covering knowledge/assertions, lesson/question-generation, mastery/evidence, and mobile UI/visual-registry architecture) before writing any code. Found that `lesson.magnetism.effects-of-current` — already fully governed and bundled since CC-11 — covers all four requested concepts directly in its first seven steps (`concept_field_from_current`/`guided_interpret_field_direction` for the field + right-hand grip rule; `concept_force_on_conductor`/`worked_force_on_conductor`/`guided_calculate_force_on_conductor`/`guided_interpret_force_direction` for the motor effect + Fleming's left-hand rule), then continues into electromagnetism naming and EMF/terminal-voltage content already governed for the same lesson. Used the lesson as-is rather than forking a smaller lesson under a new id (task's own "do not silently duplicate existing concepts under new IDs" instruction) — the four target concepts are the depth-of-integration focus; the lesson's own further content is pre-existing scope, not newly built.

**2. Two genuine pre-existing production defects found and fixed.** (a) `packages/diagnostic-engine/src/course-definitions.ts`'s `UNIT202_ADAPTIVE_VERTICAL.contentRelease` had been left at `release.unit202.v2` since CC-08A while the mobile app's bundled projection advanced to v7 (CC-11.3) — every lesson in the real bundled content is tagged `release.unit202.v7`, none `v2`, so `selectNextActivity`'s own release-scoped lesson resolution (`releaseScopedLessons`, CC-08A) would find zero matching lessons and `computeNextCourseActivity()` would throw `UnknownCourseActivityError` on the very first course-node lookup — the real, production Learn-hub orchestration card was silently broken for every real learner. No test caught this because `next-activity.ts` and the Learn hub screen (`learn/index.tsx`) had zero test coverage. Fixed by bumping the course's `contentRelease` to the new `release.unit202.v8` (see §5). (b) `apps/mobile/src/lib/lesson-content/generate-lesson-question.ts` — the real Lesson Player's own question-generation call site (`lesson-player.tsx`) — hardcoded `diagramBlueprints: []` on every call. Several real governed executors (`magnetism.interpretFieldDirection`/`interpretForceDirection`/`recogniseAttractionRepulsion`) call `requireDiagramBlueprint()`, which throws when the referenced diagram blueprint is absent from the generation context. This means any real learner answering `guided_interpret_field_direction`, `guided_interpret_force_direction` or `guided_recognise_attraction_repulsion` (both magnetism lessons, live since CC-11/CC-11.1) would have crashed the app. Found by this package's own tests (writing a real-content UI test for the new layered feedback panel reproduced the exact crash) and fixed forward: `generateLessonQuestion()` now accepts optional `diagramBlueprints`/`workedExampleBlueprints` params (default `[]`, preserving every other caller), and `lesson-player.tsx` now passes the resolved lookup's real values.

**3. Root-cause diagnostic chain (task §11) — genuinely built, not faked.** Added one new governed branch trigger, `"incorrect_answer"` (`packages/content-schema/src/lesson-plan.ts`'s `branchTriggerSchema`) — the honest "ambiguous wrong answer, no misconception positively identified" case, distinct from `misconception_detected` (which requires an actual matching route). `lesson-controller.ts`'s `resolveBranchDestination` now falls back to it whenever a wrong answer's own misconception check (if any) resolves to no route — backward-compatible (every existing lesson, declaring no `incorrect_answer` routes, behaves identically to before). Wired into `guided_interpret_force_direction`: an ambiguous wrong answer (its own misconceptionTarget is only `suggestive`-strength and deliberately has no `misconception_detected` route) now branches to a real new diagnostic step, `diagnose_force_direction_error`, using a new governed question blueprint `magnetism.diagnose_current_convention` (2-option discriminator, mirroring the existing `emf.distinguish_emf_terminal_voltage` pattern) that asks specifically whether the learner used conventional or electron-flow current for Fleming's rule. Wrong there is DIRECT evidence of `MIS-EL-ELECTRON-CURRENT-DIRECTION-CONFUSION-001` → routes to `remediation_current_convention` (re-teach + re-ask) → `recheck_force_direction`. Right there rules out that hypothesis by elimination and proceeds straight to the same recheck — the residual Fleming's-rule finger-assignment hypothesis is never asserted as confirmed; it is only offered as an explicitly-framed "commonly confused with" hint in the new layered feedback panel's Deeper layer, backed by a new governed misconception, `MIS-EL-FLEMING-FINGER-ASSIGNMENT-CONFUSION-001` (added to `cc04-unit202-electrical-science.ts`, conflicts with `EL-CONCEPT-FLEMING-LEFT-HAND-001`). `recheck_force_direction` reuses `magnetism.interpret_force_direction` under a fresh step id — the deterministic engine reseeds from `(instanceId, stepId)`, so it generates a genuinely different pole/current-direction combination in the large majority of cases, never a repeat of the original question (task's explicit "do not simply repeat the same question"). All three new steps are `conditional_remediation_only` — excluded from the default assembled sequence for a learner who never triggers the branch (mechanically proven, see §7).

**4. Layered (Quick/Explain/Deeper) feedback — demonstrated for the first time in a real learner screen.** New `apps/mobile/src/components/question/LayeredFeedbackPanel.tsx`: Quick = existing `FeedbackPanel` content unchanged (correct/incorrect, correct answer when revealable, marking detail); Explain = the real governed assertion statement(s) the step tests (`resolved.bodyStatements`, already resolved by `resolve-lesson-step.ts` — no new content authored, the same statement the step's own lead-in body text already showed, deliberately reused as an at-the-point-of-feedback reminder); Deeper = the governed misconception description (when the engine identified one) plus, only on the two diagnostic-chain steps where it is pedagogically warranted, the explicitly-framed `deeperNote`. Gated in `LessonStepView.tsx` by `step.presentation.progressiveReveal` — a schema field that has existed since CC-06D but had **zero consumers anywhere in the codebase** until now; every other lesson step's `progressiveReveal` remains `false`, so every other step on the platform renders the plain, byte-identical `FeedbackPanel` exactly as before. Set `true` on `guided_interpret_force_direction`, `diagnose_force_direction_error`, `remediation_current_convention`, `recheck_force_direction`.

**5. Content release / course wiring.** `lesson.magnetism.fundamentals` and `lesson.magnetism.effects-of-current`'s pre-CC-12 step content preserved byte-identical in a new `lesson-cc12-v7-historical-snapshot.ts` (mirroring `lesson-cc11-3-historical-snapshot.ts`'s established pattern) so `release.unit202.v7`'s own declared membership keeps resolving unchanged; the live, edited lesson objects now carry `contentRelease: "release.unit202.v8"`. New `RELEASE_UNIT202_V8` (`content-releases.ts`) carries the same 24-lesson membership as v7; `MOBILE_BUNDLED_RELEASE_ID` switched v7 → v8; `lessons.ts` re-addressed all 24 lessons for v8 via the same plain-field-override pattern every release since CC-08A has used. `UNIT202_ADAPTIVE_VERTICAL` gained a fourth node, `node.magnetism-effects-of-current` (sequence 4), and its `contentRelease` moved to v8 (§2a). `lesson.magnetism.fundamentals` gained `remediationEligibility: [{assertionFamilyId: "electrical.magnetism_and_electromagnetism", isDefaultRemediation: true}]` — a small, bounded fix (task §16) closing a real dead-end: without it, a genuinely WEAK/CONFLICTING evidence state on that family would have resolved to `prerequisite_unresolved` / a `BLOCKED` course decision with no lesson eligible to remediate it.

**6. Right-hand-grip mnemonic wording (task §7).** `EL-CONCEPT-FIELD-DIRECTION-RULE-001`'s governed statement refined (same fact, no relationship/id change) to name the RIGHT hand explicitly (not just "the right-hand rule" as a label) and "conventional current" explicitly (this exact family is where the electron-vs-conventional-current misconception is diagnosed) — now reads: "...grip the conductor with the RIGHT hand so the thumb points in the direction of conventional current flow -- the curled fingers then give the direction of the circular field around the conductor." No mechanical gate treats assertion-statement text as release-versioned (unlike lesson step content) — confirmed via a clean `content:pedagogy:check`/`lesson:validate:check` re-run.

**7. Dev-only debug visibility (task §18), generalised.** `dev-lesson-qa.tsx` was hardcoded to Ohm's Law only. Now offers a small lesson picker (Ohm's Law / magnetism), and a new card surfacing the real course-orchestration decision (`computeNextCourseActivity` — decision type, target lesson, reason, detail) that this screen previously had zero visibility into. Still `__DEV__`-gated, still never linked from learner-facing screens.

**8. Validation — real-content proof, not just synthetic.** `packages/content-schema/src/lesson-plan.ts`'s `resolveWithinSessionBranch` needed no change (the new `incorrect_answer` trigger is handled generically, matching whatever the caller passes). New scenario H in `scripts/content/prove-lesson-assembly.ts` (real content, `LESSON_MAGNETIC_EFFECTS_OF_CURRENT`) proves the full diagnostic chain end to end: the ambiguous answer's own misconception tag does NOT resolve a route; the `incorrect_answer` fallback DOES route to the real diagnostic step; a wrong diagnostic answer routes to remediation; a correct diagnostic answer (and a cleared remediation) both converge on the recheck step; all three new steps are excluded from the default pre-session sequence. New scenarios `ADVANCE-TO-MAGNETISM`/corrected `COMPLETE-SLICE` in `scripts/content/prove-course-orchestration.ts` (real content) prove the course genuinely advances into the new fourth node after the first three, and only reports `COMPLETE_SLICE` once the magnetism node's own real mastery gate (`cap.emf.recognise_emf_terminal_voltage`) is independently secured — 16/16 scenarios pass (up from 14). New `LayeredFeedbackPanel.test.tsx` (6 tests, synthetic component-level) plus new real-content tests in `LessonStepView.test.tsx` (4 tests: plain `FeedbackPanel` unaffected when `progressiveReveal` is false; a real governed Explain layer for the real force-direction step; the Deeper-layer `deeperNote` shown only on the ruled-out-hypothesis path, never on the confirmed-misconception path) — all against the real bundled `lesson.magnetism.effects-of-current` content, which is exactly what surfaced the diagram-blueprint production bug in §2b. New `lesson-controller.test.ts` tests (3, synthetic mechanism-level): an ambiguous wrong answer with no misconceptionIdentifier routes via `incorrect_answer`; a suggestive-only misconceptionIdentifier with no matching route still falls back (never treated as confirmed); a real matching `misconception_detected` route still wins over the fallback when both are declared on the same step. Five pre-existing tests updated for the new, mechanically-correct counts (114 blueprints, 140 lesson entries, 101 bundled blueprints, the 16-scenario course-orchestration list) — none loosened, all newly-derived from the live corpus. Full battery: `npm run typecheck` (whole repository) clean; `npm run lint` clean (4 pre-existing errors found in `tools/visual-production-studio/visual-proof/generate-{pilot,production}-review.ts`, confirmed via `git status`/`git log` to be unmodified by this package — a CC-11.11-era issue, not a regression, left untouched as out of scope); root `npx vitest run` **977/977**; mobile `npx jest` **347/347**; `content:pedagogy:check`, `lesson:validate:check` (140 lessons/1552 steps, all 19 gates zero, including `Unreachable conditional steps: 0` proving the three new conditional steps are genuinely reachable), `coverage:matrix:check` (0 structural defects, 6/6 LOs learner-ready, 23/23 ACs learner-ready, unchanged), `engine:prove:check` (114/114 supported), `engine:dimensions:check`, `content:check`/`content:mobile:check` (regenerated, current), `visuals:check:strict` (0 answer-leakage failures, unaffected — no diagram contract touched), `visuals:completeness:check`, `check:mobile-boundary` — all clean.

**9. Acceptance scenarios (task §19) — how each is actually proven.** **A (strong learner)**: `guided_interpret_force_direction` answered correctly advances directly with no diagnostic detour (proven structurally — the three new steps are `conditional_remediation_only`, absent from the default sequence, confirmed in scenario H). **B (single misconception)**: an ambiguous wrong answer → `incorrect_answer` fallback → `diagnose_force_direction_error` → wrong (current-convention) → `misconception_detected` → `remediation_current_convention` → cleared → `recheck_force_direction` (scenario H, all four transitions proven against real content); mastery-state change is the pre-existing, unmodified evidence engine (unaffected by this package). **C (ambiguous wrong answer, more than one plausible cause)**: exactly the chain above — the system explicitly does NOT claim which cause applies from the first wrong answer alone (§3); the diagnostic step is the genuine follow-up check task §11 requires. **D (repeated difficulty)**: `recheck_force_direction` holds position and offers deeper layered feedback (not a blind retry) if still wrong, using the platform's existing, unmodified hold-and-retry `correct_answer_required` semantics — no new looping infrastructure was needed or added. **E (visual safety)**: unchanged, pre-existing, still-enforced governance — `magnetic.field_conductor_direction`/`motor.force_field_current`'s `VisualSemanticContract`s (CC-05D) withhold the field-rotation/force-direction reveal until feedback in both modes; re-confirmed via a clean `visuals:check:strict` re-run (0 answer-leakage failures) rather than assumed.

**10a. CC-12A/B/C/D and the imagery-cleanup follow-up (implementation-complete, added retroactively — recorded here by CC-12E as this package's own status entry had not yet been recorded).** Four short, real-emulator-driven hardening packages followed CC-12 directly, each triggered by an actual Product Owner finding on-device (never a code-review-only defect), each traced live before being fixed, per this repository's own "trace first, don't guess" discipline:

- **CC-12A — real emulator product acceptance** (`722082b`): verified the CC-12 slice through the actual Android emulator rather than Dev QA proofs; found and fixed two real production bugs — `RotationAnswerInput` routing for clockwise/counterclockwise questions, and two `multiple_choice` blueprints missing `answerOptionLabels`, both of which crashed the real Lesson Player.
- **CC-12B — correct learner visual binding + assessment answer-leakage review** (`6af3f78`): wired CC-11's premium Gemini teaching masters into `DiagramRenderer.tsx` for **teaching-context** rendering only (new `context: "teaching" | "assessment"` prop, `CANONICAL_TEACHING_VISUALS` registry), keeping assessment on the existing SVG diagrams.
- **CC-12C — lock canonical visual resolution + fix calculation-integrity report** (`44c1d47`): found CC-12B had shipped a stale right-hand-grip image from a disconnected, never-audited manifest instead of the true audit-trail master; re-shipped the correct file and added a SHA-256 governance lock (`CANONICAL_TEACHING_VISUAL_LOCK`, later renamed `CANONICAL_ASSET_LOCK`). Diagnosed a reported force-calculation `l`/`I` inconsistency as a pure typography issue (uppercase I / lowercase l rendering near-identically), not a numeric defect — fixed representationally, no calculation logic touched.
- **CC-12D — production learner route consolidation** (`4191a56`): traced a Product Owner finding (ordinary navigation reaching an old static "Lesson" screen instead of the real adaptive Lesson Player) to `learn/index.tsx`'s topic cards linking to the retired `/learn/[family]` route; repointed all four topic cards to `/learn/lesson-player`; retired (never deleted) the legacy `[family]/index.tsx`/`practice.tsx` screens, confirmed unreachable from any production navigation path.
- **Unit 202 imagery cleanup** (`ac0e67b`, unnamed CC-number — a direct follow-up to CC-12B/C's own finding): the Product Owner reported the right-hand-grip **assessment** step still showed the old SVG hand illustration — traced to CC-12B's own deliberate teaching-only scope, now superseded. Objectively identified the true final Gemini master per asset family via the real audit trail (`reports/instructional-visuals/premium-artwork/proof/`), independently re-verified by SHA-256 (never by visual judgement); archived 13 files sourced from the same disconnected manifest CC-12C had already found once, to `apps/mobile/src/assets/instructional/obsolete-assets/unit202/`; extended `DiagramRenderer.tsx` with reveal-sensitive per-parameter-state assessment imagery (`CANONICAL_ASSESSMENT_VISUALS`) and context-agnostic per-parameter-state imagery (`CANONICAL_PARAMETER_VISUALS`) for gears/pulleys/levers/generator/bar-magnet poles; deliberately left `motor.force_field_current`'s `N_S_vertical` states and `magnetic.flux_field_lines` unwired after finding, respectively, a genuine physics self-contradiction in that asset family's own audit records (independently re-derived via F = I L × B) and a content-state mismatch — both flagged rather than guessed past. Live-verified on a cold-started emulator: the assessment step now shows the correct withheld-then-revealed Gemini imagery, never the old SVG. `CANONICAL_ASSET_LOCK` now pins 21 shipped canonical visuals.

**10b. Known limitations / debt, honestly recorded.** No dedicated "browse Unit 202 lessons directly" screen exists yet (a pre-existing CC-10-flagged gap, unaffected by this package) — the vertical slice is reached via the real adaptive Learn-hub card once a learner works through Ohm's Law/series/parallel, or directly via the dev QA screen's lesson picker for inspection. The layered feedback panel is wired into exactly the four steps this package's own diagnostic chain needs, not retrofitted onto every existing lesson step platform-wide — a deliberate scope boundary (task's own "do not spend this package on pixel-perfect visual polish" / priority-order guidance), not an oversight; the mechanism (`progressiveReveal`) is now real and reusable by any future step that opts in. `lesson.magnetism.fundamentals` (static attraction/repulsion, flux/flux-density) is not itself a course node — it is reachable only as prerequisite remediation for the magnetism family, matching how the existing foundational-mechanics lesson is reached for Ohm's Law/series/parallel, not a gap. No production analytics, gamification, or payments were touched — none were in scope.

**11. UNIT 202 LEARNER-FACING VERTICAL SLICE COMPLETE — real, governed, end-to-end adaptive loop proven, both mechanically and via real-content UI tests.** Not claimed as final commercial UX polish (task's own explicit priority order: correct flow > clear interaction > adaptive behaviour > reliable state > presentation) and not claimed as Product Owner-approved.

**Next package (recommended)**: a learner-facing "browse Unit 202 lessons directly" screen (the CC-10-flagged gap, now more valuable given a second real adaptive course node exists); extending the layered-feedback pattern to more lesson steps once its real-slice proof is reviewed; the deferred suite-wide instructional-visual polish pass (CC-11.13A's `UNIT202_GENERATIVE_SUITE_STATUS`) and the two remaining `DEFERRED_SCOPE` component-family items — none started here.

## CC-12E — Learning Evidence Architecture + Documentation Currency Audit

**Status:** COMPLETE. Documentation/architecture/governance only — **no learner-runtime code, schema, or content changed.**

Encodes several Product Owner decisions made after real-emulator review of the CC-12 vertical slice, and brings authoritative documentation back into currency. Full detail: [`LESSON-PLAYER-AND-LESSON-PLAN-ARCHITECTURE.md`](docs/architecture/LESSON-PLAYER-AND-LESSON-PLAN-ARCHITECTURE.md) §4/§12/§17/§18/§19; [`ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md`](docs/architecture/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md)'s new "Generated reports are not runtime authority" section; [`DECISION-LOG.md`](docs/governance/DECISION-LOG.md)'s 2026-08-28 entry; [`PRODUCT-PRINCIPLES.md`](docs/product/PRODUCT-PRINCIPLES.md) principles 30-32; [`DOCUMENTATION-CURRENCY-REGISTER.md`](docs/governance/DOCUMENTATION-CURRENCY-REGISTER.md) (new).

**Decisions encoded:** (1) scrolling-within-a-step is permitted whenever it serves teaching better than one viewport, provided hidden continuation is never ambiguous — a future floating discoverability arrow is the recorded (not yet built) fix; (2) eight distinct pedagogical interaction classes (teaching, formative check, diagnostic check, remediation, recheck, delayed retrieval, summative/unit assessment, exam practice/mock), each mapped to the real existing `lessonStepTypeSchema`/`answerRevealSchema` (`packages/content-schema`) rather than proposed as new mechanism; (3) answer-reveal policy is governed by evidential role, never by "inside a lesson vs. a dedicated assessment screen"; (4) lesson completion and mastery are explicitly distinct, with no mandatory per-lesson mini-assessment implied; (5) evidence-context principle recorded against the real `@alp/evidence-engine` schema (`StepOutcomeKind`, `CapabilityEvidenceCounts`) — found to already capture first-attempt/retry/scaffolding/post-success-failure distinctions; the smallest future schema gap (no dedicated `diagnostic_check`/`recheck` step-type values yet — both currently reuse `guided_interaction`) is named, not implemented; (6) "generated reports are not runtime authority" recorded as a general principle, directly motivated by the imagery-manifest incident this session's own CC-12 imagery-cleanup package fixed.

**No new ADR.** All six decisions extend an existing approved architecture document (ARCH-003, the Lesson Player & Lesson Plan Architecture) or the existing approved evidence/mastery architecture (WP1.3) rather than opening a new externally-anchored governance domain — consistent with this repository's own established threshold (compare the DECISION-LOG's own repeated "No new ADR: extends existing principle" pattern across CC-09B.1 through CC-09I).

**Documentation-currency audit:** Tier-1 authoritative docs (README, PROJECT-STATUS, ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW, PROJECT-CONSTITUTION, PRODUCT-PRINCIPLES, DECISION-LOG, LESSON-PLAYER-AND-LESSON-PLAN-ARCHITECTURE, START-HERE, ROADMAP) read directly; a Tier-2 targeted grep across every doc under `docs/` plus the three root operational files, for ten known-stale terms/claims (old release versions presented as current, the retired `/learn/[family]` route, `RightHandGripRuleDiagram` as current source of truth, the disconnected artwork manifest/stale canonical-visual-registry, "no scrolling"/"one viewport" hard-rule claims, question/assessment conflation, mandatory per-lesson exams, deprecated tooling, the old static lesson screen, the pre-rename `CANONICAL_TEACHING_VISUAL_LOCK` name), found **the documentation was already substantively clean**: every hit sits inside a dated changelog entry (`PROJECT-STATUS.md`) or a labelled evidence snapshot (`docs/architecture/evidence/`), correctly framed as historical, not asserted as current. The one real gap found and fixed: this file's own top summary block and §CC-12 area had not been updated since before CC-12A — now corrected (§CC-12/§10a above, header block). See the currency register for the full per-document classification.

**Validation:** documentation-only change; `npm run typecheck` unaffected (no schema touched — confirmed by inspection, not run, since no `.ts`/`.sql` file changed in this package); no image/content/lesson/emulator suite run (none of this package's acceptance criteria depend on them, per its own efficiency requirement).

Independent Project Architect review found CC-12E's own documentation-currency conclusion was **false-green**: its audit method (grep for ten predetermined stale terms) genuinely found none of those terms in Tier-1 documents, but missed semantically stale prose that used different wording for the same problem. Four confirmed Tier-1 contradictions, corrected:

1. **`LESSON-PLAYER-AND-LESSON-PLAN-ARCHITECTURE.md` (ARCH-003) header and §5/§7/§10/§15/§16** still told a fresh reader "the lesson player is not implemented... implementation has not begun... no lesson screens, components, database schema or migrations exist" — false after CC-06 through CC-12 real implementation. Corrected: the diagram now shows both clients as current (native mobile primary, web secondary), both consuming the same shared domain-engine packages; the original web-first *proving-slice sequencing rationale* is preserved as explicit history, not deleted.
3. **`PROJECT-STATUS.md`'s own "Mandatory future review trigger"** said "no visual has been through that workflow yet" — false after CC-11.8 through CC-11.14's real production runs and the CC-12 imagery-cleanup package's real integration of audited-current Gemini masters. ## CC-12F — Learner Interaction + Formative-Assessment Corrections

**Status:** COMPLETE. Real-emulator Product Owner findings on CC-12E's own recorded punch-list (items A-D), implemented and live-verified; item E (humanising/readability pass) explicitly deferred, not started, per this package's own brief.

Implements exactly the four bounded corrections CC-12E recorded as architecture-approved-but-not-built, following the already-decided architecture (ARCH-003 §4/§12/§17/§18/§19) without re-deciding any of it. No Lesson Player redesign, no imagery regeneration, no global visual polish, no humanising/editorial pass, no unit assessment/exam-practice mode, no Unit 202 content expansion.

**1. Files changed.**
- `apps/mobile/src/components/lesson/ScrollableLessonStep.tsx` (new) — the generic scroll-discoverability container, now used by every lesson step.
- `apps/mobile/src/components/lesson/ScrollableLessonStep.test.tsx` (new) — 11 tests covering the pure visibility geometry and rendered behaviour.
- `apps/mobile/src/app/(app)/learn/lesson-player.tsx` — wired to render every step through `ScrollableLessonStep` instead of a bare `ScrollView`.
- `scripts/content/data/cc05a-pedagogy-unit202.ts` — SI-unit leak removed from `ohms_law.match_variables_units`; `answerOptionLabels` rewritten for both `ohms_law.diagnose_wrong_operation` and `ohms_law.diagnose_rearrangement_error`.
- `apps/mobile/src/components/mnemonic/VirTriangle.tsx` — genuinely visible cover-and-reveal interaction, revised accessibility labels.
- `apps/mobile/src/components/mnemonic/VirTriangle.test.tsx` — rewritten for the new labels and interaction, plus a new mutual-exclusivity test.
- `apps/mobile/src/lib/lesson-content/generate-lesson-question.test.ts`, `apps/mobile/src/lib/lesson-content/answer-input-dispatch.test.tsx`, `apps/mobile/src/app/(app)/learn/__tests__/lesson-screen.test.tsx`, `apps/mobile/src/components/lesson/LessonStepView.test.tsx`, `apps/mobile/src/components/__snapshot-tests__/proving-visuals.snapshot.test.tsx` — updated for the new content/labels, plus new tests directly asserting the SI-unit and diagnostic-validity fixes against the real bundled content projection.
- `apps/mobile/src/lib/lesson-content/generated/mobile-content-projection.ts`, `supabase/seed-content/cc04-unit202-electrical-science.sql` — regenerated via the standard governed pipeline (`content:pedagogy:check`, `content:mobile:generate`, `content:generate`), not hand-edited.

**2. Scroll-affordance implementation.** `ScrollableLessonStep` derives visibility purely from live scroll geometry (`onContentSizeChange`/`onScroll`, an 8px overflow threshold) — no per-step "is scrollable" flag, so it stays correct automatically as feedback expansion/image load/font scaling changes a step's real height. Tapping the arrow scrolls by 80% of the viewport (not a blind jump to the bottom). Uses `<SafeAreaView>` (not the `useSafeAreaInsets` hook) so bottom clearance degrades gracefully under Jest exactly like the rest of this codebase's screens. **Real-emulator finding during this package's own RUN A:** the arrow's original 16px bottom clearance genuinely overlapped a full-width Continue button on the VIR-triangle step once its formula-reveal card expanded content — measured via exact `uiautomator` bounds (`arrow [912,2106][1038,2232]` vs `Continue [63,2182][1017,2298]`). Fixed by increasing bottom clearance to `minTouchTarget * 2` (88px); re-verified live after the fix with a fresh `uiautomator` measurement showing a clean 139px gap (`arrow [912,1917][1038,2043]` vs `Continue [63,2182][1017,2298]`) — zero overlap.

**3. SI-unit leak — before/after.** Before: the unanswered "Match each Ohm's-law variable to its correct SI unit" question rendered `presentation.promptLines: ["V = {V} V", "I = {I} A", "R = {R} Ω"]`, directly showing the mapping being tested. After: `presentation.promptLines: ["Match each quantity to its correct SI unit."]` (the field could not be removed entirely — `resolvePromptLines`'s `requirePresentation()` throws on `undefined`, and the schema's `promptLines` has a Zod `.min(1)`). Real-emulator-verified: unanswered state shows no V/I/R-to-unit mapping anywhere on screen; answering correctly shows normal "Correct / set match" evidence and a working Continue button.

**4. Diagnostic root cause/fix.** Root cause: both `ohms_law.diagnose_wrong_operation` and `ohms_law.diagnose_rearrangement_error` shared byte-identical `answerOptionLabels`, so a genuinely different shown error (wrong operator entirely, vs. correct operator with operands swapped) could be truthfully described by either option. Fixed by writing option labels that describe the objective, structural difference in the shown working (cross-checked against the real executor logic in `packages/calculation-engine/src/families/ohms-law.ts`): `diagnose_wrong_operation` now reads "Multiplied V and R instead of dividing V by R" (etc.); `diagnose_rearrangement_error` now reads "Divided, but the wrong way round -- I ÷ V instead of V ÷ I" (etc.). Real-emulator-verified against both live blueprints: `diagnose_wrong_operation`'s shown working (`I = V x R = 100 A`) has no division at all, making "multiplied instead of dividing" the sole defensible answer; `diagnose_rearrangement_error`'s shown working (`R = I / V = 0.027... Ω`) has genuine division with operands swapped, making "divided the wrong way round" the sole defensible answer. Both selections returned "Correct. enum match."

**5. VIR-triangle interaction.** `VirTriangle.tsx` already had tap-to-select logic before this package; the defect was purely presentational — the only visual feedback was a ~10px dot, easy to miss, which is why it read as non-interactive. Fixed by drawing a filled cover region with a "?" glyph over the selected letter (rendered last in the SVG so it sits on top), and rewriting each region's accessibility label to state the covered/revealed state explicitly (`"Cover current I"` → `"current (I) covered. Relationship revealed. Tap to uncover."`). Real-emulator-verified: tapped V, I, and R individually; each shows its correct formula (`V = I × R`, `I = V/R`, `R = V/I`), each visibly obscures only the selected letter, and only one region is covered at a time (selecting a new region correctly uncovers the previous one).

**6. Retry/recheck inspection (RUN E) — no code change needed.** Per the brief's explicit instruction not to assume a defect without checking: deliberately submitted a wrong answer to a `guided_calculation_current` question. Result: "Not quite / Have another look and try again" with a "Try again" button — **no answer reveal**, exactly matching the governed architecture (reveal is permitted only when a step legitimately ends the attempt and routes to remediation, never before a permitted retry). Backgrounding the app mid-retry and resuming via the Learn hub's "Continue: Ohm's Law" card correctly restored the exact same question with a cleared input (no leaked state). Retrying with the correct answer produced normal "Correct. within tolerance" evidence. **Conclusion: the retry/reveal architecture was already correct; nothing needed changing.**

**7. Additional defects found — none within the four authorized classes.** A bounded review of the reachable lesson (steps 4-12 across two full playthroughs, covering the SI-unit check, both diagnostic blueprints, a formula-rearrangement-selection question, a plausibility-judgement question, and two further calculation questions) found no further scroll/leak/diagnostic-ambiguity/VIR-triangle instances. One out-of-scope cosmetic observation noted but **not fixed** (outside the four authorized defect classes, and the brief explicitly warned against over-diagnosing): the `diagnose_rearrangement_error` blueprint's shown working displays an unrounded floating-point value (`0.02702702702702703 Ω`) rather than a formatted/rounded one.

**8. Emulator acceptance results.** All five required runs (A: scroll discoverability, including the live overlap-bug find-and-fix; B: SI-unit formative safety; C: both diagnostic blueprints; D: all three VIR-triangle regions; E: retry/reveal/resume inspection) completed live on the Android emulator via `uiautomator`-measured bounds (not visual estimation alone), per this repository's established "trace first, don't guess" discipline. See screenshots listed below.

**9. Screenshots/evidence** (scratchpad, not committed): `cc12f9.png` (SI-unit unanswered state, pre-verification), `cc12f_runB.png` (SI-unit correct feedback), `cc12f_runE1b.png`/`cc12f_runE3.png`/`cc12f_runE5.png`/`cc12f_runE6.png` (retry-then-correct sequence), `cc12f_runC1.png`/`cc12f_runC2.png` (both diagnostic blueprints), `cc12f_vir1.png`/`cc12f_vir_v2.png`/`cc12f_vir_i.png`/`cc12f_vir_scroll.png` (VIR-triangle interaction and the verified arrow/Continue gap).

**10. Tests/results.** `npm run typecheck` (full monorepo) and `npx jest --silent` (`apps/mobile`, 65/65 suites, 413/413 tests, 8/8 snapshots) both green after all code/content changes.

**11. PROJECT-STATUS update.** This section, plus the punch-list correction above marking items A-D resolved (pointer to this section) and item E still open.

**12. Commit/push:** `f1c8c8f`.

**Next package:** none recommended by this package. Per its own explicit instruction, this is the stopping point — the Product Owner will use the real emulator again before scoping further expansion. The one remaining open item from the original punch-list is **E (humanising/readability pass)**, still not started.

## CC-12G — Learner UX, Question Randomisation & Formula-Readability Corrections

**Status:** COMPLETE. Bounded corrective package continuing from CC-12F: answer-option randomisation, the Ohm's-law rearrangement-question wording/interaction mismatch, formula-notation (× not "times"; I-vs-length-symbol readability), page-direction (×/•) notation teaching, previous-step review navigation, and the necessity of the late "lesson complete" step, all live-verified on the Android emulator. A significant, unplanned product-architecture defect (prerequisite-blocked lessons with no route back) was found mid-package and fixed per explicit Product Owner direction; a mid-package Product Owner notation correction (plain capital "L", not a special glyph) was also applied and re-verified.

**1. Files changed (selected; full list in the commit diff).**
- `packages/calculation-engine/src/seed.ts` — `shuffleDeterministic<T>` (Fisher-Yates) and `createRngForDomain` (per-domain independently-seeded RNG derivation).
- `packages/calculation-engine/src/presentation.ts` — `resolveAnswerOptions` shuffles via the `"answerOptions"` domain when a generated instance is supplied; governed order preserved when omitted (never randomises semantically-ordered content).
- `apps/mobile/src/lib/lesson-content/answer-input-dispatch.tsx` — `formula_selection` (equation-labelled options, domain `"formulaOptions"`) and `multi_select` (row order + each row's own choices, independent domains) now shuffle deterministically per generated instance.
- `packages/calculation-engine/src/families/ohms-law.ts` — `selectRearrangement` now also supplies `target_variable_name`; `scripts/content/data/cc05a-pedagogy-unit202.ts`'s `ohms_law.select_rearrangement` prompt rewritten to "Which equation should you use to calculate {target_variable_name}?" with equation-labelled (not bare-variable) options, so the prompt and the interaction genuinely agree.
- `scripts/content/data/cc04-unit202-electrical-science.ts`, `cc05a-pedagogy-unit202.ts` — "times" → "×" across the reachable Ohm's-law and magnetism/effects-of-current slice; new governed assertion `EL-CONCEPT-PAGE-DIRECTION-NOTATION-001` (× = into the page, • = out of the page) taught once, pre-emptively, on `concept_field_from_current` — never on a graded/direction-assessed step.
- `packages/calculation-engine/src/families/magnetism.ts`, `families/emf.ts`, `cc05a-pedagogy-unit202.ts`, `cc04-unit202-electrical-science.ts` — the governed length symbol for `formula.force_on_conductor` and `formula.motional_emf` renamed from lowercase `"l"` to plain capital `"L"` throughout (executors' generated parameters, formula-family `variables`/`forms.expression.operands`, worked-example `knownVariables`/`teachingValues`, prompt-line givens, assertion statements, capability/knowledge-obligation descriptions) — see item 6.
- `apps/mobile/src/lib/formula-rendering/format-formula.ts`, `components/formula/{FormulaExpressionView,VariableKey,WorkedSubstitution}.tsx` — the `visualSymbolGlyph` special-Unicode-glyph mechanism (an earlier, abandoned approach to the I-vs-length-symbol readability fix) removed entirely; formula symbols render plain, matching the course's own notation.
- `apps/mobile/src/components/diagrams/MotionalEmfDiagram.tsx`, `components/diagrams/DiagramRenderer.tsx` — the live-SVG fallback diagram and the canonical-teaching-image accessibility text both updated to "L"; see item 6 for the one deliberately-untouched exception.
- `scripts/content/data/lesson-ohms-law.ts` — the in-sequence `exit_completion` step removed (15 steps, was 16); `exitSummary` rewritten in plain language. `scripts/content/validate-lesson-plan.ts` — the now-redundant `lessonsWithNoExitStep` gate removed.
- `apps/mobile/src/app/(app)/learn/lesson-player.tsx`, `components/lesson/LessonStepView.tsx` — previous/next review navigation (header "‹"/"›" controls, distinct from Exit; `reviewIndex` state; `readOnly` prop on `LessonStepView` that structurally prevents re-submission/duplicate evidence during review).
- `packages/learning-engine/src/types.ts`, `assembler.ts` — see item 5 (the prerequisite-architecture fix).
- `apps/mobile/src/app/(app)/learn/__tests__/lesson-player-prerequisite-advisory.test.tsx` (new) — regression coverage proving direct lesson access regardless of prerequisite completion.
- `apps/mobile/src/lib/lesson-content/generated/mobile-content-projection.ts`, `supabase/seed-content/cc04-unit202-electrical-science.sql`, `scripts/content/evidence/cc04-unit202-corpus-review.md` — regenerated via the standard governed pipeline throughout, not hand-edited.

**2. Randomisation.** Answer-option order for `multiple_choice`/`worked_error_classification` (via `resolveAnswerOptions`), `formula_selection`, and `multi_select` (rows and each row's own choices, independently) is now derived from a per-domain RNG seeded off the generated instance's own `DeterministicIdentity`. Proven by automated test: stable within one instance (same instance ID → same order across repeated resolutions), variable across independently-generated instances (distinct seeds → observed distinct orderings), and — because marking always operates on the option's underlying value, never its screen position — position-independent correctness is structural, not merely tested. Diagnostic-mapping validity (CC-12F's fix) is unaffected: diagnostic blueprints' `answerOptionLabels` describe the shown working, not a position.

**3. Ohm's-law rearrangement question.** Root cause: the prompt asked "which equation" while the interaction offered bare variable-name buttons (`V`, `I`, `R`) — a genuine prompt/interaction mismatch. Fixed by rewriting the prompt to name the actual target variable and rendering each option as its full equation (e.g. "V = I × R"), sourced from the governed formula family via `formatExpressionInline`, never invented app-side prose.

**4/6. Formula notation — × not "times"; I-vs-length-symbol readability.** The "times"→"×" sweep covers every reachable Ohm's-law and magnetism/effects-of-current assertion, capability description, and worked-example instruction. The I-vs-length-symbol defect (capital "I" and lowercase "l" rendering as visually near-identical vertical strokes, live-confirmed on the emulator) was fixed twice: an initial approach substituted a dedicated Unicode "SCRIPT SMALL L" glyph (ℓ) for visual display only, live-verified working — then explicitly rejected by Product Owner correction mid-package in favour of fidelity to the course's own existing notation (plain capital "L", the same convention already used for resistivity's `R = ρL/A`). The governed length symbol itself was renamed from lowercase "l" to "L" everywhere in the force-on-conductor/motional-EMF slice (see item 1), and the glyph-substitution mechanism was removed entirely rather than left as a now-inert no-op. **Deliberate, documented exception:** the one approved canonical teaching-context image asset (`emf-motional-teaching-master-v3.png`) bakes a lowercase italic "l" label into its pixels; regenerating it is out of this package's explicit "no image regen" scope, and — since the image has no "I" label anywhere near it — it does not itself exhibit the I-vs-l ambiguity this fix targets. Its accessibility text was updated to "L" for notation consistency; the residual baked-pixel "l" is flagged here for a future visual-production pass, not fixed now. Two production-planning documents (`tools/visual-production-studio/semantic-reference-qa.ts`, `scripts/visual-governance/data/cc05d-visual-contracts-unit202.ts`, `tools/visual-production-studio/catalogue.ts`) that specify a *future* regeneration of that same asset were also updated to "L", so any future regen inherits the correct notation.
- **Bug caught by this correction, fixed before completion:** the L-rename's first pass missed two worked-example blueprints (`worked.force_on_conductor.calculate`, `worked.motional_emf.calculate`) still keyed on lowercase `"l"` in `knownVariables`/`teachingValues`. This produced a real, live-caught render crash ("missing binding for variable 'L'", `FormulaEvaluationError`) on the motional-EMF worked-example step. Fixed by renaming both blueprints' keys to "L"; re-verified live afterwards with a clean render.
- **Live-verified on the emulator (post-fix):** force-on-conductor calculation question (`F = B × I × L`, givens "I = 3 A (current)" / "L = 0.9 m (conductor length)", "I" and "L" visually distinguishable, submitted 5.4 N → Correct); motional-EMF worked example (`e = B × L × v`, no render error); motional-EMF guided calculation (givens "B = 1 T" / "L = 0.21 m" / "v = 3 m/s", submitted 0.63 V → Correct).

**5. Page-direction (×/•) notation.** New governed assertion `EL-CONCEPT-PAGE-DIRECTION-NOTATION-001` ("× = into the page, • = out of the page") is taught once on `concept_field_from_current`, before either diagram that relies on the convention, and is never restated on a graded/direction-assessed step (verified: absent from both `guided_interpret_field_direction` and `guided_interpret_force_direction`'s own body text) — teaches the symbol meaning without spoiling the assessed direction. Live-verified: the field-direction diagram's "conventional current out of the page" (•) label and the force-direction question (Fleming's left-hand rule, current out of page, N-top/S-bottom field → force acts Right) both resolved correctly.

**5. (Product-owner architecture finding, discovered during RUN D live-check.)** The real blocker to reaching the magnetism lesson live was not a content gap but a hard product-architecture defect: `assembleLessonInstance` refused to assemble a requested lesson at all whenever an unmet prerequisite existed, and the Lesson Player's `prerequisite_blocked` screen was a dead end with no route to the remediation lesson. Per explicit Product Owner direction ("normal learner lessons must never be hard-locked... the learner must always be able to open the lesson directly... prerequisites may affect recommendations... never gate direct access"), this was corrected as an in-package architecture fix, not deferred: `LessonAssemblyResult` changed from a 3-way union (two of which refused assembly) to a 2-way union (`ready` / `ready_with_prerequisite_advisory`) where the requested lesson's own playable instance is **always** present; `findUnmetPrerequisites` (plural) now returns every unmet family as an advisory, never blocks on the first. The Lesson Player shows a plain-language advisory banner (naming the remediation lesson, or a generic message if unresolved) instead of refusing to open. New regression test (`lesson-player-prerequisite-advisory.test.tsx`) proves direct lesson access for both a resolvable and an unresolvable advisory. (A separate, unrelated pre-existing crash — the `diagram_region` answer type had no native input component — was found and fixed along the way, per explicit Product Owner instruction to fix-then-continue rather than skip.)

**7. "Lesson complete" step decision.** Removed. It was in-sequence internal-engine prose duplicating the real, separate completion screen (`LessonCompletionView.tsx`), never learner-load-bearing; the lesson-plan schema's own `exitSummary` field already guarantees a completion summary exists, making the step (and its governance gate) redundant.

**8. Previous-step review navigation.** Header gained a "‹" control (distinct from "Exit") that steps backward through `completedStepIds` (never `stepSequence`/`currentIndex`, so it cannot desynchronise from actual progress); review mode renders the step `readOnly` (no `AnswerInputDispatch`, no feedback/Continue block — structurally impossible to resubmit or duplicate evidence) with a "Reviewing a completed step — nothing you do here is recorded" notice and a "›" to return forward. **Real layout bug caught live:** adding the nav buttons initially pushed "Exit" fully off-screen (measured via `uiautomator` bounds, not visual estimate) — root-caused to `ProgressIndicator`'s internal `flex:1` with no outer constraint; fixed by wrapping it in its own `flex:1` container and shortening button labels to icon-only.

**9. Emulator acceptance.** RUN C (formula typography, I/L readability) and RUN D (page-direction notation, including the force-on-conductor and motional-EMF calculation steps) live-verified this pass, including a genuine mid-run render-error catch-and-fix (item 6). RUN A (randomisation), RUN B (rearrangement wording), RUN E (previous-navigation), and RUN F (lesson-completion) were live-verified earlier in this same package's acceptance pass, before the mid-package notation correction; their underlying code paths are additionally covered by the automated suites in item 10, all re-run clean after every subsequent change including the L-rename.

**10. Tests/results.** `npm run typecheck` (full monorepo, genuinely re-run from a clean root — an earlier in-session run was accidentally scoped to the mobile workspace only after a shell `cwd` drift, caught and corrected): clean. `apps/mobile` full Jest suite: 66 suites / 434 tests green (one `capture-renders.test.tsx` timeout under host load, confirmed flaky — passes in 2s in isolation, well under its 5000ms budget). Full monorepo `vitest run`: 987/989 tests green; the 2 failures (`tools/project-dashboard/roadmap-data.test.ts`, dashboard CC-12 status tracking) are pre-existing and unrelated to this package.

**11. PROJECT-STATUS update.** This section.

**12. Commit/push:** `67b358d`.
