## CC-12H — Full Unit 202 Runtime Qualification & Architectural Repair

**Status:** COMPLETE. Every one of the 24 lessons in the current bundled production release (`release.unit202.v8`) walked to genuine completion on the real Android emulator by correctly answering every graded question — not skimmed with Continue taps — using a new purpose-built runtime QA tool, not the earlier CC-12H smoke pass (first-few-steps reachability only). All 24 lessons reached "Lesson complete"; all 4 diagnostic/remediation-branching lessons (`lesson.electrical.ohms-law`, `lesson.electrical.resistors-parallel`, `lesson.magnetism.effects-of-current`, `lesson.waveforms.ac-dc-and-sine-wave-quantities`) had their branch deliberately triggered and confirmed resolving back into normal completion. Full detail, the lesson-by-lesson matrix, and defect writeups: `reports/unit202-runtime-qualification/CC-12H-runtime-qualification-report.md`.

**1. Runtime QA mechanism (new, reusable).** Rather than trying to replicate learner-identity/RNG-seed derivation offline to predict correct answers, the pre-existing dev-only debug overlay (`lesson-player.tsx`, gated by `__DEV__ && debugOverlayEnabled`, default off, toggled only from the `__DEV__`-only `dev-lesson-qa` screen — never reachable by a real learner) was extended with a new `resolveDevDebugAnswer` export (`answer-input-dispatch.tsx`) that resolves and displays, for the current graded step, the objectively correct submission value **and** the exact learner-facing label to tap — reusing the same option/label construction the real answer-input renderers already use. `tools/qa/lesson-runtime-walk.sh` (new) reads that ground truth directly off the live screen each step via `tools/qa/uia-dump.cjs` (new — a small dependency-free Node `uiautomator dump` parser, replacing the fragile sed/grep attribute extraction of the earlier smoke pass, which breaks on parentheses/unicode in real content labels) and drives the correct native control for whichever of the 7 governed answer types is showing. For the 4 branching lessons, one step per lesson is deliberately answered incorrectly (any wrong answer on a diagnostic step triggers its own declared misconception branch via `@alp/learning-engine`'s `resolveWithinSessionBranch` — no need to target a specific distractor). `tools/qa/lesson-ids.txt` (new) is kept honest against the real release by a new Jest test, `apps/mobile/src/lib/lesson-content/qa-lesson-manifest.test.ts`.

**2. Genuine defects found live and fixed (both root-caused, not per-instance patches; both re-verified live after the fix).**
- **Duplicate React key on multi-form questions** (`BLUEPRINT_RUNTIME_CONTRACT_MISMATCH`) — caught via a LogBox "Encountered two children with the same key, `P`" on `lesson.electrical.power`. `LessonStepView.tsx` keyed a formula family's `forms` array by `form.target` alone, silently assuming one form per target variable; `formula.electrical_power` legitimately declares three valid forms for `P` (`P=V×I`, `P=I²×R`, `P=V²/R` — genuine physics, not a content bug). Fixed by keying `${form.target}-${index}` instead — a rendering-layer fix, no content changed.
- **`direction` answer type misapplied to increase/decrease questions** (`BLUEPRINT_RUNTIME_CONTRACT_MISMATCH`) — caught live on `lesson.electrical.resistivity`'s `guided_predict_length_area_effects`, which rendered four nonsensical "Force acts Up/Down/Left/Right" screen-direction buttons for a "does resistance increase or decrease" question. Root cause: `resistivity.predict_length_effect`/`predict_area_effect` declared `answer: { type: "direction" }` even though their own `@alp/calculation-engine` executors have always returned the qualitative string `"increase"`/`"decrease"`, never a screen direction — `"direction"` is correctly reserved for exactly two blueprints in the whole corpus (`magnetism.interpret_force_direction`/`interpret_field_direction`, both `marking: direction_match` with a required diagram). A corpus-wide sweep found the identical bug on two further, currently-unreferenced blueprints (`series.predict_add_component_effect`, `parallel.predict_add_branch_effect`) before they could ever reach a learner. All four fixed to `multiple_choice` with `increase`/`decrease` options and labels, matching the pattern their own family sibling `resistivity.compare_materials` already used correctly. `mobile-content-projection.ts` regenerated via `npm run content:mobile:generate`.

**3. Files changed.** `apps/mobile/src/components/lesson/LessonStepView.tsx`; `apps/mobile/src/lib/lesson-content/answer-input-dispatch.tsx`; `apps/mobile/src/app/(app)/learn/lesson-player.tsx`; `apps/mobile/src/lib/lesson-content/qa-lesson-manifest.test.ts` (new); `scripts/content/data/cc05a-pedagogy-unit202.ts`; `apps/mobile/src/lib/lesson-content/generated/mobile-content-projection.ts` (regenerated); `tools/qa/lesson-runtime-walk.sh`, `tools/qa/uia-dump.cjs`, `tools/qa/lesson-ids.txt`, `tools/qa/runtime-walk-results.txt` (all new). Root-level `smoke_walk.sh`/`smoke_results.txt`/`sw_dump.xml`/`lesson_ids.txt` (an earlier, first-few-steps-only reachability probe from the start of this session) removed, superseded by the tools/qa/ tool above.

**4. Answer-input coverage.** All 7 of `SUPPORTED_ANSWER_TYPES` (`quantity`, `multiple_choice`, `formula_selection`, `multi_select`, `worked_error_classification`, `diagram_region`, `direction` — both screen-direction and field-rotation domains) were exercised live by real production content during this walk, unchanged from before this package (the registry itself was completed in an earlier CC-12H step, prior to this runtime-walk sub-package).

**5. Tests/results.** `scripts/content` Vitest suite: 229/229 green after the content fix. Mobile Jest: targeted suites green throughout (`mobile-runtime-contract-audit.test.tsx` 412/412, `qa-lesson-manifest.test.ts` 1/1, plus every other suite touched); full mobile Jest suite and full monorepo typecheck run once at the end, both green (see commit for the exact final run).

**6. A known automation limitation, logged rather than chased further.** Numeric-answer Submit taps and some Continue taps intermittently needed a retry (occasionally a full app restart) to register on this emulator, even at freshly-recomputed, verified-correct coordinates — cross-checked via screenshots and raw accessibility-tree bounds to rule out a scroll/keyboard-overlay/coordinate-space bug. Reads as adb/emulator input-delivery contention under many hours of sustained automated load, not a product defect: every affected step subsequently submitted correctly with the *same* computed value on retry. One genuine Android ANR was hit once (an ordinary numeric `retrieval_check` step, `lesson.foundation.physics.mechanics-force-work-energy-power`) and resolved by relaunching the app; the same step then submitted correctly on the next attempt.

**7. Open items.** No product-decision-needed items and no new cosmetic/editorial findings — both defects found had one unambiguous correct fix.

**8. Commit:** see the CC-12H commit(s) immediately following this entry.

**Next:** engineering qualification of Unit 202 is complete. The next step is the **Product Owner's quality/content/readability review of the full module** — not further engineering.

## CC-13A — Learning-Package Architecture Reset & V1 Learner Model Integration

**Status:** COMPLETE (integration/foundation scope only — see "Next" below).

**Trigger.** CC-12H's runtime qualification (immediately above) is real and remains valid — every lesson genuinely completes and every diagnostic/remediation branch genuinely resolves on-device. But the Product Owner's own subsequent quality/content/readability review of Unit 202 (the review CC-12H's own closing line called for) found that runtime PASS is necessary but not sufficient for a premium learning product: learner-visible internal/debug language, teaching content leaking formative answers, redundant duplicated completion screens, off-syllabus content, weak diagnostic framing, and text-only conceptual/component lessons with little or no meaningful imagery. Two Product Owner-approved architecture decisions followed: **ADR-0005** (ground-up learning-package production with independent visual governance — curriculum/knowledge/pedagogy/visual-planning/reference-governance/questions/runtime designed as one pipeline, visuals first-class governed content planned before authoring, Claude may extract/orchestrate but never approve technical references) and **ADR-0006** (V1 simplification — one canonical premium lesson route per ordinary lesson, rich scrollable teaching, V1 adaptation only via a dedicated formative/mock assessment's `COMPLETED + SUBMITTED` boundary driving a deterministic Guided Revision plan over full canonical lessons). CC-08/CC-12's richer adaptive machinery (diagnostic branching, mastery-driven skip/remediation) is **retained** as implemented, proven platform capability and legitimate post-V1 direction — none of it was deleted or found to be wrong; it is simply no longer a V1 ordinary-lesson production requirement. Full trigger detail: `reports/architecture/2026-08-29-learning-package-architecture-reset.md`, `reports/architecture/2026-08-29-v1-learning-model-simplification.md`.

**1. Governing documents (14 new files, Project-Architect-authored, written verbatim).** `docs/architecture/SYLLABUS-TO-LEARNING-PACKAGE-PRODUCTION-ARCHITECTURE.md`, `docs/architecture/V1-LEARNING-ASSESSMENT-AND-GUIDED-REVISION-ARCHITECTURE.md`, `docs/architecture/INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-PLANNING-REFERENCE-AND-PRODUCTION-ARCHITECTURE.md`, `docs/architecture/LEARNING-PACKAGE-GOVERNANCE-CONTRACTS.md`, `docs/architecture/LEARNING-PACKAGE-PIPELINE-AUDIT-AND-QUALIFICATION-PLAN.md` (governs the *next* package, not this one), `docs/architecture/ARCHITECTURE-RESET-INTEGRATION-MATRIX.md` (the mechanical reconciliation checklist this package executed), `docs/architecture/adr/ADR-0005-*.md`, `docs/architecture/adr/ADR-0006-*.md`, `docs/design/ALP-PRODUCT-WIDE-VISUAL-DESIGN-SYSTEM.md`, `docs/governance/LEARNING-PACKAGE-QUALITY-GATES.md`, `docs/governance/VISUAL-REFERENCE-REVIEW-PROTOCOL.md`, `docs/product/PREMIUM-LEARNING-PACKAGE-STANDARD.md`, and the two trigger reports named above.

**2. Existing documentation reconciled against the Integration Matrix's §2 checklist, file by file** — surgical additions/amendments only, no historical CC-05 through CC-12H evidence rewritten: `docs/architecture/ARCHITECTURE-OVERVIEW.md`, `CC-05-PEDAGOGICAL-KNOWLEDGE-AND-QUESTION-ARCHITECTURE.md`, `LESSON-PLAYER-AND-LESSON-PLAN-ARCHITECTURE.md` (the matrix's flagged "high-risk mismatch" — new §20 is the authoritative ADR-0006 reconciliation of §6/§7's mastery-driven assembly model), `CC-05D-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-GOVERNANCE-AND-SEMANTIC-QA.md`, `PREMIUM-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-PRODUCTION-PIPELINE.md` (new §25: VRR/ARL/AAL catalogue naming, and the reference-acquisition wording fix below), `docs/design/ALP-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-STYLE-GUIDE.md`, `docs/product/PRODUCT-PRINCIPLES.md` (9 new principles, 33-41), `docs/product/MOBILE-UX-ENGINEERING-STANDARD.md`, `docs/governance/ROLES-AND-AUTHORITY.md`, `docs/development/AI-DEVELOPMENT-PROTOCOL.md`, `docs/development/DEVELOPMENT-WORKFLOW.md`, `docs/governance/PROJECT-PLAYBOOK.md`, `docs/governance/DECISION-LOG.md` (two new 2026-08-29 rows), `docs/roadmap/ROADMAP.md` (new programme checkpoint before Phase 2), `README.md`, `docs/START-HERE.md`, and (evidence/mastery/diagnostic current docs) `docs/research/phase-1/PHASE-1-WP1.3-LEARNER-EVIDENCE-AND-MASTERY-ARCHITECTURE.md` / `PHASE-1-WP1.4-DIAGNOSTIC-AND-REMEDIATION-ENGINE.md`. Standardised marker phrase applied everywhere richer adaptive machinery is described: **"retained implemented platform capability / post-V1 option — not deleted, but not a V1 ordinary-lesson production requirement."** `docs/governance/DOCUMENTATION-CURRENCY-REGISTER.md` was updated with a full CC-13A semantic Tier-1 sweep (read for meaning, not keyword grep — the CC-12E.1 lesson) confirming no live document still asserts: visuals as downstream polish, Claude reference authority, runtime-PASS-equals-learner-ready, the four-lesson proving-slice assumption, or mastery auto-skipping V1 teaching.

Reference-acquisition authority wording fixed.** `PREMIUM-INSTRUCTIONAL-VISUAL-PRODUCTION-PIPELINE.md` §3 previously read ambiguously enough that a found/cached search result could be misread as an approved reference; it now states explicitly that Claude/automated tooling performs **candidate discovery/caching only** — a reference becomes APPROVED (entering the Approved Reference Library) only through the independent ChatGPT/Project Architect review defined in `docs/governance/VISUAL-REFERENCE-REVIEW-PROTOCOL.md`. `tools/visual-production-studio/reference-acquisition.ts` was checked and confirmed to already only download a pre-approved URL, never perform autonomous search/selection — no code change needed there.

Two new `superRefine` gates: a `CANONICAL_FIXED_ROUTE` lesson must have every step `requirement: "required"`; a step marked `mayRevealTargetAnswer` must not precede a graded (`correct_answer_required`) step testing the same capability/assertion (the embedded-check answer-leak contract).
   - `pedagogy.ts` (extended): `v1QuestionRoleSchema` (`LESSON_CHECK`/`FORMATIVE_MOCK`/`SUMMATIVE`/`EXAM_PRACTICE`) and, on `questionBlueprintManifestSchema`, `requiredKnowledgeIds`/`v1PedagogicalRole`/`revisionLessonIds` (all optional, same no-forced-corpus-edit reasoning), with a `superRefine` requiring every `FORMATIVE_MOCK` blueprint to declare at least one `revisionLessonId`.
   - `visual-governance.ts` (extended): the whole upstream ADR-0005 planning layer — `VisualOpportunityAnalysis`, `VisualRequirement` (VRR entry), `ReferenceDossier` (`reviewedBy` is a fixed `"PROJECT_ARCHITECT"` literal type — mechanically impossible to construct an approved dossier attributing review to Claude), `VisualFamilyContract`, `ProductionVisualAsset` (structurally impossible to be `PRODUCTION_ELIGIBLE` without every QA dimension PASS + Product Owner approval + a reference dossier), plus `CURRENT_DESIGN_SYSTEM_VERSION` bound to a new declared version identifier in `ALP-PRODUCT-WIDE-VISUAL-DESIGN-SYSTEM.md` §17.1.
   - `assessment-instance.ts` (new): `FormativeAssessmentInstance` (attempt lifecycle, `AssessmentAttemptStatus`, submission-boundary invariants enforced in `superRefine`), `SubmittedAssessmentResult`, and `buildSubmittedAssessmentResult()` — the only sanctioned constructor, which throws for any non-`SUBMITTED` instance.
   - `guided-revision.ts` (new): `LessonWeaknessResult`, `GuidedRevisionPlan`/`GuidedRevisionPlanItem` (schema-level dedup/contiguous-rank checks), `buildGuidedRevisionPlan()` (a real deterministic pure-function weakness aggregator/ranker, not just types), and `selectLatestSubmittedResultInScope()`.
   - `learning-package-gate.ts` (new): `GateName`/`LearningPackageGateResult`/`isPublicationReady()` — the shared result shape every publication gate reports through.
   - `index.ts` updated to export all of the above. 61 new tests across `lesson-plan.test.ts` (+8), `pedagogy.test.ts` (+4), `visual-planning-governance.test.ts` (new, 16), `assessment-instance.test.ts` (new, 10), `guided-revision.test.ts` (new, 12), `learning-package-gate.test.ts` (new, 11) — plus 16 in `scripts/content/validate-v1-learning-package.test.ts` and 3 in the new mobile debug-overlay suite (§6 below): 80 new tests total.

**5. Deterministic validators added: `scripts/content/validate-v1-learning-package.ts`** (new, mirrors `validate-lesson-plan.ts`/`validate-pedagogy.ts`'s never-trust-the-object's-own-claim discipline; `npm run v1-package:report`/`v1-package:check`). Implements: off-syllabus required-knowledge detection (a `requiredKnowledgeId` never taught by any step anywhere in the corpus), "required knowledge from an undeclared other lesson" (the exact CC-12H-era finding "component questions depending on material taught only in another lesson" — knowledge taught only by a *different* lesson than the one containing the check, with no declared `prerequisiteKnowledge` link), dangling `FORMATIVE_MOCK` → `revisionLessonIds` references, `POST_V1_ADAPTIVE`-typed steps inside a `CANONICAL_FIXED_ROUTE` lesson, plus exported helpers `validateVisualGovernance()` (required+approved visuals must resolve to an actually-`APPROVED` dossier; stale design-system-version bindings; at most one `PRODUCTION_ELIGIBLE` version per asset, and it must be the newest — obsolete/superseded assets cannot resolve at runtime) and `validateGuidedRevisionPlanLessons()` (every plan item resolves to a real canonical lesson). Run against the live 140-lesson/114-blueprint Unit 202 corpus: **clean (0 adoption, 0 failures)** — an honest baseline, since re-authoring existing content under the new V1 fields is the *next* package's job, not this one's.

## CC-13B — Whole Learning-Package & V1 Pipeline Integrity Audit

**Status:** AUDIT COMPLETE — awaiting Product Owner / Project Architect acceptance (audit only — no remediation). The repository entered this audit from a clean HEAD at `b049fd1fb4ad4f9dca2a35e5b51a08b71d91e48e` (== `origin/main`), immediately following CC-13A/13A.1/13A.2's foundation work (immediately above). This package audited the real, current-state pipeline against ADR-0005/ADR-0006 and CC-13A's own claims — it traced actual code/content/generated-artifact paths, ran real (read-only) validators/tests against the live corpus, and wrote two new read-only audit scripts (`scripts/audit/lesson-structure-audit.ts`, `scripts/audit/lesson-structure-audit-supplement.ts`, kept for reproducibility) to compute real mechanical statistics against the bundled 24-lesson/270-step Unit 202 corpus. It made **zero** production/content/schema/test/asset changes. Full detail: `reports/learning-package-pipeline-audit/` (16 required deliverables + 3 structured-data companions — start at `CC-13B-EXECUTIVE-SUMMARY.md`).

**Headline finding counts:** ~5 P0 (architecture-integrity defects), ~13 P1 (blocks class-leading quality), ~12 P2 (cleanup) — exact enumeration with full evidence lives in each per-layer register; this is a roll-up, not a false-precision count.

**Major root-cause categories:** (1) no schema field exists anywhere for extended, multi-sentence teaching prose — a structural mismatch with the new "rich scrollable teaching" mandate, not an authoring-discipline failure; (2) a real, unenforced gap in the `CANONICAL_FIXED_ROUTE` schema gate (`branchRoutes` isn't checked), currently unexploited only because 0 real lessons adopt `routePolicy` at all; (3) two structurally disconnected visual-production systems — an operative-but-non-conformant old ad hoc pipeline and a conformant-but-empty new ADR-0005 schema — plus at least 4 disconnected visual-asset-eligibility trackers, one of which is documented-authoritative but does not exist in the repository; (4) the formative-assessment → submit → Guided-Revision loop, V1's entire stated adaptive product promise, does not exist as a runtime feature at all — only as real, well-tested, but completely unreachable pure functions; (5) a mechanically-confirmed, already-once-fixed-but-never-generalised defect: 23 of the 24 real bundled lessons carry a redundant authored `exit_completion` screen duplicating the terminal completion screen (both render the same `exitSummary`; the preceding recap step is distinct pedagogical content, not itself proven duplicate — CC-12G fixed this for exactly one lesson and explicitly scoped the fix narrowly).

**CC-13B.1 correction (applied to this entry and to the audit reports themselves before acceptance):** two inconsistencies were found and corrected prior to any Product Owner / Project Architect acceptance of the audit — (a) the completion-screen finding above was originally overstated as "triple-redundant lesson-complete messaging" across several audit files; the evidence only supports duplication between the authored `exit_completion` step and the terminal completion screen, not the preceding recap, and has been corrected accordingly (the underlying 23/24 metric is unchanged); (b) the remediation plan omitted a package for the audit's own P0 finding "no schema field for extended teaching prose" — a new Package 2 ("Extend the canonical teaching-content model for rich scrollable teaching," Project-Architect-review-required, pilot-blocking) has been added and the plan resequenced to 13 packages. No remediation was performed; only the audit documents changed.

**CC-13B.2 correction (applied before acceptance):** a further sequencing contradiction was found in the visual-governance packages (originally Packages 3-6): the plan would have allowed a legacy-pipeline visual asset to become learner-visible before passing the new ADR-0005 reference-authority and production-eligibility chain, and implied a historical Product Owner reference handover could automatically count as an approved current `ReferenceDossier`. Corrected: the plan now expresses the dependency-correct order — visual planning mandatory (3) → reference-dossier authority (4) → canonical production-eligibility authority + release-ID fix (5) → qualify/integrate reusable legacy assets (6) — with an explicit rule that legacy QA-PASS status and legacy reference provenance are reusable evidence, never automatic authorisation, and that no automated process may write `reviewedBy: "PROJECT_ARCHITECT"` without actual Project Architect review. Package 9 (publication gates) now explicitly depends on Packages 4 and 5 as well. The package count remains 13. No remediation was performed; only the audit documents changed.

**Contract-adoption headline:** **0% adoption, corpus-wide, across every single ADR-0005/ADR-0006/CC-13A governance field, with no exceptions** — 0/24 lessons, 0/270 steps, 0/114 question blueprints, 0 real visual-governance object instances, 0 real formative-assessment/Guided-Revision/publication-gate-result instances anywhere. This precisely confirms, with real computed numbers rather than an estimate, CC-13A's own stated expectation. Full detail: `reports/learning-package-pipeline-audit/CONTRACT-ADOPTION-MATRIX.md`.

**Is the pipeline currently safe to begin systematic Unit 202 re-authoring? No — not yet, and this audit does not claim otherwise.** The repository has a sound, real, well-tested schema/validator foundation and a genuinely working curriculum/lesson/runtime pipeline through lesson delivery (CC-12H remains valid), and this audit's own findings are evidence-backed and remediation-planned, not merely "the audit ran." But systematic Unit 202 re-authoring is explicitly gated, per `docs/architecture/LEARNING-PACKAGE-PIPELINE-AUDIT-AND-QUALIFICATION-PLAN.md` §9, on a full end-to-end V1 pilot passing first — and that pilot itself cannot currently complete its own defined sequence, because the formative-assessment and Guided-Revision features it requires do not exist yet as runtime features. Completing this audit is not the same as qualifying the pipeline; qualification is a separate, future determination this package does not make.

**Explicit next step:** Product Owner / Project Architect review of this audit's findings and its `REMEDIATION-PLAN.md` (13 dependency-ordered packages, each with objective/root-cause/dependencies/acceptance-criteria/pilot-blocking status) **before any remediation begins.** No remediation, CC-13C, or Unit 202 re-authoring work should start from this audit's findings without that review.

**Commit:** see the CC-13B, CC-13B.1 and CC-13B.2 commits immediately following this entry. Local only, not pushed.

## CC-13C.1 — Remediation Package 1: CANONICAL_FIXED_ROUTE branchRoutes integrity

**Status:** COMPLETE — the single audited defect this package targets is fixed and tested. This is Remediation Package 1 of `REMEDIATION-PLAN.md`'s 13, executed only after that plan (and CC-13B/13B.1/13B.2's corrections to it) was pushed and available for Product Owner / Project Architect review.

**Audited defect addressed:** `V1-ROUTE-DRIFT-REGISTER.md` §2 / `BYPASS-PATH-REGISTER.md` BP-1 (P0) — a lesson declaring `routePolicy: "CANONICAL_FIXED_ROUTE"` had its step *inclusion* correctly locked (every step must be `requirement: "required"`), but a `required` step could still carry a non-empty `branchRoutes`, which `@alp/learning-engine`'s `resolveWithinSessionBranch` would apply exactly as it applies branching for the 4 known retained-adaptive lessons — a real, mechanically-exploitable route-invariance bypass, previously inert only because 0 real lessons declare `routePolicy` at all.

**Exact invariant now enforced:** `packages/content-schema/src/lesson-plan.ts`'s existing `CANONICAL_FIXED_ROUTE` `superRefine` gate is extended so that, in addition to the pre-existing `requirement`-must-be-`"required"` check, any step with a non-empty `branchRoutes` array fails validation with an issue naming the lesson/step and stating `CANONICAL_FIXED_ROUTE lessons cannot declare branchRoutes`. `CANONICAL_FIXED_ROUTE` now means, structurally: no conditional step inclusion **and** no within-lesson branch routes. Confirmed (per the schema's own `.default([])`) that omitted, explicit-empty-array, and post-parse-default `branchRoutes` are all treated identically as the harmless case — only a genuinely non-empty array is rejected.

**Tests added:** `packages/content-schema/src/lesson-plan.test.ts`, 4 new tests in the existing "ADR-0006 routePolicy: CANONICAL_FIXED_ROUTE invariance gate" `describe` block — (a) rejects a `CANONICAL_FIXED_ROUTE` lesson with a required step carrying a non-empty `branchRoutes`; (b) accepts one with an explicit empty `branchRoutes`; (c) accepts one that omits `branchRoutes` entirely (schema default); (d) confirms the pre-existing `requirement`-check invariant is unchanged by this fix. A non-canonical lesson's continued freedom to use `branchRoutes` (requirement C of the brief) is already proven by a pre-existing, untouched test in the same file.

**Targeted validation:** `lesson-plan.test.ts` 34/34 (was 30/30 before this package); full `packages/content-schema` Vitest suite 176/176 (was 172/172); `packages/content-schema` `tsc --noEmit` clean; `npm run v1-package:report` (read-only) — 140 lessons, 0 declaring `routePolicy`, all governance gates 0, **PASS**, confirming the real corpus (including the 4 retained branching lessons, none of which declares `routePolicy`) is unaffected.

**No real lesson content changed.** No `routePolicy` was added to any lesson — 0/140 adoption is unchanged and intentionally so; this package closes a structural bypass, it does not perform content re-authoring (that is Package 13, strictly gated on the V1 pilot passing). Retained adaptive/branching capability (`packages/learning-engine`, `packages/diagnostic-engine`, the 4 known branching lessons) is untouched.

**Remediation status recorded in the audit:** `V1-ROUTE-DRIFT-REGISTER.md` §2 and `BYPASS-PATH-REGISTER.md` BP-1 both now carry a "Remediation status (CC-13C.1): CLOSED" note above the original, unedited historical finding text, plus a Status column in each file's severity-summary table. The historical finding itself was not rewritten.

**Package 2 has NOT started.** No other remediation package was begun. This package does not claim the overall pipeline is qualified — Packages 2 through 13 (rich teaching-content model, visual governance, formative assessment, Guided Revision, publication gates, and the rest) remain exactly as specified in `REMEDIATION-PLAN.md`, unstarted.

**Commit:** see the CC-13C.1 commit immediately following this entry. Local only, not pushed.

## CC-13C.2 — Remediation Package 2: Governed Rich Teaching Content Blocks (Implementation + Production-Wiring Proof + Documentation Closure)

**Status:** Implemented, production-wiring-proved, and documentation-closed. This is Remediation Package 2 of `REMEDIATION-PLAN.md`'s 13 ("Extend the canonical teaching-content model for rich scrollable teaching"). **LOCAL ONLY, NOT PUSHED — pending Product Owner / Project Architect review of this closure commit**, exactly like Package 1 was before its own acceptance.

**Package 1 context:** `CC-13C.1` (immediately above) was subsequently accepted and pushed as `origin/main` commit `d0fea4426c6de1c7adf8317464d34eb74a3eba8d` ("fix: enforce canonical lesson route invariance"). This package's local commits build directly on top of that pushed baseline.

**Implementation commit `0e220eea9451f9fde5e3157cbeb01b6170fa2bb6`** ("feat: add governed rich teaching content blocks"): `packages/content-schema/src/lesson-plan.ts` gains an optional, ordered, non-empty-when-present `contentBlocks` field on `lessonStepSchema` (exactly six governed block families — `paragraph`/`list`/`visual`/`formula`/`worked_example`/`callout` — a discriminated union, every governed-content-bearing block reusing an existing `FormulaFamily`/`WorkedExampleBlueprint`/`DiagramBlueprint`/`VisualAidBlueprint` reference) plus an optional `learnerFacingHeading`. `lessonPlanSchema`'s `superRefine` enforces: legacy-`representation.*` mutual exclusivity, teaching/graded-question step separation (`contentBlocks` cannot coexist with `completionCondition: "correct_answer_required"` or a `questionBlueprintId`), and an explicit `mayRevealTargetAnswer` requirement. `apps/mobile/src/lib/lesson-content/resolve-lesson-step.ts` (resolver), `apps/mobile/src/components/lesson/LessonStepView.tsx`/`ContentBlockView.tsx` (mobile rendering), and `packages/learning-engine/src/content-dependencies.ts` (dependency walker) all gained corresponding support. Produced-artwork (`ProductionVisualAsset`) integration is deliberately NOT part of this package — visual blocks support only the existing runtime-capable governed `diagram`/`visual_aid` forms. Full detail: `docs/architecture/LESSON-PLAYER-AND-LESSON-PLAN-ARCHITECTURE.md` §21, `docs/architecture/LEARNING-PACKAGE-GOVERNANCE-CONTRACTS.md` §15.

**Production-wiring proof commit `1087634fbe1482f5c0735f30508e6cdb79e59b0f`** ("test: prove rich content production runtime wiring"): closes the "test-only wiring" risk that every implementation-package unit test individually left open — each hand-constructs a `LessonPlan` and calls `resolveLessonStep()` directly, never proving the field survives the REAL production lookup boundary `apps/mobile/src/app/(app)/learn/lesson-player.tsx` actually calls first. `apps/mobile/src/lib/lesson-content/rich-content-production-wiring.test.tsx` enters at `getLocalLessonFrom(MOBILE_CONTENT_PROJECTION, ...)`/`getLocalLesson()` — the same lookup function the real player uses for both its explicit-release and bundled-default-release code paths — with a synthetic release-member lesson, and confirms `learnerFacingHeading`/`contentBlocks` survive that lookup intact, `resolveLessonStep()` selects the `contentBlocks` path over legacy `bodyStatements`, every governed reference resolves to the real bundled object, and `LessonStepView` renders the result. **Verdict: ALREADY CORRECTLY WIRED — no production defect found, no production code changed by that package.** `@alp/learning-engine`'s `LessonInstance` was confirmed reference-only by design (step ids, never step content), so it cannot be a field-stripping point either.

**Adoption status, stated explicitly:** the rich representation is schema/runtime-capable end-to-end (governed schema → generated mobile projection → registry lookup → resolver → renderer) but has **zero real Unit 202 lesson adoption** — no lesson in the bundled `release.unit202.v8` corpus (or any release) declares `contentBlocks`/`learnerFacingHeading`. Unit 202 has **not** been re-authored to use this representation. Re-authoring is explicitly later, separately-gated work (`REMEDIATION-PLAN.md` Package 13), strictly gated on the full V1 pilot passing (CC-13B). This does not mean Unit 202 is learner-ready and does not mean the learning-package production pipeline is qualified — schema-capable ≠ production-content-adopted ≠ learner-ready. Full detail: `reports/learning-package-pipeline-audit/LESSON-DEPTH-AND-FRAGMENTATION-REGISTER.md` §8.

**Serial/open-handle diagnostic (this closure package):** `npx jest --runInBand --detectOpenHandles` run against the five affected files (`rich-content-production-wiring.test.tsx`, `local-content-registry.test.ts`, `resolve-lesson-step.test.ts`, `LessonStepView.test.tsx`, `lesson-player-screen.test.tsx`) — **91/91 passed**, reproducibly, across three separate invocations (two via direct `npx jest`, one via the `npm run test --workspace apps/mobile` wrapper). `--detectOpenHandles` identified no concrete open handle in any run, and the previously observed "A worker process has failed to exit gracefully" warning did not recur in any of the three. One transient single-test timeout occurred on the very first invocation (via the npm/cmd.exe workspace-wrapper path) and did not reproduce on either of two immediate re-runs of the identical command set — attributed to first-invocation cold-start/system load, not a concrete open handle or teardown defect. No code was changed to suppress or work around it.

**Documentation closure performed in this package:** `docs/architecture/LESSON-PLAYER-AND-LESSON-PLAN-ARCHITECTURE.md` §21 (implemented Package-2 architecture, explicit that Unit 202 has not adopted it), `docs/architecture/LEARNING-PACKAGE-GOVERNANCE-CONTRACTS.md` §15 (governance rules, explicit that produced artwork/Packages 3-5 machinery is not implemented here), `reports/learning-package-pipeline-audit/LESSON-DEPTH-AND-FRAGMENTATION-REGISTER.md` §8 (remediation-status note appended; §1-§7 historical findings unedited), and this entry.

**PACKAGE 3 HAS NOT STARTED.** Package 3 ("Make storyboard/visual planning mandatory (wire the VRR/VOA layer to real production)," `REMEDIATION-PLAN.md`) remains the separate, later, mandatory storyboard/visual-opportunity planning package that must precede any visual production work. No Package 3 work — and no other remediation package beyond 1 and 2 — was begun by this package.

**No production code or test code changed by this documentation-closure step; no Unit 202 lesson/question content changed by any part of this package.**

## CC-13C.3 — Best-in-Class Learning Quality Governance Codification

**Status:** documentation/governance only — no implementation. Codifies a Product Owner / Project Architect decision: **the ALP must be governed for best-in-class learning quality, not merely technical correctness.** This is a durable authority/process decision, not a Package-3 (or any content-production package) implementation.

**What was codified, and where:**
- `docs/governance/PROJECT-CONSTITUTION.md` (GOV-001, highest-authority document) — new "Learning-quality governance principle": implementation correctness and product/learning quality are two separate acceptance questions; passing one never implies passing the other.
- `docs/governance/ROLES-AND-AUTHORITY.md` (GOV-002) — new "Implementation correctness vs. product/learning quality authority" section: the two-question model, and an explicit list of what Claude Code is **not** final authority for (product architecture, instructional design, pedagogy, lesson/storyboard/assessment/visual-pedagogy quality, learner experience, production-workflow quality, technical visual-reference correctness).
- `docs/governance/LEARNING-PACKAGE-QUALITY-GATES.md` — extended §1 (two-question framing), §4 (storyboard standard), §5 (visual-pedagogy standard), §6 (assessment-quality standard, applies to §6/§7 together); added new §14 (false-green state model: schema-capable/validator-capable/synthetically-tested/runtime-wired/production-adopted/mechanically-enforced/pedagogically-reviewed/learner-experience-reviewed/learner-ready — none implies another), §15 (authoring-economics/governance-proportionality principle), §16 (representative real-lesson qualification gate — technical AND pedagogical, defect-class-tracing rule), §17 (scale-only-after-qualification workflow order).
- `docs/architecture/LEARNING-PACKAGE-PIPELINE-AUDIT-AND-QUALIFICATION-PLAN.md` §9 — strengthened with the explicit two-dimensional (technical + pedagogical) qualification requirement and the "isolated defect vs. architecture-permitted defect class" rule, and an explicit statement that this requirement governs Package 3 and every later content-production package.

**Deliberately not touched:** `docs/architecture/LEARNING-PACKAGE-GOVERNANCE-CONTRACTS.md` and `docs/architecture/LESSON-PLAYER-AND-LESSON-PLAN-ARCHITECTURE.md` (implementation-contract documents, not authority/quality-gate documents — no gap found requiring a change there); `docs/product/PRODUCT-PRINCIPLES.md` (already carries Principle 35, "learner-ready means educational quality, visual quality and runtime quality together"; the new decision is about internal authority/process, not learner-facing product truth, so it belongs in the governance documents above, not the product-principles index).

**V1 architecture preserved:** this package does not alter ADR-0006's one-canonical-route / no-within-lesson-branching / assessment-driven-Guided-Revision model in any way — explicitly stated in both new sections that touch pipeline sequencing.

**Package 3 relationship:** these principles constrain Package 3 and all later content-production packages. Package 3 is **not** implemented or approved by this documentation change. The prior session's Package-3 architecture-discovery report remains evidence/proposal only, pending independent Project Architect review — explicitly stated in `LEARNING-PACKAGE-QUALITY-GATES.md` §17 and `LEARNING-PACKAGE-PIPELINE-AUDIT-AND-QUALIFICATION-PLAN.md` §9.

**No production code, tests, Unit 202 content, or visual assets changed.**

**Commit:** see the CC-13C.3 commit immediately following this entry. Local only, not pushed.

## CC-13C.4 — Legacy Content Non-Authority & Knowledge-Adequacy Governance Codification

**Status:** documentation/governance only — no implementation. Codifies a further Product Owner / Project Architect decision that arose during review of CC-13C.3: **existing downstream content and previous implementation choices are not architectural authorities and must not constrain the best design merely because they already exist**, and the existing governed knowledge/capability/assertion corpus must not be presumed pedagogically sufficient merely because it is structurally valid or currently used.

**What was codified, and where:**
- `docs/governance/PROJECT-CONSTITUTION.md` (GOV-001) — extended the existing "Learning-quality governance principle" with one sentence: existing downstream artefacts (including Unit 202 content, prior implementation choices, and the governed knowledge corpus) do not gain architectural authority merely because they already exist.
- `docs/governance/ROLES-AND-AUTHORITY.md` (GOV-002) — new "Existing content and prior implementation choices are not architectural authority" section: source authority vs. legacy artefacts, no-sunk-cost-architecture, legacy-content-as-evidence-not-authority, the blank-sheet quality test, and an explicit Package-3 consequence (existing lesson/schema shape must not be treated as a design constraint).
- `docs/architecture/SYLLABUS-TO-LEARNING-PACKAGE-PRODUCTION-ARCHITECTURE.md` — annotated §2's canonical production-sequence stage 3 ("Governed knowledge/capability model") as gated by a new knowledge-adequacy review; added §4.1 (knowledge-adequacy review: structural validity ≠ pedagogical sufficiency, the review questions, the over-atomisation caution) and §4.2 (PASS / TARGETED REMEDIATION / RE-EXTRACTION as the three valid evidence-driven outcomes — re-extraction permitted, not presumed).
- `docs/governance/LEARNING-PACKAGE-QUALITY-GATES.md` §16 — amended in place: the representative real-lesson pilot proves the new pipeline can produce an excellent lesson from first principles, not that it can reproduce the legacy lesson; legacy comparison is diagnostic only.
- `docs/architecture/LEARNING-PACKAGE-PIPELINE-AUDIT-AND-QUALIFICATION-PLAN.md` §8 — added one success-criterion bullet matching the §16 clarification above.

**Deliberately not touched:** `docs/architecture/CC-05-PEDAGOGICAL-KNOWLEDGE-AND-QUESTION-ARCHITECTURE.md` (defines the assertion/capability schema shape, not the review/governance question of whether that shape's real content is adequate — no gap found requiring a change there); `reports/learning-package-pipeline-audit/REMEDIATION-PLAN.md` (not renumbered or expanded, per this task's explicit instruction); `reports/learning-package-pipeline-audit/PIPELINE-TRACE.md` (a historical CC-13B audit snapshot citing "the 18-stage canonical production sequence" — the new §4.1/§4.2 subsections do not add a new top-level numbered stage, so that citation remains accurate; preserved as historical record regardless).

**No knowledge re-extraction was performed.** No decision has been made that re-extraction is necessary — PASS, TARGETED REMEDIATION and RE-EXTRACTION remain three equally valid, evidence-driven outcomes of a review that has not yet been performed.

**Package 3 relationship:** unchanged from CC-13C.3 — Package 3 remains not authorised/not implemented. This package adds an explicit further constraint: Package 3 design must not use existing lesson structure or existing schema shape as its justification.

**No production code, tests, Unit 202 content, knowledge/assertion corpus, or visual assets changed.**

## CC-13C.5 — Authority Wording Correction: Claude Cannot Self-Approve Implementation Correctness

**Status:** documentation/governance only — no implementation. Independent review of CC-13C.3/CC-13C.4 found one authority wording defect: the "Implementation correctness" question's original wording ("Claude Code may establish this directly") accidentally granted Claude Code self-approval authority over implementation correctness, contradicting the surrounding intent that Claude is not final authority over any material acceptance question. This correction does not rewrite that historical wording as if it never existed — see the CC-13C.3 entry above for what was originally recorded — it corrects it going forward.

**Corrected authority model:** Claude Code generates and reports implementation evidence (tests, validators, schema conformance, production/runtime traces, real-content adoption evidence) and may state its own technical conclusion, but cannot self-approve that the evidence actually satisfies the approved architecture. The Project Architect independently judges both (1) whether Claude's evidence is sufficient and whether Claude's claimed conclusion follows from it — specifically watching for false-green conclusions, tests that prove less than claimed, test-only wiring, synthetic-only adoption, hidden information loss, inappropriate fallback/retry behaviour, bypass paths, duplicated sources of truth, production/runtime wiring gaps, architectural shortcuts, and accidental legacy behaviour — and (2) product/learning quality, as already established. The Product Owner retains final approval over both.

**What was corrected, and where:**
- `docs/governance/PROJECT-CONSTITUTION.md` — "Learning-quality governance principle": added "implementation correctness" to the list of things Claude is not final authority on, and made explicit that Claude produces evidence rather than self-approving it.
- `docs/governance/ROLES-AND-AUTHORITY.md` — "Implementation correctness vs. product/learning quality authority" section: question (1)'s wording corrected; the review paragraph now explicitly covers independent review of BOTH questions (previously only question (2) was described as independently reviewed); the not-final-authority list now includes implementation correctness.
- `docs/governance/LEARNING-PACKAGE-QUALITY-GATES.md` §1: question (1)'s wording corrected; added an explicit "neither question is self-approving" sentence.

**No other governance changed.** Storyboard quality, visual-pedagogy quality, assessment-quality, cognitive-load principles, authoring economics, the false-green state model, the representative real-lesson qualification gate, scale-only-after-qualification, legacy-content-as-evidence-not-authority, no-sunk-cost architecture, the blank-sheet design test, the knowledge-adequacy review, granularity/anti-over-atomisation, the PASS/TARGETED REMEDIATION/RE-EXTRACTION outcomes, Package-3 non-authorisation, and the ADR-0006 V1 canonical-route model are all unchanged in substance.

**Package 3 relationship:** unchanged — remains not authorised/not implemented.

**No production code, tests, content, or knowledge/assertion corpus changed.**

**Commit:** see the CC-13C.5 commit immediately following this entry. Local only, not pushed.

Every substantive depth/performance/scope judgment — required learner performance, required depth dimensions, supporting-knowledge scope, scope ceilings, confidence, matrix status, and every one of 18 AC-level + 8 Range-item-level review flags — was authored by the Project Architect/ChatGPT and approved by the Product Owner; Claude Code performed mechanical encoding, validation, and diagnostic work only.

**Recovered from a stalled prior session:** the prior session's partial work (matrix/manifest data, both generic Zod schemas, both validators, both real-instance Vitest suites) was found substantively correct and preserved; two genuine defects were fixed (an `approximatePassPercentage` `number | undefined` type error in the assessment-envelope derivation, resolved with a fail-closed guard rather than a fabricated default; ~20 source-acquisition-manifest clusters missing one or more required structured array fields, completed with explicit `[]` where a category genuinely does not apply, per the architect's own decision that absence must never be ambiguous between "not applicable" and "forgotten"), plus a one-character encoding drift in the committed provenance evidence copy (corrected to match the re-attached source exactly) and two test-infrastructure defects surfaced only once the build was unblocked (a Windows-incompatible `readFileSync(new URL(...))` call, and an `EXPECTED_REVIEW_FLAG_SUBSTRINGS` list that mislabelled a Range-item flag as an AC-level flag, leaving AC2.1's actual review flag with zero mechanical drop-detection coverage).

**Encoded:** 23/23 Assessment Criteria, 58/58 official Range items (`scripts/content/data/unit202-depth-performance-matrix.ts`), independently recomputed by `validate-unit202-depth-performance-matrix.ts`, not merely asserted. **Source-Acquisition Manifest:** 20 reusable domain-knowledge clusters (`scripts/content/data/unit202-source-acquisition-manifest.ts`), full 23/58 AC/Range coverage independently recomputed, 0 sourced / 20 unsourced (verified against the live corpus's `sourceRole` registry, not assumed) — the manifest is a shopping list for the next, separately-reviewed source-acquisition package, never a completed sourcing pass. **Information-loss audit** (independent fresh subagent, ~550 field comparisons against the byte-exact provenance copy): 0 substantive mismatches; the source document's mojibake transfer corruption was confirmed non-deterministic (several distinct symbols collapsed onto the same corrupted bytes) and every governed-encoding interpretation was checked and found contextually/technically correct. **Existing-corpus diagnostic** (independent fresh subagent, read-only, zero corpus edits confirmed): actual current counts EL 189/FP 44/FM 27/260 total assertions, 22 misconceptions; all 8 explicitly flagged review topics checked against the live corpus (full detail: `docs/architecture/evidence/CC-14-UNIT202-CORPUS-DIAGNOSTIC.md`) — most already handled correctly, with two content gaps (AC5.4 alternator content; LDR content for the "photo" ambiguity) and one over-depth risk worth attention in the next package (AC2.2 impedance/reactance assertions already exist beyond pure recognition depth, though short of full calculation).

**Independent final review** (fresh subagent, after implementation was otherwise complete) found one genuine defect: three `factualPropositionsRequiringSupport` entries in the manifest stated more specific technical detail than the approved matrix's own supporting-knowledge text actually supported (a security-alarm circuit mechanism, specific telephone master-socket components on exactly the sub-topic flagged as needing currency verification, and a rectifier diode count) — general technical knowledge added during authoring, which this task's own governance forbids in a not-yet-sourced shopping list. All three rewritten to state only what the approved matrix supports; re-verified clean.

**Validated:** `npx tsc --noEmit -p scripts/content/tsconfig.json` clean; full-monorepo `npm run typecheck` (9 workspaces) clean; the two new Vitest suites 36/36 passing (schema-capable, validator-capable, synthetically-tested-via-tamper-and-assert, real-corpus-adopted, and production/tooling-CLI-loadable — this is content-governance tooling, never learner-runtime wiring); both `--check` CLIs pass clean; root `npm run test:unit` 1149/1151 (the 2 failures are pre-existing and unrelated — a stale `tools/project-dashboard/roadmap-data.test.ts` roadmap-position assertion, confirmed identically failing at the clean pre-CC-14 HEAD via `git stash`); root `npm run lint` pre-existing 4 errors/13 warnings, all in files this package never touched, confirmed by path. Full detail: `docs/architecture/evidence/CC-14-UNIT202-DEPTH-PERFORMANCE-MATRIX.md`.

## CC-15 — Unit 202 Authoritative Technical Source Ingestion and Verification

**Status:** implementation-complete, review-ready. Ingests the Project Architect/Product-Owner-approved 67-source technical dossier (`unit202-approved-technical-source-dossier.md`, 2026-08-30) against CC-14's Source-Acquisition Manifest, recording per-proposition retrieval/locator/coverage evidence (`VERIFIED` / `SOURCE_GAP` / `CONDITIONAL_SOURCE_GAP`) for every required-knowledge item in all 20 clusters. Every source selected, its approved role, its approved proposition scope, its depth clip and the two mandatory source gaps (UK telephone master-socket anatomy; security-alarm transistor+thyristor topology) were declared by the Project Architect; Claude Code performed retrieval, locator verification, coverage recording, and mechanical validation only — no replacement or additional source was ever selected, and no gap was ever resolved from model knowledge.

**Recovered from a stalled prior session** (workstation reboot under CPU/RAM exhaustion, not a code defect): the prior session's dataset (67 sources, retrieval status, locators, proposition coverage) and its generic schema/validator/test architecture were found substantively correct and preserved without redesign.

**False-green audit** (this task's own mandatory checklist, run in two passes — this session's own inline audit, then a fresh independent subagent with no context from this session re-checking the same checklist against the corrected data): found and corrected **12 proposition-coverage records**. Nine were downgraded to `SOURCE_GAP`/`CONDITIONAL_SOURCE_GAP` for genuine overclaims — most notably Fleming's left/right-hand rule directional mappings, `VERIFIED` against a Nagoya OCW course-**index page listing a lesson title only** (the actual directional-mapping content was an unretrieved off-site video); periodic-time/frequency/`T = 1/f` claims in `sine-wave-characteristics`, `VERIFIED` against locators whose own content never mentions period; and several narrower partial overclaims (fractional-indices-as-roots beyond an integer-exponent source, "each listed component" schematic symbols from one transistor figure, motor-drive "protection", wireless "advantages", minutes→seconds from decimal-SI-prefix-only sources). Three were kept `VERIFIED` with corrected evidence — `B = Φ/A` and two parallel-resistance relationships, where the cited physics source gives only the un-rearranged form and the rearrangement is legitimate governed algebra, now cited alongside the approved algebra-transposition source with an honest inline note rather than left silently implying the physics source printed the rearranged form. ## Cold-handover gate

Before each CC package begins, a fresh contributor with no chat history must be able to determine from the repository:

1. What is the product?
2. Who is the initial learner/market?
3. What differentiates the product?
4. What decisions are approved?
5. Who has authority to change product scope or architecture?
6. What may Claude Code decide independently?
7. What is implemented versus only designed?
8. What phase/task is current?
9. What task is next?
10. What documents are authoritative for it?
11. What security/UX/content-governance invariants must not be violated?

Target orientation time: **under 15 minutes**.
